# Design

How Clean looks and moves, decided in one place (2026-10-08) so that screens stop choosing for
themselves. The rules are held in code in `app/.../ui/theme/` and `app/.../Ui.kt`; a new screen is
built from those pieces and types no colour, corner or button style of its own.

## Rules

| | Decision | Where |
|---|---|---|
| Colour | Black or dark base with three neutral steps above it. **One accent**, for the main action on a page, the current selection, and names that lead somewhere. The theme's secondary slots are filled from the accent, so nothing falls back to Material's purple. | `Theme.kt` |
| Meaning colours | Done and PR green, warm-up amber, failure red, drop purple, danger red. Named once, in both themes. | `CleanColors`, read through `LocalCleanColors` |
| Shape | Three corners only: 8 dp (number boxes, chips, badges), 18 dp (cards, buttons, bands), 24 dp (sheets, dialogs, the tab bar). What is docked to the bottom edge has the same corner on its top only. | `SmallShape`, `CardShape`, `SheetShape`, `CardTopShape`, `SheetTopShape` |
| Space | A 4 / 8 / 12 / 16 / 24 grid. A card's padding and line gaps scale with the text size from Settings. | `Section` in `Common.kt` |
| Type | Material's scale times the text-size setting; `bodySmall`, `labelMedium` and `labelSmall` are never under 11 sp, Material's floor (2026-10-14). Digits are fixed-width everywhere, so clocks and columns do not shift. Headings over columns are small spaced capitals, always muted. | `Type.kt`, `Caption` |
| Words | Sentence case on buttons ("Add set", "Finish"), not capitals. | |
| Motion | Short and physical, and every duration, curve and spring has a name; a screen types no number of its own (see "Motion vocabulary"). Nothing decorative. Settings, Appearance, Animations turns it all off. | `Motion.kt`, `LocalMotion` |
| Touch | A short buzz when a set is ticked, when a swipe passes the point of removing, and when an exercise is picked up to drag. | |

Exceptions: the tiny legend squares keep a 3 dp corner, and the colour tables that are data
(calendar palettes, BMI bands, fatigue colours, sticky-note colours, chart series) stay where
they are.

## Pieces (`Ui.kt`)

- `MainButton`: filled accent. The one thing a page is for; at most one per page.
- `QuietButton`: dark tint, accent words. Every other button. Both take an optional `summary`, a smaller line under the words saying what the button does (the "Update template?" options, 2026-10-12: stacked full width, the first update on offer being the `MainButton`).
- `DangerButton`: red words, no fill. Anything that throws work away.
- `Badge`: a small tag, green by default (PRs).
- `Caption`: the muted capitals over a column or on a tile.
- `EmptyState`: what a list says when it is empty, with the way to fill it.
- `Section(onClick = …)`: a card pressed as a whole; it dips and the ripple keeps to its corners.
- `Modifier.pressable`: the dip, for tiles and boxes. Plain rows keep `clickable`.
- `SwipeRow` (Ui.kt; `SwipeToRemove` is its left-only form): a row with an action per direction, each an icon and a word on its meaning colour (done for finish, danger for remove; icon and word keep it Mono-safe) that opens behind the row, turns solid past the threshold with the buzz, and is offered to a screen reader as a custom action. In a workout (`SetSwipe` in `Active.kt`, 2026-10-16) a set row swipes right to finish and left to delete by default (Settings, Workout, Layout: kv `swipe.right` / `swipe.left`, each finish / delete / off). Finish is the box's own tick (hints accepted, rest started, PRs judged) and the row springs back; on a done set it un-ticks, as the checkbox does (word "Not done"). The threshold buzz is the tick's (`feedback.tick`). A left and right round swipes as one row but acts on one half at a time: the first half not done, and once both are done the right half is un-ticked. An exercise's name row (2026-10-21, the user's words) swipes to remove the whole exercise, with the "Exercise removed" Undo, in each direction whose set swipe is delete (left by default; a direction set to finish or off does nothing on the name, `removesExercise`); there is no swipe on the name in focus mode, where a sideways swipe turns the page. While the cards are folded for reordering the swipe stays in place but does nothing (the hold owns the finger): taking its detector away mid-hold ended the hold, which broke reordering (2026-10-21, `ReorderHoldTest`). Earlier text: `SwipeToRemove`: swipe left; red opens behind the row, turns solid past the threshold, springs
  back short of it. Sets in a workout, exercises in the template editor.
- `Sheet`, `SheetRow`, `SheetGap`: menus rise from the bottom, each row with an icon and its
  current value; a row that deletes is red and last. Yes/no questions stay as dialogs.
- `drawnIn`: a chart's line draws in once from the left.
- Left and right on a chart (stage 5; `SplitLineChart`, `ChartPoint`, `SplitMarks` in `Stats.kt`; points built by
  `chartPoints` in core from `progressBySide`). A chart is one series per measure that mixes both kinds of session:
  a session done left and right is one x position with two markers joined by a stem (left a filled circle, right a
  ring, so it reads without colour; the stem is the line's own colour at 0.2 alpha), and equal sides are one marker
  with a ring around it. There are two lines, not one (the user's wish): the left markers are on a solid line and the
  right markers on a dashed one (the chart's stroke width, 6 dp on, 4 dp off), each running through every session, an
  ordinary session giving both its single value so the two run together there. In "All" each measure keeps its colour
  for both lines. An ordinary session is one marker with no
  stem (a small dot, drawn only on a chart that has a stem, so charts of ordinary work are as they always were). The
  axis takes in both ends of every stem. A key, a short solid line with a filled dot, "Left", and a short dashed line with a ring, "Right"
  (drawn on a canvas in the line's colour, the neutral text colour in "All"), appears under a chart only while one
  is in view (one for the whole "All" chart, which keeps each measure's own line colour and scale). A tap anywhere
  on the session's column opens a card with `L 100 kg · R 92 kg`, the date and the workout. Stems and markers are
  built once in `drawWithCache` into a path and flat arrays; drawing them allocates nothing.
- The imbalance chart (Charts tab, only when a session has both sides): right minus left, in percent of the larger side,
  one point per paired session by the first of estimated 1RM, reps, volume that any has (`imbalanceSeries`), not part
  of "All". The zero line is the axis; "Above 0: right leads. Below 0: left leads." is written under it, the tap card
  says `Right leads by 8 %`, and full screen keeps all of it (`ChartSeries.imbalance`).
- Left and right on the body figure (stage 6, 2026-10-12). Every outline that lies on one side of the body has a side
  (`BodyPart.side`, the person's own: in the front view the figure's right is on the viewer's left, in the back view the
  viewer's left is the figure's left; null across the midline, which is the neck's centre outline and the head). `BodyFigure`
  takes `sideColours` and `headSideColours` and shades a limb from them where it has an entry, else as before; a tap on such a
  limb names the side ("Left biceps", then the number or word for that side) and opens the page as it did. The Body page's
  fourth chip, **Sides**, is there only while the time frame has left and right work (`hasSideWork`; a remembered "Sides" falls
  back to Balance without it). It shades each limb by its own side's sets with Balance's palette and square-root rule, one
  scale across both sides so a difference shows; its rows are `Biceps  L 24 · R 18` with two thin bars (each in its side's
  colour on the figure) and a quiet `(R −25 %)` under, the largest gap first; above them one line, "Biggest gap: right biceps
  −25 %", opens that muscle. A row opens in place into its heads with `L · R` numbers and two bars each, and the open group's
  heads are drawn per side. Fatigue and Strength shade each limb by its own side, with the rows' quiet `L fatigued · R ready`
  caption only where the sides differ. The caption of the Sides view says what the gap is: the lagging side below the larger
  one, in % of the larger. The figure's spoken description in the Sides view is "Body figure, sides: largest gap right
  biceps, 25 percent less", each row one sentence ("Biceps, left 24 sets, right 18 sets, right 25 percent less").
  A muscle's page follows the view the Body page is on: in Sides its two limbs are shaded by side; in Fatigue and Strength
  they are coloured by side only when the sides differ (else the page's one tint as before). Its "Left and right" section
  (only with left and right work in the frame) has the counts, the gap, "Sets per week, left and right" (`MultiLineChart`
  with `shared`: two lines, the accent and the tertiary colour, one scale) and "Open exercises". A head's page has the same
  section and its own two limbs.
- The number pad (`Keypad` in `Active.kt`): weights and reps are typed on the app's own pad, not
  the phone's keyboard. Five columns since round 11 (2026-10-18), every key its own and at least 48 dp: the digits in three, then a
  column of the step down and up (the exercise's weight step, or one rep) and RPE (an empty place when RPE is off), then Next (the main
  key, top right; it walks weight, reps, next set), Same and Delete; the bottom row is the close key under 7, `0` under 8 and `.` under 9, its last two columns
  empty (2026-10-21, the user's words). RPE opens a two-row scale above the keys (6 to 8, then 8.5 to 10).
  Every way the pad goes away (the key, Back, the phone's keyboard coming up, Next past the last box) is `Pad.close()`: no
  box is left focused or in edit mode, and what was typed stays (2026-10-16). Ticking a set does not close it. The first
  digit typed in a box replaces what was there. It replaced step buttons under the next set,
  which the user disliked. A "Same" key (2026-10-16) copies the value of the
  same box from the set above into the focused box (`setAbove` in core: the previous set; in a left and right card the same
  side of the previous round, else for a right half the left half of its own round). It is dimmed when there is no set
  above or its box is empty. Like a step, the next digit starts afresh.
  Left and right (2026-10-16): when the same side of the round above has nothing in that box, the key takes the other side's (`setsAbove`).
  Last time's values carry across the two ways of logging (`carriedSets`, `docs/domain.md`, Left and right: carrying values over):
  an ordinary card reads a left and right session as one set per round (the heavier side's), a left and right card gives both
  halves of round k an ordinary session's set k. The grey hint, the Previous column and what a tick accepts all come from that one list.
- Ticking accepts the hints (2026-10-16): ticking a set (the box, a half of a pair, or the focus header's Done) first fills
  each empty box with exactly the grey hint it shows (last time, the template's target or the progression target, one
  value per box computed once in `viewOf` and used for both display and ticking; `acceptHints` in core), then ticks as for a
  typed set. A typed box keeps its value. With no usable reps/seconds/minutes (typed or hinted) the set is not ticked and
  the usual "Enter the reps first" shows. Unticking leaves the filled values, as for a typed set. The "Finish workout?"
  rule is unchanged: a hint is not a value for an unticked set.
- `CleanIcons.kt`: the few icons beyond Compose's basic set (Material icons, Apache 2.0, see
  `NOTICE.md`).

## Card corners (2026-10-19)
A card's corner is the look's (`Look.corner`: 18 dp, 26 in Linen). Every card, tile, band, the held card in a workout, the animation box, a Settings row's icon box, the workout banner and a finished workout's totals read it through `cardShape()`, `cardCorner()` or `cardTopShape()` (`Theme.kt`); the 18 dp shape itself is private to the theme. `OneLookTest` fails on a corner of 12 dp or more typed in a screen.

## Spacing steps (2026-10-19)
Paddings, gaps in a row or column, spacers and `Section`'s `spacing`, `inner` and `outer` take a step of `Space` (`Space.kt`): `xxs` 2, `xs` 4, `s` 8, `m` 12, `l` 16, `xl` 20, `xxl` 24, `xxxl` 32 dp. When the steps came in, the numbers between were moved to the nearest step, a tie to the more used one: 3 and 5 to 4, 6, 9 and 10 to 8, 14 and 18 to 16, 22 to 24, 28 to 32 (so a card's gap between lines is 8, not 10, and its margin above and below 8, not 6). Sizes (a box, a chart's height, the room for the tab bar) and hairlines of 1 dp keep their numbers. `OneLookTest` fails on a number from 2 to 32 dp typed in a padding, gap or spacer.

## Light looks: chart lines and sticky notes (2026-10-19) (the Light look is the only light look since 1.1.0 stage 5, see "1.1.0 Looks and Settings"; the contrast rules still hold)
On a light page (Light, Linen) the lines of a chart with several (`seriesColorsOf` in `Stats.kt`) are the same four hues deepened (`#2B62C4`, `#946200`, `#0B7D60`, `#C2306E`), each at least 3:1 against the page, the cards and white; the dark looks keep the pale ones. A sticky note's named colours have pale twins on a light page (`STICKY_COLORS_LIGHT`, `stickyBand` in `Common.kt`), so the band reads as a note with dark text; a colour the user picked on the wheel is kept as picked on both. Settings' swatches show the band the page will draw. `LightColoursTest` checks both.

## Type scale (2026-10-16)
The one scale is Material 3's, in `ui/theme/Type.kt` (times the text size setting; `bodySmall`, `labelMedium` and `labelSmall` never under 11 sp). A screen picks a role from this table and types no size of its own.

| Used for | Role |
| --- | --- |
| Page title (the big heading of a tab) | `headlineLarge`, bold |
| Section title: a heading over a group on a tab or page, and a card's title (Statistics, History, Body, Activity, Quick start, Templates, a month on the calendar) | `titleMedium`, bold, always through `SectionTitle` (`Common.kt`; read out as a heading, guarded by `OneLookTest`) |
| Sheet title | `titleLarge`, bold |
| Name of a thing on an editor page (a template or a day in the Program editor) | `headlineSmall`, bold |
| Card value, the number a tile leads with | `headlineMedium` with `numbers()`, semibold |
| Hero numbers (the weight on the ruler and in Welcome, a set's weight in focus mode, the rest countdown) | `displaySmall` to `displayLarge` with `numbers()` |
| Row label in a list or Settings row | `bodyLarge` (the default `Text`) |
| Dense row: a link row with its value at the right (`NavRow`), the rows inside Progress's History card | `bodyMedium`, value muted |
| Caption under or beside a value, hints, a card's quiet value | `bodySmall`, muted |
| Small capitals over a group (`Caption`) | `labelSmall`, semibold, spaced |
| Chip, button, tab label, badge | `labelLarge` (buttons), `labelMedium` (chips, badges, tab labels) |

Deliberate exceptions, all scaled by the text size setting: the letter rail on the Exercises tab (10 sp) and the labels drawn inside charts and the activity grid (10 to 13 sp, drawn on a canvas, which cannot use a text style); numbers set in Plex Mono through `numbers()` keep their role's size. A card or row that wants something else asks for a new role here first.

## Statistics card (2026-10-16) (REPLACED in 1.1.0 stage 4: the statistics are sections on the page, see "1.1.0 Progress"; the periods, the like-for-like comparison and the chart rules below still hold)

Progress's Statistics card (`StatisticsCard` in `Progress.kt`, rules in `core/StatsPeriod.kt`, tested in `StatsPeriodTest`), top to bottom: the title; a period choice Week / Month / Year / All (`Choice`, kept as kv `ui.stats.period`, default Month); four `Tile`s in a 2 x 2 grid (Workouts, Volume, Time trained, Sets; the card-value role, `headlineMedium` with `numbers()`); a quiet caption; a volume bar chart; "Most trained muscle" and "Top exercise" lines; the old `NavRow`s (Trends, Personal bests, Trophies, Calendar, Measurements, BMI) compact at the foot.

- **Periods.** Week is this Monday to today, Month the first of this month to today, Year 1 January to today, All the first workout's day to today. Nothing counts after today.
- **Compared like for like.** Each period is set against the same elapsed days of the one before (Oct 1 to 16 against Sep 1 to 16; Monday to Wednesday against last Monday to Wednesday). A shorter earlier period ends with its own last day (Mar 31 against all of February). The caption under the tiles says so ("vs the same days last month"). All has no change line and no caption.
- **Change format.** A tile's second line is the change and the earlier period's name: Workouts and Sets in absolute numbers ("+3 vs last month"; halves are possible with left and right), Volume and Time trained in percent ("+12 % vs last month"). Equal (or a percent that rounds to nothing) reads "same"; a rise from nothing reads "new". Direction is the sign, never a colour, so it reads the same in Mono.
- **Chart.** 80 dp of bars of volume lifted (`model.volume`, profile unit): Week the 7 days Monday to Sunday, Month every day of the month, Year the 12 months, All by month (by year past 24 months). The bar holding today is the accent, past bars a quieter tone of it, empty or coming ones a faint stub. Under it, the names of the first and last bar; the chart is one spoken sentence. Drawn in `drawWithCache`; the figures are worked out once per workouts / period in a `remember`.
- **Lines.** The muscle with most working sets (`muscleSets`, so the user's muscle settings apply) opens its page; the exercise with most working sets opens the exercise. A period with no workouts shows its zeros and "No workouts in this period" in place of the chart and lines.

## Softer cards, the tab bar, the Home header and the ruler (2026-10-12)

Light and fast: solid colours only, no blur, no shadow or elevation, no gradient, nothing that animates by itself.

- **Cards.** A `Section` is a very faint fill one step above the page (`surfaceContainer` at 55%) with an outline at 60% of the usual, and the card corner is 18 dp (it was 14). A tile (`Tile`) keeps the full `surfaceContainer`, so it still reads inside a card. All colours are the theme's, so the light theme and the dark-grey background follow. `Section(hero = true)` is the one washed with the accent (10% fill, 35% outline): Home's Today card and nothing else.
- **Tab bar** (`TabBar` in `MainActivity.kt`). A 68 dp bar (64 until 2026-10-16) in `surfaceContainer` with the theme's outline, floating 8 dp above the bottom edge (plus the navigation bar) with 16 dp at the sides, corners 24 dp. Since 2026-10-15 (the user: "I don't like the asymmetry, I want the name below the icons") the bar is Material 3's navigation bar layout: five slots of equal width, each an icon in a 56 x 32 dp pill with its word centred under it in a line that is always kept, so no icon ever moves; the selected tab's pill is filled (the accent container) and its word bold (which tabs show words, and their order, are choices: see "Tab names, tab order and the middle tab"). Workout, in the middle slot, has a larger pill (68 x 38 dp with a 28 dp play icon since 2026-10-16, round 7: "make the middle workouts button bigger"; the bar grew 4 dp so it still has room), always filled with the accent, with the word Workout under it on the same line as the other words (its pill rises 12 dp instead of 10 when the word shows, so its bottom edge stays clear of the word); with a workout running it is spoken "Resume workout" and a tap goes into the workout (the banner above it has the clock). A swipe between tabs still works (the `HorizontalPager`). The "workout in progress" banner, when its Settings scope shows it, is docked on top of the bar inside the same surface (so it takes the bar's corners) and the Everywhere overlay on pages behind the tabs is as before. The keyboard covers the bar, as it covered the old one.
- **Settling.** Every tab's list reports whether it is scrolled to its end (`BarEnd(tab, listState)`, `LocalBarEnds`). At the end the bar loses its side margins and every one of its corners (it is square, the docked banner too) and lies on the bottom edge, filling the navigation-bar area (160 ms, instant with animations off), the way a finished workout's `Total` bar settles; scrolling back up lifts it. The room a list leaves for it (`BarSpace`, `LocalBarInset` = bar + gap + navigation bar + the banner when shown) is the same floating or settled, so settling never changes what can be scrolled. A new tab page ends its list with `item { BarSpace() }`, calls `BarEnd`, and keeps a drag-only rail (the letters) clear of the inset.
- **Home header.** "Today" in small muted words over the date as the title in the phone's own order (`DateFormat.getBestDateTimePattern`, "Monday, 12 October"); at the right a chip with the week streak number (a star, and a shield while one is held) that opens Trophies. Then the cards the user chose.
- **Today hero.** The Today card is the page's one main button: "Start workout" (the program's slot, else the Current split's next template, else an empty workout), and while a workout runs the card is that workout ("In progress", its name, "Resume workout"). On a rest day or a day already done it names the day and what is next and the button starts that next template anyway. "Not this one" stays quiet beside it and goes while a workout runs.
- **Week marks.** Under "This week"'s ring, seven round marks Monday to Sunday: the accent filled for a day trained (it opens that day's workout, or the calendar's page of the day when there were several), a ring for today, plain for the rest, dimmer for days to come. Each is spoken ("Monday 12 October, trained").
- **Weight ruler** (`WeightSheet` in `WeightRuler.kt`). Logging a body weight (the Weight tile with no entry, the weight page's "Log weight", the workout and summary nudges: `AppModel.logWeight()`) rises as a `Sheet`: the value as a big number with its unit (tap to type it), a horizontal ruler under it, "Date" (today, or the phone's pickers) and Save, the sheet's one main button. The ruler is one `Canvas` drawn from the scroll position: ticks every 0.1, longer every 0.5, a label at each whole number, a fixed accent marker in the centre; it flings and settles on a tenth, with a short vibration per tenth only when animations are on. It opens at the last weigh-in (70 kg or 155 lb with none). For a screen reader it is a slider with the value and "Increase by 0.1" / "Decrease by 0.1". The entry is stored in kg and written as every weigh-in is.

## Tiles, lines and the Exercises toggle (2026-10-13)

- **Sparklines.** A `Tile` whose page is a chart can show that chart on itself: `Tile(spark = ...)` draws `Sparkline`, a 2 dp accent line (80%) of the last 26 weeks, oldest first, 28 dp tall under the number, with no axes, numbers or dots, built once in `drawWithCache`. It is decoration: a screen reader skips it and reads the tile as before. Workouts, Time trained and Volume lifted are per week (`recentWeeks`, from `AppModel.weekTotals`, worked out off the main thread); Week streak is the run's length after each week (`Streak.runAfter`: it climbs while the target is met and falls to nothing on a miss); Weight is the weigh-ins; PRs, 30 days is PRs a week over 12 weeks. A tile with no chart behind it (This month) has none. Home's "At a glance" and the Profile tab share the component.
- **Quick action on a tile.** `Tile(onAdd, addLabel)` puts a "+" (a 32 dp circle in a 48 dp touch area, spoken by `addLabel`) at the right; the Weight tile's opens `WeightSheet` on the page you are on, and the rest of the tile still opens the weight page.
- **Smooth lines.** Every chart line is a monotone cubic curve (`monotoneSlopes` in core, `Path.curveThrough` in `Stats.kt`, the one place a line's path is built): through every point, never above or below the two it runs between, so no overshoot and no dip that is not in the data. Two points are straight, one is a dot, two points on one day make a step. It applies to `SplitLineChart` (and so `LineChart`, the weight chart, Trends and the full screen, with the dashed right-side line), `MultiLineChart` and the sparklines; markers, stems, goal and zero lines, bands and the fitted trend line stay straight.
- **Exercises: All / Performed.** Two chips at the top of the Exercises tab's list (kv `exercises.show`, `all` or `performed`, default `all`). Performed keeps the exercises with at least one logged session in this profile; it works together with search, favourites, the equipment and muscle filters. The "hide built-in exercises until searched for" option (`library.hide`) is a second way of the same kind and is left as it is: with All it behaves as before, with Performed its note is not shown (nothing is hidden that Performed has not already left out). The picker inside a workout does not show the chips.

## Screens

- Pages slide in a little from the right and back out, in 160 ms, and are solid after 80; the
  workout rises from the bottom and drops back; tabs switch at once and keep their scroll position
  (`MainActivity.kt`). Longer, softer transitions were tried first and read as the app being slow.
- Workout: Finish is the filled button; Add set and Add exercise are quiet; a ticked row is tinted
  green and its box bounces; the rest drains as a line under the top bar and along the "workout
  in progress" bar; a superset is a line down the left of its cards.
  - Moves (2026-11-16): the next-set highlight glides from row to row, a done tint spreads from the tick, a finished exercise folds up into its line and unfolds, a removed set closes its gap and Undo opens it, and a set's "Change type" grows from its number into a centred dialog with the set left lit; see "Motion vocabulary". The full calendar ends at today (no later day is drawn).
- Left and right, which side went first (2026-10-13): a finished round that was done right first (`Round.rightFirst`) shows a small muted "1st" after its right value in `RoundRow` (workout page, summary, the exercise's History tab), nothing on rounds that went left first, and its spoken text ends "right first"; no setting, no column. The logging screen has no mark. In a card, ticking a half in a later round while the round directly before it has exactly one half ticked ticks that other half too (the same values an empty box takes from its hint, one tick after the half already done), with an "Other side ticked too" Undo in the bottom bar; it does nothing when the earlier round has no half ticked, when the card is another, or when the missing half has no reps to take.
- Left and right, stage 3 (2026-10-12): a card carries its own paired flag; a round's badges are one row under the round ("1RM L", "Reps R", "Weight L R"), each half is judged and hinted against its own side, and a finished workout's exercise has a small options button with "Read as left and right" / "Read as ordinary sets" (Undo offered).
- Left and right, stage 4a (2026-10-12): a finished paired block is drawn as its rounds (`RoundRow` in `Workouts.kt`, shared by the workout page and the summary). Under a small L and R head (`RoundHeads`, a `Caption` over each column) a round is one row in the ordinary set row's type: the number or kind letter in 36 dp, then the left set and the right set in equal columns, a half not done as a muted dash. The round's badges are one row under it, indented like a set's ("1RM L", "Reps L R", matched in the matched colour), and tap through to the exercise. When the sides differ, a small muted caption sits on the block header in place of the "1RM" head ("Right -2 reps" when reps decide, "Left -7 %" otherwise); equal sides show nothing. Each round ends with the two sides' estimated 1RM in two narrow right-aligned columns headed "L 1RM" and "R 1RM" (asked for by the user, 2026-10-15), where an ordinary set has its one 1RM: on the workout page and the exercise page's History tab, not on the summary. A round reads as one line to a screen reader ("Set 2, left 20 kilograms 12 reps, right 20 kilograms 10 reps"). History's card shows the best round on one line, the letters quiet, wrapping to two lines at most. Since 2026-11-02 (the user's words: "the 1RM should show up in the history of workouts") every other exercise on History's card has its best estimated 1RM by the chosen formula (warm-ups and drop sets left out, to a tenth) in a narrow right-aligned column headed "1RM", or under "R 1RM" when the card also holds a left and right exercise; a finished workout's page already gives each set's 1RM. A count of sets or PRs may end in a half and is written "2.5", "1".
- Left and right, stage 4b (2026-10-12): the exercise page. PRs tab: a row shows the comparable figure (the weaker side's) as before; for an exercise with left and right history a quiet `bodySmall` line under it gives `L` and `R` each with its own figure, each opening its workout; the reps-at-weight and longest-hold tables carry a quiet `L 12 · R 10` under a weight. History tab: a paired block is its rounds (`RoundRows`) with the lagging side as a caption under them, an ordinary block as before. Info tab: "Imbalance" (latest gap, `Right −2 reps · −17 %` or `Even`, opens the History tab at that workout) and "Rounds logged" under the "Left and right" line, shown only with paired history. Personal bests: a quiet `L 100 · R 92` under a best lift; PR rows say `L` or `R`. Muscle and head pages: `Left 24 · Right 18` under "Sets", and an exercise reads `6 sets as 3 × ½` where a round counted as one. Each of these lines is one spoken sentence (`LabelValue` and `LinkRow` take `spoken`).
- Left and right (2026-10-12): an exercise done one side at a time draws a pair of sets as one row
  (`PairRow`, `SideHalf` in `Active.kt`). The set's number or letter sits at the left, its type applying
  to both; then two halves of equal width, each a `SmallShape` container with its own weight box, reps
  box and 48 dp tick over one quiet line holding the side's letter and last time's matching set. A
  half is a faint tonal surface, tinted green when done and with the accent when it is the next to do
  (left before right, so ticking the left moves the accent to the right); the tick pops through
  `LocalMotion`. The column heads read Set, Left, Right, with the unit under each. The row swipes away
  as a whole.
- History card: name with a PR badge, one muted line of date, time and volume, then the sets.

## Gestures and continuity (2026-10-09)

- The five tabs lie side by side and are swiped between (`HorizontalPager` in `MainActivity.kt`), under the floating tab bar.
- The tabs (2026-10-12): Home, Progress, Workout, Exercises, Profile; the app opens on Home. History and Body are pages reached from Progress (a back bar, Back returns to Progress). Progress is two small cards side by side under the title, then the Activity card (the calendar of days trained with its Time / Volume / Sets shading, week totals, scroll back and a tap to the full calendar; moved here from Home on 2026-10-16, so Home and Arrange home no longer list it and an `activity` key in a stored `home.cards` is skipped when read), then Statistics (the Statistics card, see "Statistics card" below; its pages are `NavRow`s (`bodyMedium`, 48 dp each, touching) at the foot, each with its headline number). The cards are History (left) and Body (right), of equal height, each a miniature of the page it opens: History is the workout count and the latest five workouts as one-line rows (name, quiet short date, ellipsised); Body is the figure small in the current view's colours with the view's name under it. No chips, rows or tooltip, and nothing inside takes a tap: the whole card opens the page, so the small figure is `interactive = false` and still the shared element with the Body page. Home opens with a header (today's date, the week streak chip) and is cards (`HomeCard`): Today (the hero), This week, At a glance, Last workout, Activity, Recovery, PRs 30 days, Body weight; the user picks and orders them under a quiet "Edit home" at the bottom of Home (a page: a switch per card, Up and Down on the shown ones, Reset). Profile has the Settings gear at the top right (`Title`'s actions) and then arrangeable cards (see "Arranging Profile"). The Today card's Start workout is Home's one `MainButton`; the Workout tab's quick start is its own. The five tabs sit in the floating bar described above.
- `Modifier.swipeSteps` is the sideways swipe for stepping through a small set: the exercise
  page's tabs, and the Body views (on the figure only, so the rest of the page still swipes
  to the next tab).
- The body figure is the same thing on every page (`sharedAcrossPages`): from Progress, the Body page or a
  template to a muscle's page it glides to its new place and is not swapped.
- A colour means one thing: a muscle's bar has the colour the muscle has on the figure, and in
  the fatigue view every muscle has one (red fatigued, amber recovering, green ready).
- Where there is a picture of a thing, show it small beside its name (the exercise list's stills).
- Reordering exercises in a workout (2026-10-12): holding a name folds every card down to its name
- Two workout fixes (2026-11-16, the user's words: the number pad "closes not on touch end but touch start, so swiping would also close the number pad", and the folded list should gather around the held name, "ensuring that the position of the selected exercise's position doesnt move on the screen"; compiled, `ReorderHoldTest`, NOT yet run on a device). With the number pad up, a touch landing outside the pad and the weight and reps boxes closes it at touch down (`closePadOnTap` in `Active.kt`, on both workout screens' root); the touch is not consumed, so a scroll or swipe carries on under the finger, and only the release of a plain tap is taken, so a tap does nothing else; a touch on another set's box moves the edit as before. Holding a name keeps it at the same y: the folded list is laid out around the held card (the cards before it above, those after below), wherever on the screen it is, with names off the screen allowed; nothing moves while holding, and only the finger near an edge scrolls the folded list. The list is given blank room at its ends while folded (`roomTop`, `roomBottom`, cut down to what the ends need after two frames) so the folded list can sit anywhere, and on letting go the cards unfold with the dropped card where it was let go, as far as the list's ends allow. The others glide as before.
  (180 ms, the sets fade as the height shrinks) and unfolds them on release. The held card is drawn
  at the finger, on top, a little larger with a shadow, so it does not jump when it swaps; it swaps
  when its centre passes a neighbour's centre, with a tick, and the neighbours glide (a spring, 0.8
  damping). Near the top or bottom edge the list scrolls; on release the card eases into its slot in
  160 ms. With animations off all of it snaps.
- A row that has parts opens in place (2026-10-12): the Body page's group rows carry a chevron at the
  trailing edge (48 dp touch target) that grows the row into one indented sub-row per head, each bar in
  the colour that head has on the figure, and a quiet "Other work" line. The name still opens the page,
  a sub-row opens the head's. One at a time; animated size, none without animations.
- The open-group figure: with a group open, its heads are drawn over it in their colours, every other
  group steps back to 40%, and only then do heads take taps. A muscle's page opens its own group.
- A muscle's page, and a head's, open like any other page since 2026-10-14 (they rose from below before). The figure still glides between its places.
- Pinch-zoom on the figure (2026-10-12; Body page and muscle / head pages): up to 3x, drawn inside the
  canvas so the shared element keeps its bounds. One finger at normal size is not touched, so the
  list, the pager and `swipeSteps` work as before; from a second finger on, the figure takes every
  change until all are up (and `swipeSteps` gives up the moment a second finger lands). Zoomed in, one
  finger pans it. A double tap resets, listened for only while zoomed so single taps stay instant;
  opening a page from a tap resets first. No hint; it is found by poking.
- **Blur** (Settings, Appearance, "Blur progress photos", kv `photos.blur`, off by default): the Profile card and the timeline show each photo decoded a few pixels wide and stretched; a photo opened full screen, and Compare, are sharp.

- The activity chart (on Progress since 2026-10-16) scrolls into the past (2026-10-12): the grid keeps one cell size (a 26th of the card) but runs from the first workout's week to this one in a sideways scroll that opens at the present, month names over their columns and the year at each January; only the columns in view are drawn. Its colour scale is the last 26 weeks alone, so an old big day does not pale recent weeks (older days above it take the top colour). Three seconds after the last touch and fling it glides back to the present in 2.4 s (`LinearOutSlowInEasing`); with animations off it stays until the title is tapped. The title ("Activity", with a quiet "since Dec 2023") snaps to the present when the chart is away from it, and otherwise opens the calendar like the rest of the card. At the chart's right edge a leftward drag moves on to the next tab by the ordinary nested scrolling.

## Undo (2026-10-12)

A slip that is easy to make can be taken back for 8 seconds (`UNDO_MILLIS`). `AppModel.say(text) { undo }` shows the line in the one Undo bar (`UndoBar` in `Ui.kt`, at the top, a card with Undo and a line under it that drains over the time left, still with animations off); the old value is kept in memory by the caller. Since round 11 (2026-10-18) steps stack: one taken while the bar shows joins it (`Undos`, `Undos.kt`: "2 removed", "2 deleted", else "2 changes"), the time starts again, and Undo takes them all back, newest first; `say(text, expire = ...)` runs `expire` when the time passes unused (a deleted photo's kept file goes then). Cancelling a workout offers it back too (the same draft, so its clock, and the rest if it has time left), except when a new workout replaces it from the clash sheet. Used for deleting a workout (the rows come back with the same ids and text, the workouts are renumbered as for any save, and a Health Connect record is written again), a template, a weigh-in, resetting or deleting an exercise's settings, and taking an exercise out of its group. In a workout the same bar is used (set or exercise removed, sticky note removed, sets replaced by auto-fill); it had a bar of its own at the bottom with 6 seconds until round 11. A rename or merge of an exercise is undone from Settings, Data instead, because it can be needed long after (`domain.md`). The template editor asks "Discard changes?" on Back when something is unsaved.

## Rings and the Trophies page (2026-10-12)

- `ProgressRing` (`Ui.kt`): a ring filled clockwise from the top, in the accent on a quiet track, with its content in the middle. It sweeps to a new value in 600 ms and is still with animations off; `from = 0f` makes the first sweep fill it as it appears (the celebration). Home's "This week" card carries one (count / target, a star when the target is hit) and the ring itself opens Trophies; the card keeps the week report.
- Trophies (`Trophies.kt`) is a page of `Section`s: Streak, Last 26 weeks (two rows of 13 cells: star = hit, ring filled as far as it got = missed, shield = forgiven, outline = this week; a cell opens that week's report), Badges (earned ones with their day, then the next on each ladder as a line with a thin bar, the rest dimmed, not hidden) and Habits. Every row opens the week, workout or page that explains it.
- A shield is shown beside the Week streak number while one is held. Star and shield are in `CleanIcons.kt`.
- The summary's celebration is one `Section` at the top: the ring completing, a badge scaling in, one line each; it opens Trophies. With animations off it is shown still and silent.
- The summary after Finish (2026-10-21, the user's words: "I want to see the exercises themselves"), top to bottom: the celebration, goals reached, the program line, Done (date, note, time, volume, working sets, PRs against last time), the weigh-in hint, **Exercises** (every exercise in order with its notes, sets, kind letters, RPE and PR badges, rounds for left and right: the same `WorkoutCardSets` the workout's page draws, without the options button), PRs, Compared with last time, Left and right, Muscles worked, then Done / Open the workout / Share.

## Program (2026-10-12)

- The editor (`Program.kt`) is seven plain rows, weekday and template or "Rest" with a quiet exercise count; a row opens a `Sheet` of the templates (Current split first, then folders) with Rest last. "Starts" is a `Choice`. Stopping is a `DangerButton` with a `Confirm`.
- The Today card (Home) is one `Section` that opens the template's page; `MainButton` Start and `QuietButton` "Not this one" take their own taps. A rest day or a day already done is the same card with a quiet "Start it anyway". The card is hidden while a workout is in progress.
- A day of the week is a small square in the meaning colours (green done on its day, amber on another, red not done, quiet for rest and what is still to come, a dot for training on a rest day); on Trophies each opens its workout, or the program.

## Accessibility (2026-10-12)

Rules now in force; a new screen follows them and `tools/a11y/check.py` finds what slipped.

- **48 dp.** A thing that is tapped is at least 48 dp each way to a screen reader and a finger. `Modifier.minTouch()` grows the thing itself (use it where there is room); `Modifier.reach()` lays a 48 dp area over a smaller thing that must keep its size in the layout (`size(32.dp).reach()`), as the template card's menu, the swatches and the variation cross do. Known exceptions, where 48 dp would visibly change a dense layout: the Trophies grid cells (13 to a row), text rows with a value (`LabelValue`, muscle rows, the lines of a finished workout's page), the "Set" caption over a workout card's columns, and the letter rail (a drag shortcut, hidden from a screen reader, which walks the list itself).
- **Names and roles.** An icon that is the only label of a control has a content description; an icon beside text has none. A control says what it is: the set's tick is a checkbox ("Set 2", done / not done), the weight and reps boxes read "Set 2 weight, 80 kilograms", the set number reads "Set 2, warm-up. Change type", a `Toggle` row is one switch, a colour swatch is a radio button named for its colour, PR badges say "Reps, matched" or "1RM, new PR". A swipe has the same thing as an action ("Remove", on every `SwipeToRemove`) and, for a set, a row in its type menu.
- **Colour is never alone.** What a colour says (matched or new, done or missed, ready or tired) is also in a word, a shape or the spoken name.
- **Headings.** Page titles (`Title`, `BackBar`), `Section` titles, sheet titles and empty states are marked as headings.
- **Charts and figures** carry one spoken summary on the canvas (`LineChart`, the activity grid, the ring, the body figure); the muscles of the figure are reached through the rows under it.
- **Text follows the text-size setting.** Nothing drawn sets a raw `sp`; canvases multiply by `LocalTextScale`. Text that may be long ends in an ellipsis (`maxLines`, `TextOverflow.Ellipsis`) rather than wrapping a bar out of shape.
- **Meaning colours live in `CleanColors`**, for both themes: done, warm-up, failure, drop, danger, and ready / recovering / fatigued / full / underweight (fatigue, strength and the BMI bands). The light values keep 4.5:1 against the light background. Text on a coloured cell is black or near-white by `Color.readableOn()`, one threshold for every band and cell. The fatigue view never takes its colours from the look's colour scale (the blue ramp of AMOLED and Dark): it is ready / recovering / fatigued in every preset, grey only in Mono (2026-10-16).
- **Numbers**: a volume is `AppModel.volume(kg)` (profile unit, thousands grouped); a value there is not is `NONE`.

## Progress photos (2026-10-12)

Light and fast, like the rest: solid colours, no blur, shadow or gradient, nothing that animates by itself. Photos are private: only under the app's folder, never in the gallery, out only through the share sheet by hand.

- **The fan** (replaced on 2026-10-16 by the Progress photos card, see "Arranging Profile"; what follows is how it was). A `Section` titled "Photos" with the newest photo's date at the right; under it up to four photos of 96 x 128 dp, each 44 dp to the right of the one before so they overlap, tilted 5 degrees apart around the middle, the newest drawn last (on top), each in a `SmallShape` with the quiet outline. Static: no loop, no animation. With photos the whole card opens the Photos page. With none, three empty outlined frames in the same fan and one `QuietButton`, "Add a photo", which opens the add sheet (the card itself opens nothing). Spoken as one line ("Progress photos, latest ...").
- **The timeline** (`PhotosScreen`). Newest day first. A day is a `titleMedium` heading (the date as "Mon 12 Oct 2026", a heading to a screen reader) with one quiet line under it: how long ago, and the body weight logged on that day when there is a weigh-in on it. Thumbnails are three to a row, 3:4, `SmallShape`, each with its pose as a quiet `labelSmall` under it (colour is never alone); within a day front, side, back. A tap opens the photo full screen. At the top: pose chips (All, Front, Side, Back), then the page's one `MainButton`, "Add photo", and a `QuietButton`, "Compare", once there are two photos. With no photo the page is an `EmptyState` with "Add photo". A tile shows a plain surface until its bitmap is ready; bitmaps are decoded off the main thread at thumbnail size and kept in a small cache.
- **Add** (`AddPhotoHost`, a `Sheet`). The pose to file under as chips (Front unless the last choice was another, remembered), "Take a photo" (the camera app, no permission asked) and "Choose from the phone" (the system photo picker, several at once). Photos taken are dated now; picked ones by their own date when they have one.
- **Full screen** (`PhotoScreen`). A black page (the one place the app is black whatever the theme, with white icons and the status bar's icons made light) with the photo fitted whole, a pager over the photos of the filter the page was opened from, swiped sideways. Pinch zooms up to 4x; at 1x one finger is not taken, so the pager swipes; zoomed, one finger pans and the pager waits; a double tap resets; a single tap hides or shows the two bars. The top bar is Back and a menu (a `Sheet`: Change date with the phone's pickers, Change pose, Share, and Delete in red and last). Delete is at once, with an Undo in the snackbar. The bottom bar is the date and pose, then "n days after the first" and the weight of that day when known, the weight in the accent because it opens the weight page. The thumbnail and the full photo share an element across the two pages (`sharedAcrossPages`, key `photo:<name>`).
- **Compare** (`PhotoCompareScreen`). Pose chips (those with two photos, and All), then two halves side by side: the oldest and the newest of the pose to begin with. Under each half its date and two arrows, older and newer, stepping through that pose's photos; a swipe on the half (`swipeSteps`) does the same. One pinch zooms both halves together. A tap on a half (2026-11-02, the user's "make the before and after clickable") opens that photo full screen in the photo viewer, with its date, over the same pose's photos. Under them: how far apart ("14 weeks apart"), and when both days have a weigh-in the two weights and the change, which opens the weight page; a quiet "Share" makes one picture of the pair with the dates under it.
- **Accessibility.** A thumbnail is a button named "Front photo, Mon 12 Oct 2026"; the full-screen photo and each compare half carry the same name; arrows say "Older photo" and "Newer photo"; day headings are headings; the fan is one spoken line.
- **Not in this piece:** a reminder to take photos, photos on Home, photos by place or per exercise.

## The welcome questionnaire (2026-10-13)

Six questions, one to a page, shown once (`Welcome.kt`, `WelcomeScreen`). The user's words: simple, "very light and fast UI", "not trying to overwhelm". Solid colours only: no blur, shadow, gradient or picture; the one movement is a quiet fade and a small slide between questions (160 ms, none with animations off).

- **When.** At start-up, before the first frame (`AppModel.loaded` waits for it), when the active profile has no workout rows, no measurements and no kv `welcome:<profileId>` (`showWelcome` in core); and again whenever the active profile changes to such a profile (a new one made in Settings). A profile that holds any data is never welcomed, so the existing profile never is. Finishing, skipping or bringing history in writes `welcome:<profileId>` = `done` (removed with the profile). Delete everything restarts the app with a new empty profile, so it comes back by itself. If the app is closed in the middle it starts again at the first question; what was already written stays.
- **Frame.** A page of its own in `App` (no tab bar, no workout banner): a top row with a quiet Back at the left (not on the first question), six small marks in the middle (the accent up to the current question; spoken "Question 2 of 6"), a quiet Skip at the right; a big title, one muted line of why, the control; the page's one `MainButton` at the foot, "Next" and "Done" on the last. System back steps to the previous question (on the first it leaves the app, as on any first screen). Every question can be passed with Next without answering.
- **Restore on the first page** (2026-10-16, round 7). Under the name field: a muted "Moving from another phone?" and a quiet "Restore a backup", the same flow as Settings, Data (`rememberHistoryIn().pickRestore`: pick the zip, the summary dialog, Replace everything). A restore restarts the app into the restored database, whose profiles hold data or their `welcome:` key, so the rest of the questions never show; cancelling leaves the first question as it was. The last page keeps its own "Restore a Clean backup".
- **Writing.** An answer is written when its page is left with Next, through the function that already writes it (`AppModel.update` for name, unit and height, `addMeasurement` for the weigh-in, `setWeeklyTarget`). Skip, Back, "I'd rather not say" and "Skip this" write nothing from the page they are pressed on. A ruler's big number is muted until the ruler has been moved, and a ruler page with no move writes nothing. The weekly target (3 preselected) is stored only when Next is pressed on its page. The weigh-in is logged once; changing it later is done on the weight page.
- **Questions.** 1 name (prefilled, a blank one keeps the current name). 2 kg or lb as two big choices (`BigChoice`). 3 height on the weight sheet's ruler (`Ruler` with a `RulerSpec`: 100 to 230 cm in centimetres, or 4 to 8 feet in inches when the unit is lb; stored in cm, inches times 2.54), "Used for BMI only", with "I'd rather not say". 4 weight, the same ruler in the chosen unit, logged now, with "Skip this". 5 days a week, 1 to 7 as big choices, the weekly target from this week. 6 history: "Import from another app", "Restore a Clean backup" (the same flows as Settings, Data, `rememberHistoryIn`: a summary and a confirmation, nothing written before) and "Start fresh". An import that succeeds ends the questionnaire on Home and, when a target was answered, makes it apply from this week while the imported weeks keep the user's own usual pace (`finishWelcome(target)`); a restore ends in the app's restart.
- **Not asked:** goals, experience, gender, age, notifications, theme. Where you train will be the question before the last once places are built.

## Motion, Progress cards and the rest's sound (2026-10-13)

The user's list, read against Material 3's motion guidance (sideways for things that lie side by side, depth for going in, vertical for what rises and is put away).

- **Directions.** Sideways now belongs only to siblings: the five tabs, an exercise page's tabs, a figure's views. A page opened from anywhere grows in (scale from 94% over 200 ms, solid after 80) and going back it shrinks away (140 ms); it no longer slides sideways, which read as a tab change. The workout and a muscle's or head's page still rise from below.
- **Tabs.** A tap on the bar glides to the tab (300 ms, Material's emphasized decelerate), and the bar marks the page a swipe is heading for (`pager.targetPage`), not the one it has come to rest on. The selected pill's fill fades (200 ms). Once the first tab is drawn, the tabs next to the one shown are kept composed, so a swipe does not begin by building a page.
- **Tiles.** Workouts and Week streak carry no line: a count that only climbs says nothing as a chart. Time trained, Volume lifted and Weight keep theirs.
- **Progress, History card.** Every workout as a tiny row, newest first, scrolling inside the card (`HistoryRows`): name, a star with the workout's PR count when it has any, the day. Three seconds after the last scroll it drifts back to the newest (1.2 s), as the activity chart drifts to the present. The card is as tall as the Body card beside it (`Twins`, which measures the figure's card first).
- **Progress, Body card.** A sideways swipe on the figure steps through Balance, Fatigue and Strength (`swipeSteps`, so the tabs do not move); three dots show which. The view is the one the Body page opens on.
- **Reordering in a workout.** The held card is moved before its size is animated (`animateContentSize` clips to the card's own slot, which cut the card off as soon as it left it), and it is held where the finger took it: the touch's distance under the card's top is measured at pick-up, not assumed.
- **Weight ruler.** Ticks of 0.05 kg or lb, 20 to a unit, and the number always shows two decimals so it does not change width while dragged.
- **Rest sound.** Settings, Workout: under "Bell when the rest ends" a choice of Deep bell (the default), Gong, Soft tone, Wood block and Bright bell (the first bell, which the user found too high); picking one plays it.
- **Dimming is gone.** The screen is no longer dimmed during a rest (the user: "dimming behaviour is very bad"); jumping the brightness down and back at every touch could not be made gentle, since Android takes over the fade back to automatic brightness.

## Motion vocabulary (2026-10-14)

**Cards morph into their pages again, both ways (2026-11-16, the user's words: "it should be like morph animation, borders should grow out, elements inside should morph out and up into the new position"). Everything else is still the blink (2026-10-20).** Tapping a card on Home (cards and tiles), Progress (History, Body, Activity) or Profile opens its page as a container transform on one clock, `Motion.CARD_TO_PAGE` (500 ms, Material 3's emphasized curve `Motion.Emphasized`, the same both ways; it was emphasized decelerate reversed on Back until the user found the acceleration weird): the card's border and fill grow from the card's bounds to the page's, the corners going from `cardShape()` to square, the outline carried along the edge and fading as the container becomes the page, the card's own content fading out by 30 % of the way and the page's fading in from 30 % to 80 % of the way (`cardContentAlpha`, `pageContentAlpha`, read off the same eased progress as the bounds, so every part keeps one pace), each held where it rests on its own side while the container grows over it or shrinks away (the user's words, 2026-11-16: new elements must not "pop in from the top"; the container otherwise draws its content from its own top corner), the tab bar sliding away and back. Whatever is on both card and page moves and resizes from its place on one to its place on the other, as one thing drawn once (`Modifier.morphs(role)`): the body figure (the very same figure, `sharedElement`); the card's label into the page's title and a tile's big number into the page's number, even in other words or form ("WEEK STREAK" into "Trophies", "6" into "6 weeks · about 1 month"): `MorphText` draws both sides as one moving box in which the old words fade out as the new fade in (when the words are the same, like "Pull" on a workout card and on its page, there is one text that only changes size), so there are never two copies at two places; every card with a label does this with its page's bar title, and the number goes to the page's number where it has one (the Week streak tile into Trophies' streak, a 1RM tile into Linen's big figure on the exercise's Charts tab; a page with no number, the tile's number fades with the card); and a tile's or card's chart line into the page's chart line as one line (`rememberChartMorph`, `ChartMorphLine`; since 2026-11-16, "the graphs are not similar at all across morphs"): both real lines step aside and one line, resampled to 48 points on each side, moves and reshapes point by point from the card's to the page's, its colour, width and Linen's end dot going between the two, while each chart's axes, labels and wash fade with their own side. Pairs by role: Trends' charts named by `Tile(link = ...)` (Trends opens at that chart when a tile opened it), the weight chart, an exercise's est. 1RM line in "All" or on its own; only a plain line travels (bars, left and right and imbalance charts fade). A page's chart grown from a card does not draw itself in. Not shared, because stretching or two pictures at once looked wrong in the rendered frames: the Activity heatmap and calendar (the rows of Recent workouts, History, Recent PRs and Body measurements, the weight number, the latest badge and the photos are shared since the paragraphs on History's rows and on what is on both below). What is only on one side fades with its container. Page to page, tabs, dialogs, sheets, Settings, a row or button inside a card, and the workout stay the blink; with animations off (Clean's switch or the phone's animator scale 0) the card cuts too. Pairs: Home and Profile tiles and cards open the page named in `HomeCards.kt` / `Stats.kt` / `Profile.kt`; the label goes to the page's title for all of them; the figure on Body; the number on Trophies (Week streak) and the exercise's Charts tab in Linen (1RM); the chart on Trends, the weight page and the exercise's Charts tab. The card's own surface (fill and border) stays under the container; what moves is its content (`opensInto` sits on the content column).

**Text that travels never changes layout at a moment you can see (2026-11-17, the user's test of 1.0.4: names jumped to their full length the frame after a tap, ran over their neighbours mid-move and snapped back to "Lying Leg Curl (M..." after it).** Every `MorphText` (titles, numbers, row names, dates, view words, badge text, the banner's name and clock) lays each side's words out with that side's own resting constraints (its width, lines, ellipsis, size, colour: noted when its screen is laid out, `MorphTextSpec.rest`), never unbounded, so the card's version looks exactly like the card at rest and the page's exactly like the page at rest. While the box travels it draws both versions, each scaled from its own size to the box's (the font-size ratio, anchored at the start, or at the end for a column of figures set to the right) and clipped to the box, the card's fading out from 35 to 50 % of the way and the page's in from 50 to 65 % (`Motion.textOut` / `textIn`, read off the same eased progress as the container), so a different ellipsis, wording, weight, size or colour changes where nothing shows it; two versions that are set alike and both fit stay one text. A frame after a tap (or Back) is the frame before it, and every frame after the move ends is the page at rest: the `cardMorph` / `pageCardMorph` / `historyRows` test helpers check both (`stillAtTheStart`, `settledAndStill`). A row the History card's list cuts off at its bottom does not travel (it would show its hidden part the moment it left the list): it stays and fades with the card (`LocalMorphEnabled`). The History card (`WorkoutCard`) pairs what the workout's page has: the PR count with the PR figure in the page's totals bar, the date, the duration and the volume with the date line and the bar's other two figures, each exercise's name in the Sets column with its heading (id = the block's place in the workout) and the best estimate with the figure on that exercise's best set; "Best set" is no longer a column of the card. A chart's line carries the tint under it with it (`ChartWash`), reshaping from the card's to the page's, so the wash does not fade where the line left it. A History row's name, date and PR count go by legs so they never cross (`Motion.legged`: on opening a box first goes to its line and only then sideways and in size, on Back the reverse; the name keeps its width until the PR count has moved off; `historyRowsNeverCrossOnTheirWay` checks that no two of a row's boxes overlap at any time). A card that lies partly behind the floating tab bar is drawn only above the bar while the bar is there and shows its hidden part as the bar leaves (`MorphClip.barTop`). A swipe between the body figure's views slides the new coloured figure in from the side of the card over the grey body, drawn above everything in the card and clipped to the card, not the outline (`slideRoom`), and the old one out the other way.

**Everything that travels is on one clock (2026-11-17, stage H, the user's words: "what is it called when, the beginning position and the ending position are the goal, and the transition time is uniform, but the speed is variable so that all movements start and end at the same time", after the History card's off-screen workout names arrived before the on-screen ones).** Every box that moves (position or size) between a card and its page starts on the first frame of the change and arrives on the last, on the container's own eased progress (`Motion.cardBounds`, `Emphasized`, `CARD_TO_PAGE`), both ways; its speed is whatever its own distance makes it. No delays, no shorter windows, no stagger. This holds for the container, the shared boxes (`morphs`, `MorphText`, the figure, photos, the banner), the rows that go below the page (`row:out`), the chart line and its tint, the corners and the outline. The History rows' name, date and PR count go by legs (`Motion.legged`, `LEG_SPLIT`) so they never cross, but the two legs are laid end to end along the same eased progress (`Motion.leggedAt`), so every piece of every row is at its start on frame 0 and at its end on the last frame, together. Only fades have windows of their own inside the clock (the card's and the page's content, the text cross-over `textOut` / `textIn`, `morphIn` / `morphOut`, and `rowIn` / `rowOut`, a row going below the page fading only near its off-screen end): nothing that moves does. `MotionFramesTest` (`onTheClock`) checks every travelling box in `cardMorph`, `pageCardMorph` and `historyRows`: its position, normalised by its own distance, equals the container's progress in every frame.

**Numbers, charts, rings, the calendar, the figure and lists (2026-11-16, stage F3, picked by the user from mockups).** All named in `Motion.kt` (`ROLL`, `STRETCH`, `WAVE_STEP` / `WAVE_CELL`, `SETTLE_*`; the figure's colours use `short()`, the day mark's pop `reward()`), all a cut with `LocalMotion` false, and every screen at rest as it was. (1) *Numbers roll* (`Roll.kt`): a tile's big number (`FitValue`), the Statistics card's four hero numbers (they are tiles) and Profile's Lifetime totals (`RollingText`) roll digit by digit when the value changes, each changed digit sliding up out and the new one in from below, units and words crossing over; the overlay is aligned at the left, where the text is drawn at rest, so nothing shifts when it starts. A card that changed while it was not in view keeps the old figure until it comes into view and rolls once then (`rememberSeen`: per card key and label the last value seen, in `AppModel.seen`, for the session, no stored state; "in view" is the composable's bounds meeting the window, so a tab composed beside the one shown does not count); a Week / Month / Year switch changes the value in view and so rolls. (2) *A range switch stretches the line sideways* (`ChartStretch.kt`, `SplitLineChart`, `MultiLineChart`, the weight page and an exercise's Charts tab): the line drawn for the old range slides and scales along its dates, 400 ms on the emphasized curve, until its dates sit where they are in the new range (a longer range squeezes it towards the right while the older part comes in from the left, a shorter one stretches it and the part outside slides off the left), the y scale eases at the same time, the values on the axis and the dates under it cross over, and at the end it is exactly the new chart (the move draws the points of both ranges together, clipped to the plot, and the last frame is the chart at rest). It starts over, with no move, for another measure or unit. The Statistics card's volume bars do the same for a new period: each bar grows or shrinks to its new height as it slides into its new slot. (3) *The week ring and day marks fill from where they were* (`ThisWeekCard`, `WeekDays`; Trophies' per-week rings are not the same thing and stay as they were): the same show-the-old-until-seen rule, then the ring sweeps from the old fraction to the new on the existing sweep and a day done since fills on the short curve with one pop on `Motion.reward()`. (4) *The calendar fills in a diagonal wave the first time it is seen in a session* (`rememberWave`, `waved`; the full calendar page, and the Activity card's squares when the card first comes into view): from the top left of what is in view to the bottom right, 45 ms per diagonal (none waits past the twentieth), each square fading and growing from 60 % over 140 ms. Not when the page grew out of a card by the morph (that first time is used up), and not on later opens. (5) *The body figure's colours ease* (`BodyFigure`): when the colours change in the same figure without a swipe (a workout was done, the figures were refreshed) each muscle goes from its old colour to the new on `short()`; a swipe still slides them and a chip tap is still a new figure, so neither is touched. (6) *Lists settle in* (`settleIn`, `settleNext`, `Settle`, `LocalSettle`): when a page opens by the blink (not by a card's morph) its first six rows or cards rise 8 dp and fade in, 30 ms apart, 200 ms each, held until the blink has brought the page in; on History, Trends, Personal bests, Trophies, Measurements, the template page, the Settings list and the Settings pages and the exercise page's tabs. Only the page's first appearance: `AppModel.open` makes the page's `SettleState` (armed unless a card opened it) and it is disarmed once the window has run, so Back to the page and rows scrolled into view stay still.

**The banner, the tab pill, the exercise underline, new PR badges, the coin and the ruler (2026-11-16, stage F4, picked by the user from mockups).** Named in `Motion.kt` (`PILL`, `SLIDE`, `SHIMMER` / `SHIMMER_AFTER`, `COIN`, `stretchLead` / `stretchTrail`; the bounce is `reward()`, the ruler's settle `follow()`), all a cut with `LocalMotion` false, every screen at rest as it was. (1) *The workout banner grows into the workout* (`WorkoutBanner(morph)` in the tab bar, key `BANNER_CARD` = `banner:workout`, `AppModel.open` lets `Screen.Active` take a card key for this one card): the banner is a card of the tabs page, so the workout page is a container transform from the banner's bounds and Back shrinks it into the banner, on `Motion.CARD_TO_PAGE` like every card; the tab bar stays where it is under the growing container (it does not slide away). The workout's name and clock travel from the banner to the page's (`MorphText` roles `name` and `clock`, `WorkoutTitle` in `Active.kt`; the page's name is a text, and a tap on it makes it the name's field, so the words are there when Back is pressed). Only the banner on the tab bar does this; the banner docked on a page, the Today card, the middle button and every other way to open the workout are as they were. (2) *The tab bar's pill glides* (`TabBar`, `TabItem`, `rememberStretch` in `TabStretch.kt`): one pill is drawn behind the five slots and runs from the old tab to the new on `Motion.pill()`, its leading edge ahead of its trailing one (`Motion.stretchLead` / `stretchTrail`, so it stretches and then settles); on a swipe it follows `pager.currentPage + currentPageOffsetFraction` through the same stretch, and when the finger lets go the glide goes on from there; a word or icon changes colour as the pill reaches it, not when the tab is chosen; the middle button keeps its accent pill and the moving one passes behind it. (3) *The exercise page's underline glides the same way* (`StretchIndicator`, `ExerciseDetailScreen`, `swipeSteps(follow)`): it stretches between Info, History, Charts and PRs on a tap and follows a swipe; the content slides sideways with the finger (less where there is no tab to go to), and a tap or a step brings the new tab's content in from the side it is going on `Motion.slide()`; a swipe that does not reach a step brings the content back with `follow()`. Swipe and tap work as before. (4) *A new PR badge lands* (`Arrive.kt`: `rememberLanding`, `Modifier.landing`; `PrBadges(landing)`): a badge earned live on a ticked set scales in with `Motion.reward()` and one light highlight sweeps across it once (from 120 ms in, 450 ms); badges already there when the row appeared (the screen opening, scrolling back to a row) are not touched. (5) *A new badge, shield or streak flips in like a coin* (`Modifier.coin`, `CelebrationBlock`): rotateY through one turn with perspective, from 40 % size and transparent to full, on `Motion.coin()`, once. (6) *The weight ruler flings and settles* (`Ruler` in `WeightRuler.kt`): a fling coasts on decay and then settles with `Motion.follow()` exactly on the ruler's step (0.05 for a weight, one unit for a height); a light tick of the phone (`TextHandleMove`, whatever the tick setting says other than off) fires for each whole unit passed while dragging or coasting, instead of one for every step. Not measured by the frame tests: the haptic itself.

**The workout screen's moves (2026-11-16, picked by the user from mockups).** All named in `Motion.kt` (`GLIDE`, `SWEEP`, `FOLD`, `GAP`, `DIALOG`, 300 ms each on `Motion.Emphasized`), all a cut with `LocalMotion` false, and the screen at rest is as it was. (1) The next-set highlight is one rectangle drawn behind a card's rows (`Glide`, rows say where they are): when a set is ticked it glides from that row to the next set's, fades in where there was none and out when nothing is left. (2) A ticked row's done tint spreads from the tick's end of the row across it (`doneTint`); un-ticking fades it; the tick's own bounce is `reward` as before; a half of a left and right pair keeps its faint tile until the tint has covered it. (3) A finished exercise's set rows fold up into its one-line summary (height follows, rows fade and rise a little, the line fades in after them) and unfold the same way back on a touch; the line is what the app always showed. (4) Removing a set (swipe or menu) fades the row and closes its height before the set is taken from the card, so the rows below glide up and nothing jumps; Undo, and Add set, open the gap and slide the row in (`RowGap`, `Arrivals`: a set or a pair that appears in a card is found by comparing its items with the last ones); a removed exercise fades out while the cards below glide up, and Undo fades it back in with the gap opening (`animateItem`). (5) The "Change type" of a set number (and a half's "Left side type" and "Right side type") is a dialog of the workout itself, not a window and not a menu at the left edge (`RowDialogHost`): a scrim with the set's row cut out and ringed with the accent, so the set stays in view and lit, and a card that grows from the tapped number (its clip rectangle and corners move, its content fades in, never scaled) to the middle of the screen; when the middle would cover the row it sits just under it, or over it, or takes the larger side and scrolls. A tap outside or Back shrinks it back into the number. The Correct page, which has no host, keeps the menu. Dialogs from an exercise's menu (sheets and windows) are unchanged.


**Cards on pages grow into the page they open too (2026-11-16, the user's words: "we can start to fully move to this").** A card on a page that opens another page is a container the same way, page to page and Back: the History page's workout cards into the workout's page (`page:history:<workout date>`), the session cards on an exercise's History tab into the workout (`page:exercise:<name>:<date>`) and the two cards on the workout summary that open Trophies (`page:summary:program`, `page:summary:celebration`). The key starts with `page:` (`isPageKey`) and the same clock, clip, outline and fades are used, read off the page's own transition instead of the tab bar's: both pages sit in the one `AnimatedContent`, the card's page stays solid under the container (it is kept until the change is done, and is there from the first frame going back) and the page that grew is the container, on top. The card's title (the workout's name) travels into the page's bar title, one text. On Back the card's page is composed again, so the card is not on screen yet: the container shrinks onto where the card last was (`CardBounds.kept`), and the page keeps its scroll, so the card is there when it lands. The History page's cards are also what Progress's History card travels into (the row's name and badge): they stay that for Back to Progress, and are the card's title only while their own change runs (`WorkoutCard`). A page card not found, scrolled away or a page opened any other way is the blink; with animations off, a cut. Not done: the rows (`LinkRow`) inside cards on the Month, Week, Routine and template pages, which are rows and not cards.

**Every card and row on the tabs that opens a page morphs into it (2026-11-16, the user's words: "not all cards have the smooth animations, some still have blink ... we can start to fully move to this").** The same container transform on the same clock (`Motion.CARD_TO_PAGE`), Back its reverse, for: the template cards on the Workout tab (`workout:template:<id>`, or `workout:recent:<id>` in the Recent list, so a template shown twice never has two containers of one key; the template's name is the label that travels into the page's title, as on the Today card, whose template name is now a `MorphText` too); Statistics' rows on Progress (Trends, Personal bests, Trophies, Calendar, Measurements, BMI, and the Most trained muscle and Top exercise lines: `progress:stat:<page>`); and the exercise rows on the Exercises tab (`exercises:<list row key>`, the key of the row in the list, so a recycled row is the same one and the Recent and A to Z lines of one exercise are two). A row is a container with square corners, no outline and the fill of what it sits on (the card's for Statistics, none on the page for exercises): `Modifier.opensInto(border = null, fill, square = true)`; the row's own words (`NavRow` / `TopRow` label, the exercise's name) travel into the bar title. The ripple stays. A row inside a card that has a key (Profile's) keeps the blink unless it is given its own key. Buttons, chips, menus, dialogs and sheets are not part of this (Start and the template's menu keep their own taps; Start opening the workout is never a morph).
**History's rows travel into the History page (2026-11-16, the user's words: the small card's workout name, PR number and date "don't move to new position", the star should "morph into PR", and the names that are not on the full page should "transition downwards to their supposed positions off page").** Each row of Progress's History card is one thing, `morphs("row:out", date, late = true)`, holding three more, all paired by the workout's date: the name is a `MorphText` (`id`, role `row:name`) that moves and resizes onto the page card's title (the same words, so one text that changes size); the star and count (`row:prs`) are one box that cross-fades into the page card's `Badge`, "★ 3" becoming "3 PRs"; the date (`row:date`, `MorphText` in its own box in the info line, "15 Nov" into "Sun 15 Nov, 18:00") travels to the start of that line, and the rest of the line fades in with the page. The page side is `WorkoutCard` while `LocalPageCard` is `progress:history`; the Last workout card on Home and every other page are unchanged. A row whose card is not laid out on the page (further down the list) has no texts to become, so it keeps its own words, travels down to where its card would be under the last one laid out (`RowsBelow` in `Workouts.kt`: an empty box per row of the card, one card's height and gap further on than the one before) and fades between 250 and 450 ms of the 500, so nothing is left when it ends (`Motion.rowOut`; Back: 50 to 250 ms, `Motion.rowIn`), clipped to the container. Same clock and clip as the rest; with animations off, a cut. At rest the card's rows scroll and drift back as before.

**What is on both a card and its page travels instead of fading (2026-11-16, the user's words: common text and elements "move to their new place, like the History card's rows now do").** Where the card and the page it opens show the same words or the same number, the one thing moves and resizes from its place on the card to its place on the page (`MorphText(..., id = ...)`, `Modifier.morphs(role, id)`; roles named for the card, an id where there are several), on the same clock and clip as the rest, and anything with no counterpart fades as before. Built: (1) Home's Recent workouts rows (`LinkRow(titleMorph, dateMorph)`, roles `row:name` and `row:date`, id the workout's date) onto the same workout's card on the History page (`WorkoutCard` treats `home:recentworkouts` as it does `progress:history`, name and date only: the card has no PR count); (2) Recent PRs rows (Home and Profile; role `pr:name`, id the exercise) onto the exercise's line on Personal bests, the first row of each exercise only; the set and day are not the same words as that page's best estimate and fade; (3) Profile's Body measurements rows (roles `m:label`, `m:value`, `m:date`, id the file) onto the Measurements page's row of that file, sizes changing on the way; (5) the weight tile's number and the wide Body weight card's latest figure (`number`) onto the latest weigh-in, the first entry of the weight page's list (the same words, so one text that changes size); (6) Profile's Trophies card's "Latest: <badge>" (`badge`, id the title) onto that badge's row on the Trophies page; (8) Profile's Progress photos (`photo`, id the file name, `scaled`) onto the same photos in the timeline, as images that move and resize (the card's and the timeline's cells are both 3:4, so a photo is only scaled, never stretched; the timeline cell is clipped to its corners inside the moving photo, so it has them on arrival); only the photos that have a cell on the timeline's first screen travel (the first three), the rest fade with the card.
