Left fading, and why: (4) Lifetime totals' numbers: a cell is a tap of its own inside a card with no tap, so it is the blink and not a container (a cell given its own key would be a new card to build, not a pairing); (6) Milestones' rows: the Trophies page shows badges, not milestones; (7) This week's day marks: the Week page does not show the seven days; (9) the Activity squares: they are one canvas, not cells that can each travel, and a stretched picture looked wrong. At rest nothing is different (a test draws the page and the tabs page with animations on and off and compares them); with animations off it is a cut.

**The body figure's two moves (2026-11-16, the user's words: the view word and the colours "don't transition to the new position" / "the colour ... come from the left and right sides, only when swiping").** (1) The view word under the small figure on Progress's Body card ("Balance", "Fatigue", "Strength") is a `MorphText` with the role `view`, and on the Body page the selected chip of the view `MorphChoice` is its other side (`onPage`; the other chips fade with the page), so the word travels and resizes into its chip and back as one text; at rest the chip is as it was. (2) A sideways swipe between views, on the small figure and on the page's figure, does not crossfade the colours: the grey body stays still and the coloured layer slides, the old view's colours out the way the finger went and the new view's in from the other side, both clipped to the union of the body's outlines (`BodyFigure(slide = ViewSlide)`, `Motion.VIEW_SLIDE`, 300 ms on the standard curve). The swipe sets `ViewSlide.dir` just before it changes the view, and the figure takes it; a tap on a chip (or anything else) changes the colours as it did. The page's figure is a new one for each view, so the `ViewSlide` holds the colours the last one showed. Heads follow the new colours without sliding. With animations off it is a cut.

How it works (`CardMorph.kt`): the tabs page stays composed under the pages (`MainActivity.App`), so on Back the card is where it was on the very first frame (this was what made the old Back fly in from the top of the screen); its scope is `TabsScope`, a visible/gone transition, and the page's scope is its `AnimatedContent`'s. A card names itself with `LocalCardKey` (`home:<card>`, `progress:history|body|activity`, `profile:<card>`; only the lists give a key, so Arrange's copies and pages' own cards never morph), and its own tap (not a row or button in it) runs under that key (`cardTap`, `AppModel.fromCard`), so the page it opens is that card's (`cardKeyOf`, kept after Back). The change is a morph only when the motion is on, the page was opened by a card's tap, the card is in the tab shown and on the screen (`CardBounds`); otherwise it is the blink, which is also what happens when the card was scrolled away or removed. Shared things match only while a card change is under way (`MorphConfig`), so a page opened over the card's page and Back to it do not make the card's things fly. The container's clip (`MorphClip`) is one object for both halves, the page root is the other half of the shared bounds (`cardTarget`, with a floor so the tabs page never shows through) and `CardEdge` draws the outline from the bounds the container was clipped to in the same frame. Linen's white sheet is part of the page content and grows with it. `MotionFramesTest` renders the real app frame by frame and checks the container is drawn, grows or shrinks steadily and Back leaves the tabs page as it was; the strips are in `app/build/motion-frames/`. Not run on a device.

(The paragraphs below are older; where they speak of grow-in, shared axis, fade through or the card's own Back fade, the blink and the rules above replaced them.)


**The page clock (round 11, 2026-10-18; replaces the older page rules below where they disagree).** The user found every page animation jarring except the Activity card opening into the Calendar, which became the model. Rules: content is never scaled (a container grows or shrinks; text, charts and figures keep their size and are clipped); no frame shows two pages both above 10 %; a page moves as one piece, and the tab bar leaves and returns with the tabs page (`movesWithPage`); one clock with Material 3's emphasized curves, Back the exact reverse of opening. Three patterns only, all in `Motion.kt`: *card to page* (`cardBounds`, 400 ms; the card's content fades out in the first 30 %, the page's fades in over the rest; `sharedBounds` with `scaleToBounds(ContentScale.None, TopStart)`, the default `ScaleToBounds(FillWidth)` was what blew the cards' content up); *page to page*, shared axis X (`axisXIn/Out`, 350 ms: the page left slides 30 dp and fades in the first 35 %, then the new page slides in from 30 dp; Back mirrored; Settings' pages too); *tab to tab*, fade through on a tap (90 ms out, then 160 ms in from 92 %); swipes move the pager. Animations off (Clean's or the phone's) cut. The exercise picker is a card in the middle of the screen with the workout dimmed behind it (the user's choice, 2026-11-16), blinking in and out like every window. `MotionClockTest` checks the timelines and writes strips to `app/build/motion-strips/`. Not checked on a device yet.

From the Material 3 review (`docs/m3-review.md`, items M1, M2, M5, T1, C1, S1; the user chose this batch). Walked through on the emulator for crashes; how the movement feels has not been judged by the user yet.

- **Names** (`Motion.kt`). Curves are Material 3's: `Standard`, `Decelerate` (arriving), `Accelerate` (leaving). `quick` 100 ms (fades, colour, a thing leaving), `short` 160 ms (small moves: the bar settling, folds, lifts), `arrive` / `leave` (160 and 140 ms unless given `PAGE_IN` 220 or `TAB` 300), `slow` 600 ms (a chart drawing in, the ring), `follow` (a spring with no visible bounce, for anything the finger moved), `reward` (the one bounce), `press` / `pressNow` (the dip), `drift` and `clock` (the two self-drifts and the rest's drain). No other file holds a duration or a damping number.
- **A transition means a relationship.** Siblings slide sideways (tabs, a page's own tabs, views). A page opened from a row, card or tile grows in from 94% while the page behind shrinks to 98% and stays opaque; Back reverses both. The workout rises from the bottom and drops back, and after Finish it drops away to show the summary. A muscle's and a head's page no longer rise: they are pages gone into, and the figure's shared glide connects them. (Updated 2026-10-17: a page opened now uses shared axis Z, and the full-screen chart fades through; see "Fades and shared axis" below.)
- **A card opens into its page (2026-10-16, the fourth relationship).** A card on Home (every Home card, tiles included), Progress's History and Body cards and its Activity card morph into the page they open (Material 3's container transform): the card's bounds grow to the page's full rectangle over `Motion.CONTAINER` (450 ms, `Motion.Emphasized`) with the page's content fading in over `CONTAINER_FADE`, and Back shrinks it into the card again. Rows (Statistics links) and everything else keep the grow-in above. How: `Section(onClick)`, `Tile(onClick)` and Progress's `MiniPage` carry `Modifier.opensInto()` (`Ui.kt`), which notes the press under the key the host gave the card (`LocalCardKey`: `home:<card key>` from Home's list only, `progress:history` / `body` / `activity`; Arrange Home's copy gives none), so a new card needs no line of its own. `AppModel.open` hands that key to the page it opens within a second (`cardOf`, by page; kept after Back so the page can shrink; never for Arrange Home, Arrange profile or the workout), and `MainActivity` puts `cardTarget(key)` on every page's root, the other half of the `sharedBounds`. Pages opened any other way, the body figure's own shared element, the tab pager and the workout's rise are untouched. Off when animations are off in Settings or the phone's animator duration scale is 0 (`Motion.systemAnimationsOff`, read once): then no key is recorded and the plain switch happens. The card keeps its press dip and ripple.
  - **Fix (2026-10-16, NOT yet run on a device).** The card side of the shared bounds had `ExitTransition.None`, so the card's frame vanished the moment the page began to open and only the page grew; the card now fades with `containerFade` (out going, in coming back) once its bounds have a match (`isMatchFound`), and a card that is not the one opening still does not fade. The small body figure inside a card (`BodyFigure(interactive = false)`) is no longer a shared element of its own (it fought the card's bounds); only the live figure still glides between the Body, muscle and head pages. Profile's cards now carry keys (`profile:<card>`, `OpenableProfileCard`) like Home's (`home:<card>`) and Progress's (`progress:history`, `progress:body`, `progress:activity`), so every card of the three tabs that opens a page grows into it; rows in the Statistics card and buttons inside cards are not cards and open as before. `openBody` / `openHistory` keep the tab when a card was pressed a moment ago (a Home card used to jump the pager to Progress under the growing page, so there was nothing to shrink back into).
- **Fades and shared axis (2026-10-17, "some animations cut too fast and are jarring"; NOT yet run on a device).** Two Material 3 patterns now do the cuts. *Shared axis Z* (`Motion.sharedAxisZIn/Out`, scale 300 ms `Emphasized`) is a step into a page or back, replacing the grow-in of 94% over a shrinking page: the new page grows from 90% and is solid after 150 ms, the old one grows to 105% beneath it and fades out unseen once the new one is solid; backing out, the page underneath settles from 105% and stays opaque while the top one shrinks to 90% and fades over 150 ms. The page underneath is never see-through, so nothing dips through the background or pops at the end. Settings' list and its pages use the same step (into a page forward, back to the list reversed; each page starts at the top). *Fade through* (`Motion.fadeThroughIn/Out`, `FADE_THROUGH_OUT` 90 ms `Accelerate`, then `FADE_THROUGH_IN` 210 ms `Decelerate` from 92%) is for unrelated swaps: the full-screen chart in and out, and a tab-bar tap that jumps two tabs or more (the pager fades out, goes there, fades in; a neighbour still glides, a swipe is unchanged). Arrange Home and Arrange profile fade the page in over the solid one, or fade it out over the solid one beneath, instead of both fading at once. A group's heads open with a fade as well as the grow, and the number pad fades as it slides. The workout's rise and the card container transform are as they were. All of it follows `Motion.speed` and is instant with animations off. Not given a fade: the Body view switch (Balance / Fatigue / Strength; the rows are a column of siblings in one card and a crossfade would stack them) and Home's Today card between a plan and "In progress" (two cards with the same shared-bounds key).
- **Bounce is for an achievement.** `reward` is used by a set's tick and a PR arriving, nowhere else. Swipe-to-remove's return, the reorder glide and everything else the finger moves use `follow`.
- **Colour roles.** Every Material role is set in both schemes (`Theme.kt`): the snackbar is the theme's own inverse (near-white on black, near-black on light) with the other theme's accent for Undo, the light theme's text and outlines are Clean's blue-greys, and `surfaceTint` and `inversePrimary` follow the chosen accent.

## Tab names, tab order and the middle tab (2026-10-14) (REMOVED in 1.1.0 stages 1 and 5: three fixed tabs and a plus button, no Tab bar page)
**Tab bar style (round 8 part 2, 2026-10-16; a part, kv `theme.mix.tabstyle`, `Look.tabWords`; Mix and match, with the basic parts).** Every tab shows its word, always, in `labelMedium` on one line, so the choice of "Tab names" (kv `nav.labels`: Selected only / Always / Never / Fade after 10 s) no longer applies and is gone from the Tab bar page (the stored key is ignored; the fade code and `Motion.LABEL_HOLD` / `LABEL_FADE` were removed). Two styles, either on any preset (Linen: **Words only**; Light, Dark, AMOLED, Mono: **Icons and words**):
- **Words only.** No icons. Each outer slot is a 32 dp pill as wide as its slot (less 2 dp a side) holding the word; the selected one is filled (on the black bar white with dark text, `BlackBar`; on a bar that matches the cards `secondaryContainer` with `onSecondaryContainer`), the others have the same box unfilled with the quiet colour (`BlackBarQuiet`, `onSurfaceVariant`). The middle slot is a 38 dp tall pill, at most 68 dp wide, in the look's accent (`Look.orange`) with "Workout" in `readableOn` colour, bold.
- **Icons and words.** An outline icon (`CleanIcons.TabHome`, `TabProgress`, `TabList`, `TabPerson`: a house with a door, a line rising along its axes, a three-line list with bullets, a person; 24 dp box, 1.4 stroke, round caps and joins, stroked paths drawn for Clean, no two alike) at 24 dp in a 56 x 32 pill that fills when selected, the word 2 dp under it (selected stronger: white on the black bar, `onSurface` otherwise; the others `BlackBarQuiet` / `onSurfaceVariant`). The middle slot is the same 68 x 38 accent pill with "Workout" in `onPrimary` (no play icon), centred in the bar.
Both keep the Material 3 basics: five equal slots, all outer words on one line (the content is centred in the 68 dp bar), 48 dp+ touch, `Role.Tab` with the selected state and the tab's name (the middle one says "Resume workout" while a workout runs), a hold on an outer tab opens the Navigation page, the middle button dips when pressed (0.94) and has a 2 dp ring when it is the tab shown. Settings' live preview draws the bar for a change of `theme.mix.tabstyle` or `theme.mix.tabbar`.

Every tab is
spoken by name whatever is drawn. The order of the four outer tabs (kv `nav.order`) is set on the **Tab bar** page (`Navigation.kt`, from Settings, Appearance or a hold on any
outer tab): a drag list with grips and Move up / Move down actions, Done, Reset to default. Workout is always the middle one, and the
pager lays its pages out in the bar's order. A tap on the middle button makes the Workout page rise into place (a short slide up and fade, `Motion.PAGE_IN`) instead of gliding
past the tabs between; a swipe still slides it sideways as a sibling. Tab items are `combinedClickable` (tap, hold), so they have no press dip.

## Still to do

Menus not yet moved into sheets (set type, rest options, switch variation, the calendar day),
the exercise list rows, the Body page and the muscle page have had the shared colours and shapes
but no layout pass of their own.

## Presets and the Look (REMOVED in 1.1.0 stage 5: the app has Dark and Light, one accent, see "1.1.0 Looks and Settings"; the text below is history)

A look is a preset (`theme.preset`: `light`, `dark`, `amoled`, `mono`, `linen`; AMOLED is the app's own look and the default) with optional parts swapped (`theme.mix.<part>` = a value id) and the older single options as overrides (`accent`, `palette`, `background`, `theme` for Dark / Light / System). `resolveLook` (`ui/theme/Presets.kt`) turns them into one immutable `Look`; `AppModel.look` is that, and `CleanTheme(look, ...)` builds the Material scheme from it (`Look.colorScheme()`) and provides `LocalLook`. With AMOLED and no mixes the scheme is exactly the one built before presets. The parts and their values are the table in `docs/customization.md`, section 1. A screen types no colour or corner of its own for these: cards (`Section`) read the fill, transparent (AMOLED's since 2026-10-15: no fill, the 1 dp outline alone), see-through or solid, the outline and the soft shadow; the Today card the tint; buttons the round or card corner (`buttonShape()`); the tab bar the black or matching style; area shading under chart lines the fill opacity; the calendar and body figure the colour scale (`AppModel.palette`, which is the user's palette when one is chosen, else the preset's); `Motion.speed` shortens every tween by the look's factor (never the rest's clock). A card colour only fits its mode (solid white on a light look, solid slate on a dark one); any other mix falls back to the see-through card. `AppModel.choosePreset(id)` sets the preset and clears every mix and override.

Appearance page (step 1b): top to bottom Preset chips, the Mono panel (only on paper; directly under the presets since 2026-10-16), the Day & night panel (only with that chip), Mix and match, then the rest: Home, Tab bar, Charts, Customize. Every dropdown row is 48 dp tall and speaks as "Part: value"; since 2026-10-16 there is no separate Colours card: Mix and match's Accent row lists the presets' accents, then the named accents and "Your own colour…" (the wheel), Colour scale the presets' ramps, then the named palettes and your own, and an Icon accent row follows App icon (Preset's own, the accents, Ink); a named or own colour is stored in the old override keys (`accent`, `palette`, `theme.iconAccent`), which still win over `theme.mix.*`, and picking a preset's value clears the override; rows show small swatches; a part with one distinct value across the presets is shown disabled. The launcher icon is a set of aliases (`IconAliases.kt`), switched when the look's icon tile or icon accent changes. Since 2026-10-16 every alias opens `Launch` (`Launch.kt`: its own task affinity, out of recents, no history), which hands over to `MainActivity` at once: Android closes a task begun by an alias once that alias is disabled, which is what a change of preset does, and that was the app vanishing ("crashing") on a preset change when it was opened from the icon; `LaunchAliasTest` keeps every icon alias on `Launch`. After a switch the dynamic shortcuts, which Android drops when the launcher activity changes, are set again. `SheetHost` and `SheetTab` build the same tree in every look, so a page keeps what it remembers (Settings' open sub-page, a list's place) when the look turns Linen or back.

Saved presets and reset (2026-10-16): under the six chips a "Custom" sub-heading holds the looks the user saved as chips (the first four, then a "Show more" chip that opens them all and becomes "Show less"); a hold on one opens Rename and Delete (delete says so with an Undo). Two quiet buttons end the Presets section, "Save as new preset" (a name dialog; a name already used by another is refused, the same name as its own replaces it) and "Reset to preset" (an M3 dialog, "Reset the look?", Reset / Cancel; afterwards the snackbar offers Undo). Kv `theme.saved` (whole app): entries joined by `,`, each the URL-encoded name, a space, and URL-encoded `key=value` pairs joined by `&`; the keys are every look key (`LOOK_KEYS` in `Presets.kt`: `theme.preset`, the overrides, `theme.iconAccent`, Mono's sliders, the Day & night rule) plus `theme.mix.*` as stored at that moment. Choosing a saved one clears all look keys and writes its own (`AppModel.applyLook`), so the look is reproduced exactly; it shows as chosen while the stored look equals its keys, and then no base chip is chosen. Reset ("Reset to preset" since 2026-10-16, the user's words: reset means the preset's defaults) clears the same keys but keeps `theme.preset`, and with Day & night also its day and night choices and switch, so the look is the chosen preset's own (AMOLED only when nothing was chosen). Every part that can be overridden has a way back to the preset's value: Mix and match's rows list the presets' own values ("Icon accent" has Preset's own), Customize's Theme and Dark background start with a "Preset's own" chip that clears `theme` / `background`, the Mono panel has "Reset to Mono's own" (clears `mono.*`, shown while any is stored), and an imported font's button is "Use the preset's" (Linen's Inter, the phone's font elsewhere). Choosing a preset or a saved look opens no Live preview pop-up (2026-10-16).

Saved presets under Day & night (2026-10-16, round 7): the Day preset and Night preset menus list the five built-in presets, then the user's saved looks by name, stored in `theme.day` / `theme.night` as `saved:<name>` (`SAVED_REF`, `dayNightChoice`, `dayNightSaved` in `Presets.kt`). A saved look that is itself Day & night is not offered (it would point at itself). While it is that look's turn, the app draws exactly what choosing the saved look would draw: the stored options with the look keys replaced by the saved ones (`withSavedLook`), so its mixes, overrides and Mono sliders all apply. Renaming a saved look moves the reference with it; a deleted one falls back to Light by day and Dark by night (Undo brings it back). Kv only, so it survives restarts and backups.

### Mono (step 2) (REMOVED in 1.1.0 stage 5)

No hue anywhere (2026-10-16). While the look is on paper (`Look.mono`, which includes Mono chosen under Day & night), every colour is a grey, built in the one place the look is made rather than screen by screen: the accent is the ink whatever accent was chosen, the colour scale is `monoScale` (paper to ink) whatever palette was chosen, the Material scheme's tertiary and error roles are the ink at a strength (so the matched badge, the favourite star and every delete are grey), and the meaning colours (`monoMeaning` in `Theme.kt`) are greys of different lightness on the paper-to-ink ramp: ready lightest, then recovering, then fatigued darkest; a ticked set / PR darker than a warm-up or matched; failure and drop between; danger and the full-strength colour are the ink. Because everything that meant something by colour reads these (the body figure in every view and `BodyMini`, the summary figure and its legend, the fatigue and strength legends, PR badges, set-type colours, BMI bands, the calendar shading, charts, the ring, buttons, switches, the tab pill, snackbars), they follow without a change of their own. The chart lines of the "All" chart are four greys of different lightness (`seriesColors()`), template colours are the ink at ten strengths, sticky note bands go through `Color.inLook()` (the grey of the same lightness, `toGrey()`, which takes the luminance with the usual weights and keeps alpha). Negative inverts the grey result as before. Exceptions that still show hue: the colour wheel and the swatches of accents and palettes on the Appearance page (they are the controls for colours Mono does not use), the widgets and the launcher icon (outside the app's own screens), and the ink's own faint cool tint (`#16181D` at the slider's zero).

Card border (2026-10-17, round 10, the user's note "Card border"). The control already exists: Mix and match's **Outlines** part (kv `theme.mix.outlines`, a basic part), None / Thin (1 dp, the scheme's `outlineVariant` at 60 %) / Ink (1.5 dp, the text colour) / Hairline (1 dp `#E6E3DD`, which also becomes the row divider), each preset's own value first (Light and Dark none, AMOLED thin, Mono ink, Linen hairline); it is saved in a saved look, cleared by Reset to preset and previewed live like any part, and no second control was added. Every card reads it through `cardBorder()`: `Section`, `Tile`, the Progress mini pages, the share card, and since round 10 the page growing out of a card (`cardTarget` draws the same outline while the morph runs, fading in as the card's own fades out, so the growing edge is never missing, which mattered most with AMOLED's transparent cards). Two exceptions, now limited to the preset's own look (`Look.tileOutlined()`, `Look.heroOutlineAccent()`, tested in `CardBorderTest`): Linen's grey number tiles go without the hairline only (Thin or Ink outline them too), and a tinted Today card's outline is the accent at 35 % only while the outline is the default colour (with Ink or Hairline it matches the other cards). None with Transparent cards leaves no card edge at all; that is what the two choices say.

Colour-blind safe (2026-10-16). A switch in Appearance, Mix and match (kv `theme.cbsafe` = `on`, kept when a preset is picked, part of a saved look) swaps every green-to-red meaning for Okabe-Ito blue to orange, in the one place the meaning colours are chosen (`Look.meaning()` in `Theme.kt`, from `Look.colourBlindSafe`): `MeaningDarkSafe` / `MeaningLightSafe` beside `MeaningDark` / `MeaningLight`, so no screen changes. Dark: ready and done sky `#56B4E9`, recovering `#E69F00`, fatigued and failure vermillion `#D55E00`, strength full `#F0E442`, underweight (BMI) reddish purple `#CC79A7` so it stays apart from healthy. Light (4.5:1 on the light scheme background `#F6F7FA`, tested): ready and done `#0072B2`, recovering `#8A6400`, fatigued and failure `#B84F00`, underweight `#A8497F`. Warm-up, drop, danger (delete) and the matched badge (amber tertiary, against the now blue PR) are unchanged. Mono ignores it (its greys carry the meaning); Negative inverts the safe set like the usual one. No screen hardcodes a meaning colour (the remaining `Color(0xFF` outside `ui/theme` are note colours, palettes and the four chart series, all the user's choices).

Mono is paper and ink. **Paper** (slider 0 to 100) goes from white to a light grey, each channel `255 - 0.4 p`; **Ink** goes from `#16181D` at 0 to a dark grey, each channel `22 + 0.62 k` above 0. Every other Mono colour is mixed from the two: quiet text is the ink at 60 % over the paper, tracks and raised surfaces the ink at 10 % (cards are the paper itself), dividers the ink at 18 %, outlines the ink at 1.5 dp, the accent the ink, and the colour scale four steps of the paper-to-ink mix (20, 45, 70, 100 %), so the calendar's and the figure's more-trained days and muscles are darker ink and an empty cell or untrained muscle is a faint ink wash. The sliders apply while the look's background part is paper (so a Mono mixed with another background ignores them). They live in kv and survive choosing a preset. **Paper texture** (default 35 %) is a fine paper grain over the whole app (multiplied onto light paper, screened onto a dark page), strength `slider x 0.55`. Since 2026-10-16 (round 7, "too block-like and repeating") it is two seamless tiles of prime sides (251 and 199 px), each at half the strength, so the pair repeats only every 251 x 199 px and no tile edge or block shows; each tile is a soft tooth (the average of three random values per pixel) over fibre-sized and larger smooth clouds (wrapped value noise with smoothstep), at about the old grain's mean darkening (`Grain.kt`); dialogs and sheets, which are separate windows, are not grained. **Numbers** are IBM Plex Mono (`TextStyle.numbers()`); a screen asks for it on a figure that is shown big or has to line up, never on prose. **Negative** turns every colour of the look (page, cards, text, accent, outline, colour scale, the Material scheme and the meaning colours) over through one function, `invertColour`. True invert (default) is `1 - c` per channel, so hues flip (orange to blue); with it off only the HSL lightness turns over and hues stay. The result is dark (status bar icons light) and the launcher icon is the black tile with the light ink.

### Linen (2026-10-16, round 8, stage 1A) (REMOVED in 1.1.0 stage 5)

A sixth preset (`linen`): a calm, warm off-white, no shadows, tone and hairlines separating things, orange the only colour. Stage 1A is the data, tokens, font and icon; the screens that draw the sheet, grey tiles, two-tone titles, charts, rings and icon circles are the next stage. Tokens (all in `Presets.kt`, `LINEN_*`): page `#F1EFEA` with a faint fixed grain (texture value `fine`, strength 0.18, not Mono's slider); the sheet and cards white `#FFFFFF`; stat tiles `#F3F1ED` with no outline (`Look.tile`, `tileFill()` / `tileBorder()`; every other look's tile is its card); chart and list cards white with a 1 dp hairline `#E6E3DD` (`Look.outline`, also the scheme's `outlineVariant` through `Look.divider`); text `#1C1C1A`, muted `#8A8883`, faint `#A9A7A1` (`Look.faint`, the muted colour in every other look); accent orange `#E8642A` (the same on dark and light; icon accent Orange). Cards and buttons: 26 dp card corner (`Look.corner`, read by `cardShape()`, which `Section`, `Tile` and the Progress mini pages use; the theme's medium and large shapes follow; places that still use the constant `CardShape` stay 18 dp until the next stage), buttons fully round, main buttons (`MainButton`) black `#1C1C1A` with white words (`Look.inkMainButton`; the accent stays for badges, the selected chip, the chart marker and the middle Workout button). The tab bar is the existing black one. Font Inter (`res/font/inter.ttf`, one variable file, `InterFamily` with weights 300 to 700; an imported font still wins); numbers in Inter Light (`Look.lightNumbers`, `LocalLightNumbers`, applied by `TextStyle.numbers()`), the small muted units and the larger sizes come next. Chart fields for the next stage: `Look.chartLine` (the text colour), `chartLineWidth` (1.5 dp), `chartFade` (a grey fill fading to nothing, `chartFill` 0.14); charts do not read them yet. Other new parts and their Look flags (the four older presets keep today's value): page layout `cards` / `sheet` (`sheetPage`: a title on the background over one white sheet with 36 dp top corners), main buttons `accent` / `ink`, titles `bold` / `twotone` (`twoToneTitles`), row icons `plain` / `circles` (`iconCircles`). App icon tile `linen` (`#F1EFEA`, the white tile's mark; 40 aliases now). The four older presets resolve exactly as before (`PresetsTest`). Credits: Inter's OFL notice is in `NOTICE.md` and Settings, Credits.

Linen rendering (stage 1B, 2026-10-16; walked through on the emulator: Home, Progress, an exercise chart, Profile, Settings, the active workout). Everything below is gated on a `Look` flag, so Light, Dark, AMOLED and Mono draw exactly as before.
- **Orange is a highlight only.** In Linen the Material `primary` and `secondary` roles are the ink (`Look.inkMainButton`), so buttons, quiet buttons, dialogs, the exercise page's tab row, headings and switches are black; `Look.orange` (the accent read directly) is used only for the Home streak chip, the selected `CleanChip`, a chart's last point and its value, and the middle Workout button. A workout's next set is shaded `Look.tile`, not the accent.
- **Strength's Full is orange.** The Strength view's legend is "Full" then the colour scale strongest first; in Linen (with its own orange scale, not negative) Full is `LINEN_FULL` `#8A300A`, the ramp's top step `#B8430F` one step deeper, in place of the light themes' gold `#7D6200`, which read olive beside the orange (`Look.meaning()`, `StrengthRampTest`; 2026-10-20). Light (the same orange scale) has `LIGHT_FULL` `#3A1404`, a near-black burnt orange, since 2026-11-16 (the user's pick from mockups); the light colour-blind set follows it there. Balance and Sides use the scale alone; Fatigue keeps its red, amber and green.
- **Grain only on the page.** In Linen the root draws no grain; `GrainLayer()` (page colour and grain) lies under the tabs and each `SheetHost`, so the sheet and cards stay white. Lists in a sheet start 16 dp below its edge (`sheetGap`). The ring's track is `Look.tile` on white and white inside a `Tile` (`LocalOnTile`). The Home date is `d MMMM` in every locale.
- **The sheet** (`Look.sheetPage`, `PageSheet.kt`). A tab pins its title on the page colour and puts its list in one white sheet with 36 dp top corners that the list scrolls in (`SheetTab(header) { list }`; a tab's list adds its header item only when `pinsTitle()` is false). Home, Progress, Workout, Exercises and Profile use it; the tab bar floats over the sheet and lists still end in `BarSpace()`. A page with a `BackBar` (every Settings page, the exercise page, History, Body, Measurements, muscle and head pages, Trends, Trophies, Personal bests, a template, the workout page, Program and so on) gets the sheet from `SheetHost`, which `MainActivity` wraps round each page: it fills the page colour, draws a white rectangle from under the bar (64 dp + the status bar, + 48 dp on the exercise page for its tab row via `RegisterSheetBar(extra)`), and masks the two top corners in the page colour over the content so nothing scrolled to the top stands outside the curve. The page's Scaffold takes `containerColor = pageContainer()` (transparent inside a `SheetHost`, otherwise the theme's background), so the sheet shows through. The workout (`ActiveScreen`) is also wrapped; its own top bar reports its measured height (`Modifier.sheetBelow()`). Not given the sheet: the tabs' own Scaffold, the full-screen chart, the photo viewer and compare, the arrange pages, the summary and any other page without a `BackBar` (they stay on the page colour with cards).
- **Cards on the sheet.** `Section` (white, 1 dp `divider` hairline, 26 dp corners through `cardShape()`) and `Tile` (`Look.tile` grey, no outline) were stage 1A; constant `CardShape` is replaced by `cardShape()` in the card-like places left (undo bar, sticky notes, rest panel, welcome choices, share card, navigation and shortcut rows, the container transform's overlay clip, the photo frame); buttons use `buttonShape()`. No shadows: the chart tap card has none in Linen (a hairline instead).
- **Titles** (`Look.twoToneTitles`). `Title(text, second, actions)` is one row: the text in the text colour (semi-bold), the optional second line under it in `Look.faint`, the actions at the right. Home has no greeting: the day name over the date (`HomeHeader`). Profile is "Profile" over the name. The `BackBar` title is semi-bold on the page colour. Other looks are unchanged (Profile still titles the page with the name).
- **Header buttons.** `HeaderButton(icon, description, onClick, main)` is a 40 dp white circle (black with a white icon for the page's main action, Exercises' "+") inside the usual 48 dp touch area; elsewhere it is the plain `IconButton`. The `BackBar`'s back arrow is one; its actions sit together on a white capsule (`HeaderActions`), so a lone icon is a white circle and "Save" a white pill.
- **Row icons** (`Look.iconCircles`). `SheetRow` icons and Settings' heading rows sit in 36 dp circles in `Look.tile`. `LinkRow` and the like take no icon.
- **Numbers** (`Look.lightNumbers`). A tile's value (`FitValue`) is Inter Light at 34 sp with its trailing unit (a plain word after a figure: `kg`, `lb`, `%`) at 45 % of the size in the muted colour, and still steps down to 70 % to stay on one line. `BigNumber(value, unit)` is the 64 sp headline above an exercise's chart on the Charts tab (the latest value of the chart chosen, not for "All" or Imbalance).
- **Charts** (`Look.chartFade`, `chartLine`, `chartLineWidth`). `SplitLineChart`, `LineChart`, a single-series `MultiLineChart` and `Sparkline` draw a 1.5 dp line in the text colour with the area under it a gradient of that colour from 16 % to nothing at the bottom, and a faint grid (the top and bottom lines plus two between, in `divider`). In `SplitLineChart` the last point is an orange dot with a white ring, its value on the axis and the dashed latest line orange (the dashed line at 45 %); left and right charts, bars and the imbalance chart keep their marks and fall back to the text-colour line without the wash. The sparkline's last point is an orange dot. Since 2026-10-16 a Linen sparkline ends 10 dp short of the card's right edge (its wash too), so the orange dot on its last point shows whole instead of half cut off by the card. Since 2026-10-20 the sparkline's wash fades out sideways over its last 28 dp (and over its first 28 dp when it does not start at the card's edge), so it never ends in a straight vertical wall.
- **Ranges and chips.** `RangeChoice` (1M / 3M / 1Y / All, Week / Month / Year / All, the Body page's time frames) draws a black `Look.text` pill with white words for the selected one and plain muted words for the rest, no outlines. Every other chip goes through `CleanChip` (`Choice`, the exercise filters, the rest presets, variation chips): fully round, a hairline outline, orange with white words when selected; elsewhere it is Material's `FilterChip`.
- **Ring.** `ProgressRing` is a dark (text colour) arc on a white track in Linen.
- **Badges.** PR and matched badges keep their meaning colours; a badge that uses the accent is orange because the accent is.

## Home cards and Arrange Home (2026-10-14, customization step 3) (REMOVED in 1.1.0 stage 3: Home is fixed, see "1.1.0 Home and Profile")

Home is the header, then cards that are wide (full width, as before) or half. A run of half cards between wide ones is laid out in two
staggered columns (each card into the shorter column) or, by the Home layout option, in even rows (pairs as tall as the taller). Half cards
add no margin of their own (`LocalHalfCard`: `Section` drops its outer padding, a `Tile` takes `tileModifier()`); the gap is 12 dp. A tile
is the number, a quiet line, and its chart: edge to edge by default (`LocalEdgeCharts`: the sparkline is 44 dp, full width, on the card's
bottom edge, clipped by the card shape) or inset (28 dp, inside the padding). Edge to edge (fixed 2026-10-16), the line keeps 10 dp clear
of the bottom so the corners never cut it and has a faint fill (the line's colour at 12 %) down to the edge; the weight line is one mean a week.
The wide Body weight card, edge to edge, is the latest weigh-in and "+2.7 kg since 19 Jul" inside the margin over a 96 dp line of weekly means
to the sides and bottom; its axes, dates and full screen are on the weight page (the full chart there was cut by the card's edges). Fixed 2026-10-16 (round 7, "body weight card is messed up"): the card read its range through `measurementRange()`, which also draws the 1M / 3M / 1Y / All buttons, so the buttons were laid out outside the card, under its top left corner; on AMOLED's transparent cards they showed through over the "Body weight" title, and on solid cards the first one peeked out at the left edge. The card now reads the kept range with `measurementDays()`, which draws nothing; the buttons stay on the weight page. A tile's big number (`FitValue` in `Tile`) stays on one line and steps down to no less than 70 % of its size when it does not fit, so a half-width Body weight tile with its "+" keeps "161.5 lb" or Mono's wider figures on one line. Cards are chosen, ordered and given a width on Arrange home
(`Screen.EditHome`, `EditHomeScreen` in `Home.kt`: reached by holding a card, "Arrange home" at the bottom of Home, or the row under Settings, Appearance, Home). It shows the real Home:
the same cards at the same widths in the same staggered or even columns (`EditGrid`, one layout holding every card, built the way `HalfCards` lays out a
run), each inert and tilting a little (a `Motion.jiggle` of half a degree; nothing moves with animations off). Hold a card and drag it (`detectDragGesturesAfterLongPress`):
it lifts (a little larger, a long-press buzz) and follows the finger; when the finger is over another card the dragged one takes that card's place in the
order and the rest glide to their new slots with `Motion.follow()` (a jump with animations off); near the top or bottom edge the page scrolls. Letting go
keeps the order and writes `home.cards:<profileId>` once (the same text Home reads, so Home looks as the edit mode did). Two round buttons sit on a card's top right corner, half outside it so they cover none of its content (28 dp to see, 44 dp to touch; each card has 22 dp of room above it and, if half, at its side): a left-right arrow flips it
between wide and half (not on a card that can only be wide), the cross just right of it hides it; a card that shows nothing still has a 64 dp slot with its name so
it can be moved or hidden. Hidden cards are chips in a tray below ("Hidden: tap to add"); a tap adds one at the end. The top bar has Back, the title, Reset (back to
the default, with an Undo in the snackbar) and Done; the two Look options sit under the tray. For screen readers each card is one item that says its name, width and
what it shows, with the actions Move up, Move down, Make half width / Make wide and Hide; the tray chips are buttons. No scaling is applied: the cards are drawn at Home's size. The tiles on Home are the ones listed in `docs/customization.md`, section 2.

Entering and leaving Arrange (2026-10-16, round 7: "shouldn't immediately go to the top in a jump cut"). Home and Profile hand the arrange page an anchor (`AppModel.arrangeAnchor`, made by `arrangeAnchorOf`): the held card, or for the "Arrange" button the first card whose top is on screen, with that top's place on screen. The arrange page scrolls so the same card's top is in the same place before it is first drawn (the page is transparent for that one frame), so it opens where the list was, near the bottom when the button was pressed. The page fades over the list (`Motion.short`, both ways) instead of growing in, since the cards stay where they were. Each card's wiggle then grows from still to its full half degree (`Motion.jiggleIn`, 500 ms, each card 40 ms after the one before, at most 8 steps), and on Done, Back or the system back the wiggle settles to still (`Motion.jiggleOut`, 180 ms) before the page fades back to the list, which is where it was left. With animations off nothing wiggles and the page closes at once.

More Home cards (2026-10-16, `HomeCards.kt`; all start hidden in the tray, none is in `HOME_DEFAULT`). Key, default width, can be half, and what it opens:
Monthly volume (`monthvolume`, half, yes; this month's volume over six months; Trends), Longest streak (`beststreak`, half, yes; `streak.best`; Trophies),
Trained this week (`weekfigure`, half, yes; the figure shaded by this Monday-to-now's sets, muscle words under it, beside it when wide; Body on Balance),
Top muscles this week (`topmuscles`, half, yes; bar and sets per muscle, 3 when half and 5 when wide; a row opens the muscle, the card Body on Balance),
Recent PRs (`recentprs`, wide, yes; 3 rows half, 5 wide, the kinds only when wide; a row opens the exercise's PRs tab, the card Personal bests),
Recent workouts (`recentworkouts`, wide only; last three, a row opens the workout, the card History), 1RM: a lift you pick (`chosenlift`, half, yes; the
exercise is kv `home.lift:<profileId>`, removed with the profile; "Choose a lift" opens the exercise list, the "+" on the tile changes it, the tile opens the
lift's Charts tab), Weekly volume (`weeklybars`, wide, yes; the last eight weeks as bars, this week in the accent; Trends), Next workout (`nextworkout`, half,
yes; the program's pick else the rotation, as the Today card; Start; Resume while a workout runs; opens the template page), Average duration (`avgduration`,
half, yes; last 30 days, weekly means as the line; Trends), Fatigue at a glance (`fatigueglance`, half, yes; the figure in the fatigue colours and the tired
muscles in words, "All ready" when none; Body on Fatigue), A year ago (`yearago`, half, yes; `YearAgoCard`, rules in `core/YearAgo.kt`: the lift with the most sessions that has one within 30 days of a year back and one in the last 30 days, its best estimated 1RM near then against the best of the last 30 days, "Then", "Now", the date of then and a signed change, green for up and muted for down, a sparkline when wide; shown only at 5 % or more, else the volume of the 30 days around a year ago against the last 30 days at 20 % or more, else nothing and the card takes no place; the lift opens its Charts tab, the totals Trends). A card with nothing to show says so in a quiet line; the figures and the bars have spoken descriptions.

## Goals (2026-10-16)

Rules in `domain.md`, Goals. A goal is shown as a name, a thin bar, "72 %" and a quiet line under it (what the goal is, then "Reached" or "On track for about 12 Feb"); the number carries the meaning, the bar is only the picture. Where they show: the exercise Info tab (the existing bar, plus the day line), under "Goal 100 × 5" in a workout (just " · 72 %", no bar), and the weight page (`WeightGoalSection`: "Set a goal", or the goal row, bar, percent and day, a tap to change it, "Remove goal", and the target as the chart's goal line). The Goals card (`Goals.kt`, `GoalsCard`; Profile card `goals`, wide, default after Milestones; Home card `goals`, wide, starts in the tray) lists exercise goals (A to Z), the body-weight goal and the weekly target (this week's count of the target and the streak, once there is a workout); a row opens the exercise's Info tab, the weight page or Trophies. Wide it ends with a quiet "Set a goal"; half it lists the top two rows. With no goal the card says so and is itself the way to set one: a small sheet chooses Exercise (the exercise picker, then a weight and reps dialog) or Body weight (the weight ruler with the title "Target weight", which needs a weigh-in first).

## Arranging Profile (2026-10-16) (REMOVED in 1.1.0 stage 3: Profile is fixed, see "1.1.0 Home and Profile")

Profile is the title row with the Settings gear (not a card) and then cards arranged exactly as Home's: wide or half, a run of half cards in two
columns (staggered or even, following `home.layout`, and `home.charts` for edge-to-edge lines), a hold on a card or the quiet "Arrange profile"
button at the bottom opens the arrange page (`Screen.EditProfile`, also a row under Settings, Appearance, Home). The arrange page is Home's
(`ArrangeScreen` in `Home.kt`, generic over `ArrangeCard`: real cards drawn inert and jiggling, hold and drag with live reflow, a width switch and a
cross on each corner, "Hidden: tap to add" tray, Reset with Undo, Done). The layout rules (`parseCards`, `moveEntry`, `toggleWidth`, `hiddenCards`,
`homeSegments`) are shared in `HomeLayout.kt`; Profile's cards are `ProfileCard` in `ProfileLayout.kt`. Stored as kv `profile.cards:<profileId>`
(`key:w,key:h`, removed with the profile in `Repo.deleteProfile`); the look options on the arrange page are the same two as Home's.

Cards (key, label, default width, whether it can be half, what it opens), default list first:
- `profile` Profile, wide, never half: name, units, height, "Edit profile", "Switch profile" (Settings, Profile page).
- `totals` Lifetime totals, wide / can be half: workouts, time trained, volume lifted, week streak (two by two with the weekly lines when wide, a column when half); each cell opens History, Trends or Trophies.
- `photos` Progress photos, wide / can be half (image-led, `ProfilePhotosCard` in `Photos.kt`): wide is the latest four photos side by side running to the card's edges (the `Section` bleed slot), cropped to fill, the newest with its day over it on a scrim gradient (`colorScheme.scrim`, text by `readableOn()`), then "Add photo" (the existing add sheet) and, with two photos of one pose, "Compare first and latest" (`Screen.PhotoCompare`, which opens oldest against newest); half is the latest photo filling the card with its day over it; with none a line and "Add a photo". The card opens the timeline, a photo opens full screen. Thumbnails are decoded off the main thread as before. This replaced the fan.
- `membersince` Member since, half / can be half: the month and year of the first workout and how long ago; opens History. Shows nothing with no workouts.
- `weight` Body weight, half: Home's `WeightTile` (the weight page; its "+" logs one).
- `measurements` Body measurements, wide / can be half: the latest of each measurement file (weight first, up to four; one when half) with its day and its change since the entry before; opens Measurements, a row opens that file. With none, a line and "Log body weight".
- `milestones` Milestones, wide, never half: the three latest reached (with the day) and the next one with a progress bar; opens Trophies.
- `trophies` Trophies, wide / can be half: badges earned of all and the latest earned; opens Trophies.
- In the tray: `streak` Week streak (Home's `StreakTile`; the streak is already in Lifetime totals, so it is not shown twice by default), `workouts` Workouts (`WorkoutsTile`), `recentprs` Recent PRs (`RecentPrsCard`).
Measurements and Trophies, which were links on Profile, are the cards' taps; the old Photos fan, summary tiles and this-profile section are the Progress photos, Lifetime totals and Profile cards.

Milestones are derived from the rows each time and nothing is stored: `core/Milestones.kt` (`milestones`, `latestReached`, `nextMilestone`, tested in `MilestonesTest`). Ladders: workouts 1, 10, 25, 50, 100, 250, 500, 1000, 2000; kg lifted 10,000, 100,000, 250,000, 500,000, 1,000,000, 2,500,000, 5,000,000, 10,000,000; PRs (counted with `recordCount`) 1, 10, 50, 100, 250, 500, 1000; best week streak 2, 4, 8, 13, 26, 52. A count milestone is reached on the day of the workout that passed it; a streak has no day, so it counts as reached but is not listed under "latest". The next milestone is the unreached one nearest done by its share of the target (ties to the smaller target). Volumes are worded in the profile's unit (the stored numbers stay kg).

## Rest timer styles, feedback and PR celebration (2026-10-14) (REMOVED in 1.1.0 stage 5: one rest panel, one tick feedback, one PR moment; what stays is in "1.1.0 Looks and Settings")

While a rest runs, `RestDisplay` shows it as Settings, Workout, Rest timer says: Full screen (a 260 dp ring with the time at display size, the
next set in words, -15 s / +15 s / Skip, the preset chips, Hide), Ring (a card with a 72 dp ring), Slim bar (one line and a 3 dp bar) or Full screen
then hide (full for 3 to 15 s, then a pill; the pill reopens; the last 10 s reopen it by itself). The top bar's time and line stay in every style. The panels arrive
and leave with `Motion.arrive()` / `leave()` and snap with animations off. A PR's glow (`Motion.glow()`, 1 s, accent wash and outline fading) or confetti
(`Motion.confetti()`, 1.2 s, 36 accent pieces from the tick's side) is drawn over the set row; it is none with animations off. Settings, Workout, Feedback
holds the tick haptic, the rest-end choice with the sound picker, the PR celebration and the PR chime.

## Set rows, focus mode and one-handed mode (2026-10-14) (REMOVED in 1.1.0 stage 5: comfortable rows only, no one-handed mode; focus mode is the workout's own All / Focus switch)

Settings, Workout, Layout holds three choices (kv `workout.rows`, `workout.focus`, `workout.oneHand`). **Set rows**: Comfortable is the row as it always was,
with the Previous column (a left and right pair keeps last time's set under its boxes). Compact leaves both out and packs the rows closer (1 dp of padding
instead of 4; every cell still 48 dp high), the weight and reps boxes share the width, the PR badges move to a line under the row and the stopwatch button to
before the boxes; the column heads follow. **Focus mode** (off by default since 2026-10-16, at the user's word; on before; a value stored by the user is kept) shows the workout as a pager, a page per exercise: the position ("Exercise 2 of 5"),
the name (a heading, opens the exercise), "Set 2 of 4" (with the side for a half of a pair), the weight × reps in `displaySmall` with the numbers font (typed
values in the text colour, hints in grey; each value opens the number pad), "Last time ...", a full-width `MainButton` "Done", and under them the ordinary card.
Done is the row's own tick. When the page's exercise is finished the pager moves to the next one with a set left (after `Motion.SLOW`, with `Motion.short()`;
at once with animations off). A last page holds Cancel workout; Add exercise ends every page. The top bar's "All" / "Focus" text button switches modes, and
says so to a screen reader. **One-handed mode**: everything starts a third of the height down (a spacer in focus mode, content padding on the list) and Done is
72 dp high.

## Chart style, template colours and left and right 1RM in History (2026-10-14) (chart style and template colours REMOVED in 1.1.0 stage 5; the left and right 1RM columns in History stay)
- **Chart style** (kv `chart.style`: line, bars, smooth) reaches the charts through `LocalChartStyle`; a chart never reads the kv itself. Line is the chart as it has always been. Bars: rounded bars from the bottom edge, the axis a little below the lowest figure; a left and right session is a filled left bar and an outlined right bar side by side. "All" stays lines under Bars. Smoothed: the 5-point moving average as the curve, the raw points faint under it. The motion of drawing in, the goal, trend and latest lines, the tap card and the full-screen link are as for lines. Sparklines on tiles are always lines.
- **Template colours** are ten hues, lighter on dark pages and deeper on light ones, never typed in a screen: `templateColor(index)` and `TemplateDot` (`TemplateColors.kt`) are the only way to draw one. A dot is a mark beside a name, not a button; it carries the spoken name "Template colour blue". Picking is a sheet of 52 dp swatches in two rows with a ring and a tick on the chosen one, and None. The Off mode draws nothing.
- **History's left and right columns**: two narrow columns (44 dp each), right-aligned, with quiet heads "L 1RM" and "R 1RM" shown only when the card has a left and right exercise; ordinary rows keep Sets and Best set, a dash stands for a side with no estimate.

## Live preview in Settings (2026-10-15, changed 2026-10-16) (REMOVED in 1.1.0 stage 5)
A change to a visual setting shows what it did in a pop-up (`SettingsFloat` in `Preview.kt`, wrapping every page reached from Settings: the Settings list and each heading page, Tab bar, Launcher shortcuts, Arrange home, Program, Notes to variations). The frosted band and the preview pinned to the top or bottom of the page are gone. The pop-up is a dialog-style card over a scrim holding a small copy of the part of the app the setting affects, made of the app's own composables (the look: Home header, Today card and two tiles; Home's layout and chart options: Home's cards; tab names and order: the tab bar). It takes no touch and is spoken as one line. It shows the state from before the change first, holds it for `Motion.PAGE_IN`, then crossfades to the new one over `Motion.slow()`; with animations off it shows the new one at once. It opens once the value has been still for 450 ms, so a slider or colour wheel shows it after the drag settles, and a further change updates the open card instead of stacking another. Tapping outside, Done, or about four seconds close it. Which settings count is `previewKindOf` (the `theme.` and `mono.` families, accent, palette, background, theme, font, text size, `home.layout`, `home.charts`, `nav.labels`, `nav.order`); the setting rows themselves are untouched, the host watches the stored options. Text size is applied by the app's typography rather than the look, so its before and after look alike. A floating pill at the bottom right ("Live preview", a switch, 48 dp) turns the pop-ups on or off (kv `preview.live`); off, it stays to turn them on again. Lists end with `settingsFloatSpace` of room so it never covers the last row, and with a workout banner docked at the bottom (`BANNER_ROOM`) the pill and the room sit above it. Check on the emulator: change preset, accent and a Mono slider on Appearance and watch the card; switch Home layout and chart edge; change the tab names on the Tab bar page; turn the switch off and see nothing open. Since 2026-10-16 (the user's words): choosing a preset (a built-in one, a saved look, Day & night or Reset; any change of `theme.preset`) shows no pop-up, only the smaller changes do; and the pop-up no longer crossfades once: it holds Before 0.9 s, fades to After, holds, and goes back, three times over, ending on After for 1.2 s before it closes itself; two pills at the top right, Before and After, show which one is on screen (the one showing filled in the accent). With animations off the two swap without the fade. Since 2026-10-17 (round 10, the user's words: "make the before and after clickable") the pills are tabs: a tap shows that side with the same fade, stops the swaps and keeps the pop-up open until Done or a tap outside; the tapped pill gets a check before its word, and a second tap on the other pill moves to it (`PreviewSide`, tested in `PreviewSideTest`). Each pill has a 48 dp hit area around the small visible pill. Untouched, the swaps and the self-closing are as before.

## Rest notification, the Today widget and launcher shortcuts (2026-10-15) (the compact form of the rest notification and the shortcut chooser REMOVED in 1.1.0 stage 5; the rest stays)
Three things outside the app, all local. The rest notification is quiet and public on the lock screen; its own view is either the larger
form (countdown, a bar of how far the rest had gone when it was posted, the next set, a chevron up at the bottom right) or the smaller
(countdown, a chevron down at the top right), switched by that chevron and by Settings, Workout, Rest timer. It uses the system's
notification text styles, so it follows the phone's light or dark theme, and has "+15 s" and "Skip" under it. The Today widget is
drawn in the look's colours (card, text, muted text, accent, text on accent): a title row with the week streak, the template's name
with a Start button beside it (Resume while a workout runs), and seven day marks under them: filled accent for a day trained, an
accent ring for today, a dim dot for a past day without one, a dim ring for a day to come. The launcher icon's shortcuts are the
chosen of Start (named after the next template), Resume (only while a workout runs) and Log bodyweight, in the order of
Settings, Workout, Launcher shortcuts, a drag list built like the Tab bar page with a switch per row. Every target is at least
48 dp and every button and mark has a spoken name.


Workout notification (2026-10-19): while the app is behind with a workout in progress (not one entered after the fact), `WorkoutNotice` shows a quiet ongoing notification on channel "Workout in progress", public on the lock screen: the workout's name, the time since it started counting up (the notification's own chronometer), and a line "Resting until 17:32:05 · Next: …" (`workoutNotifText` in `core/Outside.kt`). A tap opens the workout. It is taken back when the app returns and redrawn when the rest changes or ends. Kv `workout.notif` (on unless `off`; Settings, Workout, "Workout on the lock screen"). On Android 13 and later the notification permission is asked once at the first workout (kv `workout.notif.asked`) and when the switch is turned on; refused, the switch goes off.
## The summary's body figure views (2026-10-15)
The summary's "Muscles worked" figure has a `Choice` above it: **Training** (default, shaded by the sets of the week so far, as before), **Fatigue** and **Strength**, coloured exactly as the Body page's views of the same names (`fatigueColour`, `strengthColour`, the same `Legend`s, from `AppModel.muscleFigures`). The choice is not stored; the summary always opens on Training. No new model was added: fatigue and strength are the ones in `docs/domain.md`.

## Workout note

A workout has one line of note, stored in the Strong CSV's Workout Notes column (docs/data-format.md). It is written in the workout screen under the name and clock (`NoteLine` in `Workouts.kt`: a quiet italic "Add note" that becomes the note; a tap edits it in a single-line field; kept in the stored draft, older drafts read empty), and on the summary and the workout's page in the same way; there it is saved when the field is left (`AppModel.setWorkoutNote`, `Repo.setWorkoutNote`: only the Workout Notes field of that workout's rows, with an Undo in the snackbar). Correcting a finished workout carries the note through. History's card shows it as a quiet single line, ellipsised, and History's search looks in it. Hevy export puts it in `description`, the text export on a "Note:" line, JSON in `workoutNotes`; FitNotes and the plain CSV have no workout note and leave it out.

## Compared with last time (2026-10-16)

A section at the end of the workout summary (after "PRs", before "Muscles worked") and at the end of a finished workout's page: `CompareLastSection` in `CompareLast.kt`, computed in `remember` keyed on the workout list from `AppModel.sessionsIn`. A bold line "N of M improved" (M counts only exercises with an earlier session; "First time for all of these." when none), then one row per exercise in workout order: name, today's best set under it against last time's ("100 kg × 5  vs  97.5 kg × 5"), and on the right a sign and the change in words ("+2.5 kg, −1 rep", "same", "down −2.5 kg", "First time"). Direction never rests on colour: ▲ up, ▼ down, = same, ★ first time, plus the words; up takes the `done` meaning colour, everything else `onSurfaceVariant`, so Mono keeps the meaning. A row (48 dp, one spoken sentence) opens the exercise's History tab. Rule: `docs/domain.md`, "Compared with last time".

## Empty states

A screen or card with too little to draw does not show a bare "Not enough ..." line. It shows `EmptyPreview` (`Ui.kt`): a faint sample of what will appear (30 % opacity, not interactive, cleared from the semantics so a screen reader never reads fake numbers), one line saying how much more to log ("Log 2 more sessions of Bench Press to see its chart", counted where it can be, else a plain sentence), and where it makes sense a quiet button (Start workout, Log weight, Add a photo). The page's one main button stays the page's: the preview's action is a `QuietButton`. A tile that would show a dash takes `Tile(sample = true)`: a faint sample line under its words.

The samples are hand-written constants in `PreviewSamples` (`Ui.kt`: a rising line, bars, rows, a shaded body figure, photo frames), drawn in the theme's accent and so grey in Mono. They are never built from the user's data or from `original/`. A screen that is empty because of a period filter (it has data, just not in this range) keeps its plain line; the preview is for having too little data at all. Applied to: exercise Charts tab, Body weight card and weight page, Trends, Personal bests, Statistics (empty period), Home's 1RM / Volume / Monthly volume / Avg duration / chosen lift tiles and Weekly volume, Trained this week, Top muscles, Recent PRs, Recent workouts, Next workout and Fatigue cards, Home with no workouts, History, Profile's Milestones, Goals and Progress photos cards. Arrange home keeps its own placeholder for "A year ago".

## The share card (2026-10-16)
A finished workout's "Share" (its menu and the summary) opens a `Sheet` (`ShareWorkoutDialog`, `Share.kt`) with a live preview of the picture and three choices: "Share picture" (the one main button; PNG into `cache/shared/`, lent through the existing `FileProvider`), "Share as text" (the share sheet with `exportText`) and "Export FIT file" (as before). "Copy as text" and the template file stay on the workout's menu. The card is drawn with the current `Look`, not fixed colours: the page background with a card on it (`cardFill`, `cardBorder`, `CardShape`), the accent, the number font, the colour scale, so Mono and Negative come out grey or inverted on their own. It is laid out as 360 x 450 dp at a density of 3, so the PNG is always 1080 x 1350 px whatever the phone; the sheet shows it scaled down, and the same recorded `GraphicsLayer` is what is sent. Content: name, date and start time, a PR badge; Time, Volume (profile unit), Sets and PRs; the workout note on one line; up to 8 exercises (sets, name, best set or best round) and "+N more"; the body figure (front and back, the real outlines) shaded by this workout's sets only; the word Clean at the bottom, plain text.
- Left and right, a kind per half (2026-10-16, the user's words "separate failure, warmup etc, for left and right"; not yet run on a device). The round number at the left still opens "Change type" for both halves at once, and its menu gains "Left side type…" and "Right side type…". A half on its own: a hold on the half, or a tap on its side letter in the quiet line under its boxes (a 48 dp area; spoken "Left, set 2, failure, change type"), opens a dialog titled "Left side: change type" with the four kinds. When the halves differ the round shows its number in the default colour, each half's side mark gains its kind letter beside it (`L F`, `R N`, in the kind's colour; a compact row shows that quiet line only then), and a finished round (`RoundRow`) shows the letter before each half's value and says both kinds when read aloud. Equal halves look as before. No new colour.

## Presets inside the workout, dialogs and sheets (REMOVED in 1.1.0 stage 5: `workoutSkin()` has the Dark and Light shape only)

The preset's own style reaches into the workout in progress, its dialogs and sheets (2026-10-16, the user's word: "preset themes should be more pervasive"). Screens type no colour for this; they read `workoutSkin()` (`ui/theme/Skin.kt`, `Look.workoutSkin(scheme, meaning)`), which gives the set boxes, the tick box, the done and next row tints, the number pad's surface and keys and an outline for the small panels over the list. Light, Dark and AMOLED return exactly the values the screens had before (tested in `SkinTest`).

- **Mono**: boxes, ticks and keys are paper with a 1 dp ink outline; the box holding the pad has a 2 dp ink outline; text is ink and quiet text ink at 60 %. A ticked set is shown by ink: the tick box filled in ink with a paper check, the row tinted with ink at 10 % (the next set at 5 %), never a grey blob. The pad and `Sheet` are paper with an ink outline along the top edge; `QuietButton` and `DangerButton` are outlined in ink, the main button is filled ink (the accent is the ink). Dialogs go through `CleanDialog` (`Ui.kt`): a paper surface with a 1 dp ink outline (the scheme's high containers are paper in Mono). The undo bar, the rest card and the rest pill carry the same outline. Everything reads the scheme, so the negative look follows.
- **Linen**: boxes and the unticked tick box are the grey tile with no outline on the white sheet; a ticked set is a black (ink) tick box with a white check and a faint orange tint on the row (12 %, the next set 6 %); no blue or green. The scheme's high containers are white, so dialogs and tooltips are white with the sheet's large corner; the main button is black.
- **Light, Dark, AMOLED**: unchanged.

`CleanDialog` is `AlertDialog` with the same parameters (title, text, confirm and dismiss buttons, properties) and replaces it everywhere; only Mono draws its own surface. A text button inside a dialog stays a text button. Date and time pickers are the platform's and are not restyled. The rest panel's ring already follows the look (ink in Mono, dark in Linen).

## Iso-Lateral Row fix (2026-10-16)

**Removed 2026-11-02** (the user's words: "why is the review workout log in the app, it should be fixed outside the app"): the page, its Settings row and the Home notice are gone, and the fix is applied once by itself at start-up (`AppModel.fixIsoRowHistoryOnce`; see `docs/domain.md`). What follows describes the page as it was.

`IsoRowFix.kt`, a one-time page. Entry points, only while the plan is not empty and the fix is not applied: "Fix Iso-Lateral Row history" at the top of Settings, Data (with "Undo the Iso-Lateral Row fix" while the saved rows exist), and a small notice `Section` at the top of Home ("Correct your Iso-Lateral Row history", a tap opens the page, a quiet "Not now" hides it). The page: the rule in plain words (and that PRs, charts and volume follow by themselves), a Totals section (workouts moved to Wide Grip, workouts converted to left and right, set rows split, blocks left alone), then one `Section` per affected date with a `LinkRow` per block ("→ Wide Grip", "→ stays Iso-Lateral Row (Machine), left and right" or "Already left and right, unchanged"; a split lists every set as `F 90×9 → L 45×9 + R 45×9`; a tap opens the workout), a main "Back up and apply" and a quiet "Cancel". The button opens the file picker for a backup zip; nothing is written until it was saved, and a cancelled picker or a failed write says so and changes nothing. After applying, the page closes and the snackbar offers Undo for 8 seconds.

## Bar and plates (2026-10-19)
The plate calculator (a barbell set's menu, and the warm-up calculator's lines) uses the bar and plates one has: kv `plates.kg` / `plates.lb`, one per unit, `20|25,20,15:2,10:4` (the bar, then each size with how many plates in all after a colon, none meaning as many as needed; absent is the standard bar and plates). Rules in `core/Plates.kt` (`loadPlates`: largest first, never more of a size than half those owned, an odd plate left out; what cannot be made is said). Edited in "Bar and plates" (`PlateKitDialog` in `Plates.kt`), from the calculator's dialog or Settings, Workout, for the profile's unit; "Standard" removes the key.

## Template length (2026-10-19)
A template's usual length is the median of its latest five workouts of the same name that lasted a minute or more (`templateDurations` in `core/TemplateDuration.kt`), written `~55 min` or `~1 h 20 min`, rounded to five minutes (`estimateText`). It shows after "N days ago" on a template card, on the Today card's line and as "Usually takes" on the template's page (which before was the plain average of every session). A template never done shows none.

## Workout-day reminders (2026-10-19)
Off by default. Settings, Workout, "Remind me to train on": a chip per weekday (kv `remind.days`, ISO numbers joined by commas, absent when none) and, once a day is chosen, the time (kv `remind.time`, `HH:mm`, 18:00 unless set). On those days at that time a "Workout day" notification shows (channel "Workout reminders"), unless the active profile already has a workout logged that day; a tap opens the app. One inexact alarm is set for the next reminder (`nextReminder` in `core/Reminders.kt`, `WorkoutReminder` in `Reminders.kt`) and set again when it goes off, when the choice changes, at each start and after a restart or update of the phone (`RECEIVE_BOOT_COMPLETED`, a local permission). The notification permission is asked when the first day is chosen on Android 13 and later.

### Exercise search (2026-10-21, the user's words: "The search is very bad")

One matcher, `searchRank` in `core/ExerciseSearch.kt` (`ExerciseSearchTest`), used by the exercise list everywhere it appears (the Exercises tab, Add exercise and Replace exercise in a workout, the template editor's picker) and by History's search for workout and exercise names (notes are still found as typed). Before it, the list kept the names that contained the whole typed text as one piece, in A to Z order, so "press incline", "isolateral" or "db lateral" found nothing.

- Every typed word must match, in any order, ignoring case, brackets, hyphens and punctuation; a word may be a whole word of the name, its start, or anywhere inside the name with the spaces taken out ("isolateral", "push down" for Pushdown).
- Since 1.1.0 stage 6 (see "1.1.0 Library"): plurals also by `-ies` ("flies" finds Fly) and a plural's singular has its shorthand too ("rdls"); joined or split words match both ways ("pulldown" and "pull down", "facepull"); more shorthand: ghr, ht / hipthrust, bss, gm, hspu, cgbp, dl, sq, alt, pullup / chinup (each finds the other), pull up and chin up as two words, fly / flye / flyes / flies, pressdown / pushdown, skullcrusher, facepull, tbar, hex (trap bar).
- Shorthand and other words (`SYNONYMS`, `PHRASES`): db, bb, kb, sm, bw, ez, hs, ohp, rdl, sldl, bp, lp, cg, wg, inc, dec, ext, tri / tris / tricep, bi / bis / bicep, lat (lateral or lats), pec / pecs (chest), delt(s), quad, ham(s), glute, calf, ab, "pec deck" (Chest Fly (Machine), butterfly), "skull crusher", "military press"; a plural finds the singular.
- A word of four letters or more may be one edit off (two from eight letters), as a whole word or as the start of one; shorter words must be exact.
- A word not in the name may be in the exercise's other names (`otherNames`), then its main muscles and its equipment filter word.
- Order: the name itself, then names that start with what was typed, then every word whole or as a word's start, then inside the name, then other names, then slips, then muscles or equipment; ties go to the exercise done most often, then most recently, then A to Z. While something is typed the list is that one ranked list, without Favourites, Recent or letter headings; with the box empty it is as before. "Create …" is offered as before.
- Speed (2026-10-22, the user's words: "Search is very laggy and slow"). The list used to be ranked in composition on the main thread, reading the query again for every exercise and allocating a table per typo check: 3.4 ms per keystroke over the 1605 built-in exercises on the PC's JVM, several times that on a phone, with every keystroke waiting for it. Now each exercise's words are read once per list (`SearchTarget`, built off the main thread), the query once per keystroke (`SearchQuery`, which also holds the typo check's rows and stops a check once it cannot come under the limit, and tries the other names before any slip), and the list is made on `Dispatchers.Default` with the newest keystroke cancelling the older search; the previous list stays on screen until the new one is ready, and "Create …" waits for the search it depends on. 0.73 ms per keystroke (`ExerciseSearchSpeedTest`, budget 1.5 ms). History's search reads the query once and matches off the main thread the same way.

## Numbers and dates in lines (2026-10-25)

An estimated 1RM standing on its own is shown to a tenth in the shown unit (`showEstimate`, `tenth`); logged weights keep their own
decimals. No estimate is shown beside a warm-up. A tile's trend line stops at the last finished week or month (`finished()`). A day in a
line carries the year only when it is not this year's (`prettyDay`). The Previous column brackets last time's set type only when it
differs from the row's. Proposals still open are in `docs/uiux-pass.md`.

### Correcting a finished workout (2026-11-02, the user's words: "it should be a different system to correct exercises, and you should be able to edit the start and end times")

Edit on a finished workout's page opens the **Correct** page (`CorrectScreen` in `Active.kt`), not the workout screen: a page with the usual back bar titled "Correct workout" and a Save button. Under it the workout's name (typed in place), its note, **Start** and **End** (each opens the phone's date and time pickers; moving the start keeps the end, so the length follows) and **Duration**; with the end not after the start, Duration is replaced by a red line and Save is greyed. Then the workout's cards, as in a workout (same set rows, number pad, set types, RPE, notes, add or remove sets and exercises, the card menu's Move up and down), with no tick boxes: every set that holds its values counts when saved, an empty one is left out. There is no clock, rest timer, focus mode, PR celebration, banner or notification, and it is apart from the workout in progress (`AppModel.correction`, a second draft): one may run while another is corrected. Save replaces the stored workout in one transaction (`Repo.correctWorkout`) and everything derived follows; Back with no change leaves at once, with changes it asks "Discard changes?". A correction left in the workout in progress by a version before this one moves to the Correct page at start-up.


## What's new (2026-11-16, the user's design)
Once after an update, the newest version's changes show as a card in the middle of the screen with the app dimmed behind it: the title "What's new in <version>", a close button, one change per page to swipe through and dots for where you are (`WhatsNew.kt`, `WhatsNewPopup`). Back or a tap outside also closes it. Only the newest version is shown; every version is listed under Settings, Credits, beside the Source code link. The text is `app/src/main/assets/changes.md` (one `## <versionName>` section per version, one `- Title: sentence` line per change; add the section before a release). The rule is `whatsNewDue` in `core/WhatsNew.kt` (`WhatsNewTest`): shown when the running versionName has a section and kv `whatsnew.seen` (the last version shown) differs; a first run (the welcome questionnaire is due) records the version as seen and shows nothing.

## 1.1.0 shell: tabs, plus button, Workout sheet, running look (2026-11-17, stage 1 of `docs/overhaul-1-1.md`; where this and older sections disagree, this wins)

- Three swipeable tabs: Home, Progress, Library (`Tab.Exercises`, label "Library"). The Workout and Profile tabs are gone; Profile is `Screen.Profile`, a page with a back bar opened by the initial at Home's top right; Settings by the gear beside it. Both are 34 dp round `GlassButton`s (48 dp touch area).
- Everything that floats takes one material, `Modifier.glass(shape)` in `Ui.kt` (tab bar, plus button, round buttons, the Workout sheet); stage 2 made it real glass, see "1.1.0 glass" below.
- Tab bar: 60 dp stadium, three slots, 24 dp outline icon over its word (the words hide while a workout runs, to make room), the selected tab in a soft pill (white 11 % in Dark, black 7 % in Light) that glides and follows a swipe; no accent on the bar. It always floats (the old settling into the bottom edge is gone). Beside it, 8 dp away, the plus button: round, as tall as the bar, a plus in the text colour. Running: a pill with a pulsing accent dot (steady with animations off), the workout's name and clock, or "Rest m:ss"; a tap resumes. The docked banner and its setting are gone.
- Workout sheet (`WorkoutSheetContent` in `Templates.kt`, hosted by `WorkoutSheet` in `MainActivity.kt`, `AppModel.workoutSheet`): 90 % tall over the current page, which shrinks to 93 % and dims. "Start a workout", New template, a menu (make from past workouts, import, export), a search over template names, "Quick start, empty", then every template under its folder heading (name, last done, a quiet "Up next" on the one Up next picks; a tap opens the template page, a hold or the ⋮ opens Start, Edit, Folder, Colour, Share, Delete). Closes on a tap outside, a drag down on its top, Back, or anything that opens a page. `clean://open?tab=workout` opens it (resumes a running workout instead).
- Running look (D6a): `RunningLook` wraps the tabs page and every page but the workout's own; the page colour takes 6 % of the accent, glass the same, back to normal when the workout ends.
- Current split (D5): kv `split.on` (absent = off), a switch under Settings, Workout. Off, the folder is an ordinary one and Up next is `upNextTemplate` in `core/UpNext.kt`: among templates used in the last 28 days (workout name equals template name) the one done longest ago; none used then, the most recent; templates never used, the first; no templates, the Quick start. On: the old rotation. A program still decides first. Used by the Today card, Next workout card, widgets and the sheet's marker.

## 1.1.0 glass (2026-11-17, stage 2 of `docs/overhaul-1-1.md`)

- One material, `Modifier.glass(shape, haze, lift)` in `Ui.kt`. Neutral: no coloured outline, no glow. Dark: tint rgba(30,31,35,0.74) over a 22 dp blur, a white light from 9 % at the top to 2 % at the bottom, a 1 px lit top edge (white 20 %), a darker 1 px bottom edge (black 45 %), a hairline inside (white 4 %), a soft wide shadow below (black 60 %, 14 dp lift). Light: white tint from 85 % at the top to 70 % at the bottom, top edge pure white, bottom edge black 6 %, shadow black 16 %. The tint is the least that keeps text at 4.5 : 1 over any blur; it is never more transparent.
- While a workout runs the tint moves 15 % toward the accent (Light 10 %; Dark becomes about rgba(38,52,82,0.74)), and the page colour takes 8 % of the accent in Dark, 6 % in Light (`RunningLook`). The workout page itself stays untinted.
