- Blur is Haze (1.7.1) on Android 12+: the tab bar and plus button blur the tabs pager (`hazeSource` on the pager, off while a page covers the tabs), the Workout sheet blurs the page area (`hazeSource` in `App`, only while the sheet is up). Nothing is recorded or blurred while nothing floats.
- Solid fallback: Android below 12, **Reduce transparency** (Settings, Appearance; kv `glass.reduce`, off by default; hint "Solid bars, sheets and menus") or the phone's own high-contrast text setting. The same material is drawn with an opaque tint (97 %) and no blur. The accent tint stays. Surfaces that cannot see a blur source (Home's round buttons sit inside the page that is the source; dialogs and menus are other windows) are always the solid version.

## 1.1.0 Home and Profile (2026-11-17, stage 3 of `docs/overhaul-1-1.md`)

- Home (`Home.kt`) is fixed; nothing is arranged, hidden or added. Header: small "Today" over the date as one line in the phone's own short form ("Tue 17 Nov", `shortDay`), title letter spacing -2 %, line height 110 %, the two round glass buttons at the right (the week-streak chip moved to Activity). Then, top to bottom:
  - **Up next** (`TodayCard` in `Program.kt`), the only raised card: label, template name, a grey line "Last done Tue 10 Nov · 6 exercises · about 1 h 5 min" (parts that do not exist are left out), the program's reason line when a program decides, the deload and readiness hints, one main button "Start <Name>" and a quiet "Not this one" when there is another choice. A workout running turns it into **In progress**: name, clock, "3 of 18 sets · Next: <exercise>", Resume. No template: the Quick start card. The card grows into the template's page (card key `home:today`).
  - **Weight**, on the page: small uppercase heading, the latest weight large with its unit small, "+0.4 kg in 30 days" grey, a round "+" that opens the ruler; the row opens the weight page. No weigh-in: "Log your weight" and the "+".
  - **Activity**, on the page: heading with "1 of 3 this week · 30-week streak" at the right (the streak opens Trophies, the week opens the calendar), and the calendar of the last 17 weeks fitted to the width (`HomeCalendar`, `Calendar(span = 17)` in `Stats.kt`; accent shades, today ringed, no scrolling). A tap opens the full calendar at that day; logging a past workout by holding a day is on the calendar page. Its first-fill wave has its own key (`home-activity`).
  - **Last workout**, a plain row: name, "Sat 15 Nov · 1 h 04 · 3 PRs" grey, a chevron.
  - A profile with no workouts keeps the first-run card. Kv `home.cards`, `home.layout`, `home.charts`, `home.lift:*` and `profile.cards` are ignored. Settings, Appearance has no Home section.
- Profile (`Profile.kt`, `Screen.Profile`) is a page with a back bar, fixed: the initial in a circle, the name and "kg · since April 2026 · Edit" (Edit opens the Settings profile editor), then sections of a small heading and rows split by hairlines, no cards: **Measurements** ("Add" opens New measurement; the latest of each with its day, Height ("Add" when empty), BMI once there is a height and a weigh-in, "All measurements"), **Photos** (newest three thumbnails, "Compare" at the heading, or one quiet "Add a photo"), **Goals and trophies** (a row per goal with its percent and "On track for ..." line, Trophies with "N earned", "Set a goal" when none), **Lifetime** (workouts, time trained, volume lifted as plain lines), **Switch profile**. An empty part is one quiet line, never an empty box. No morphs from Profile rows (a plain page change).

## 1.1.0 Progress and its pages (2026-11-17, stage 4 of `docs/overhaul-1-1.md`)

- **Progress** (`Progress.kt`): the title, the History card top left and the Body card top right exactly as before (keys `progress:history`, `progress:body`, their morphs), then `StatisticsSection`, laid out on the page with no card (D2): a small uppercase "Statistics" heading (`PageSection`); the period as a `Segmented` control (Week / Month / Year / All, kv `ui.stats.period`, default Month); the four numbers in one row (workouts, volume with its unit in the label, time trained, sets), each a `titleLarge` number that rolls (`RollingText`, `Roll.kt`) with a small grey label under it; one grey sentence against the same elapsed part of the period before (`comparisonSentence` in `core/StatsPeriod.kt`: "1 fewer workout than October, volume down 8 %"; Week says "last week", Month the month's name, Year "last year"; All has none); `VolumeBars` (the bar holding today in the accent, the others `onSurface` at 22 %, coming days a faint stub); then hairline-separated `NavRow`s with their headline at the right: Personal bests ("18 this month", PRs counted in the calendar month), Trends ("N this week"), Trophies ("30-week streak"), Calendar ("N this month"), keys `progress:stat:bests|trends|trophies|calendar`. The Activity calendar card, the Measurements and BMI rows and the "Most trained muscle" / "Top exercise" lines are gone from Progress (the calendar lives on Home and behind the Calendar row; Measurements and BMI on Profile).
- **Most trained muscle** is a row at the top of the Body page (the muscle with most sets in the Body page's time frame, opens its page, key `progress:stat:muscle`); **Top exercise** is a row at the top of Personal bests (most working sets over the whole history, opens the exercise).
- **`PageSection(title) { }`** (`Ui.kt`) is the D2 section: a `PageHeading` (small uppercase grey) over the content, air around it, no fill. Trends, Trophies and Muscles-to-set use it; the Calendar page's months and the History page's months are small uppercase headings (`Caption`) with the count at the right in small grey; Personal bests' months likewise.
- **`Segmented(options, selected, label, morph?)`** (`Ui.kt`): a quiet track with the chosen option a raised pill, for exclusive switches that change what the page shows: the statistics period, Trends' and the Calendar's range / measure, Personal bests' two views, the Body page's view switch (the chosen word is `MorphText` role `view`, so the Body card's word still travels into it) and its time frame. Chips (`Choice`) stay for filters.
- **History page**: one card per workout, tighter (inner padding `Space.m`, outer `Space.xs`); the name wraps once (two lines) and never ends in an ellipsis; each exercise line is a small grey count ("3 ×") and its name, also wrapping once.
- **Body page**: the figure sits on the page with no frame (the muscle block is a plain column, not a `Section`), the view switch and time frame are segmented controls, muscle rows were already plain lines.
- **B4, depth** (one place, `Common.kt`): `cardFill()` is always solid, so AMOLED's see-through card is the lighter step above the page; `cardBorder()` draws an outline only when the look chose its own colour (Ink in Mono, Linen's hairline), so the bright thin outline is gone; `Modifier.cardDepth(shape)` is the soft wide shadow (16 dp, black 16 % spot) in Light only and is used by `Section` and `MiniPage`; Home's Up next card is a `Section(hero = true)` and takes all three (its accent tint is kept, its accent outline is gone). Secondary text is the theme's `onSurfaceVariant`, already the tinted grey. Mix and match's Outlines "thin" and Cards "clear" therefore draw no outline and a solid card until stage 5 removes them.

## 1.1.0 Looks and Settings (2026-11-17, stage 5 of `docs/overhaul-1-1.md`; where this and older sections disagree, this wins)

- **Two looks, one accent.** Theme is **Dark / Light / Phone** (kv `look.theme`: `dark` default, `light`, `phone` follows the system), with a **Pure black background** switch (`look.black`, Dark only, off by default: Dark's page is a near-black slate `#121316` with cards one step lighter `#1C1E24`; with the switch the page is black and the cards `#121316`; Light's page is `#F3F4F6` under white cards with a soft shadow) and **one accent of six** (`look.accent`: Blue, Teal, Green, Orange, Pink, Purple, blue first and default; each has a shade for dark and one for light that keep 4.5 : 1 against their page). The accent drives buttons, selection, links, the running tint and the **colour scale** (calendar, body figure, chart shading, `Look.scale`: four steps of the accent from dim to bright, from the page up on Dark, from white down on Light); meaning colours (green PR, amber matched and warm-up, red delete, fatigue, strength and BMI, and their colour-blind safe set) never follow the accent. The font is Inter everywhere with tabular digits (`typography()` sets `tnum`; no imported font, no number font). Widgets, the rest notification and the share card draw in Dark or Light and the accent only.
- **REMOVED**: Mono, Linen (and its sheet layout), Day and night, Mix and match (every `Part`), saved looks, the icon accent and colour scale pickers, the 40 launcher icon aliases (one launcher activity with the app's own icon, plus a monochrome layer for Android 13's themed icons: `tools/icons/make_icons.py` writes it), imported fonts, Live preview, the Tab bar page, template colours, chart style (always the smooth line), rest timer styles (one: the glass panel that shrinks to a pill after 5 s, back for the last 10 s, preset chips 60 / 90 / 120 / 180 on the panel), tick feedback (light, none with Animations off), the PR celebration choice (one moment: the landing badge with its shine, and the chime with two taps, silent with Animations off), set-row density (comfortable), one-handed mode, swipe directions (right finishes, left removes), the exercise tab choice (History), the compact rest notification (expanded only), the launcher shortcut chooser (Start, Resume while a workout runs, Log weight), the rest tone choice (the deep bell; it came back in the 1.1.0 remake, stage 5, and kv `rest.tone` is read again), and the Lists page (stalled-exercise notes, week totals in the calendar and the hidden built-ins are fixed on). Focus mode stays as the All / Focus switch in the workout's top bar (kv `workout.focus`, remembered).
- **Migration** (once at start-up before the first page, `migrateLook` in `core/LookMigration.kt`, idempotent through kv `look.v1`, never throws): `theme.preset` amoled becomes Dark with Pure black on; dark Dark; light Light; mono and linen Dark; daynight Phone; no preset or an unknown one (a saved look) is amoled, the old default. The older single options still on top count as the user saw them: `theme` Light / Dark / System becomes Light / Dark / Phone (not under Day and night), and for a dark look `background` Black / Dark grey sets Pure black. Light never has pure black. The accent is kept when it is one of the six, else the nearest by hue (Red gives Orange; a hand-picked colour by its hue; a grey, Ink or anything unreadable Blue); with no accent stored the preset's own (orange in light and linen, else blue). A phone with none of the old keys only gets the marker. The old keys stay in the database and are ignored. An imported `font-*` file in the app's folder is deleted at start-up, and a backup zip that carries one has it ignored.
- **Settings (D4, F5)** are four pages: the main page is the search field and four rows with a one-line hint, **Workout**, **Appearance**, **Data and backup**, **About and help**. Sections sit on the page under a small grey heading (no cards). **Appearance**: Theme (a segmented control), Accent (six swatches), Text size, Background (Pure black, greyed with a hint in Light), Reduce transparency (`glass.reduce`, also on with the phone's own setting), Colour-blind safe colours (`theme.cbsafe`), Animations (`ui.motion`, on by default; the old Performance mode turned over), and two older choices kept at the foot: the sticky note colour and Blur progress photos. **Workout**: Rest (default rest, rest between sides, "Rest by set type" in a dialog for warm-up, failure, drop and after a hard set, when it ends Sound / Buzz / Both, Alert outside the app, go back when the rest ends), Training (weekly target, Program, Current split, Suggest what to try, Celebrate on finishing), Estimated 1RM (formula, Factor in RPE, rep limit), Weekly sets per muscle, Auto-fill sets, and In a workout (summary, RPE key, next set, shrink finished exercises, lock screen notification, reminders, bar and plates). **Data and backup**: backup and restore, import, export, Health Connect, exercise animations, templates, renamed exercises with undo, notes to variations, delete everything (its confirm is unchanged until stage 7). **About and help**: source code (the repository address as selectable text, nothing is opened), what's new, licences and credits, tips. Profile editing and switching is a dialog on the Profile page (`ProfileEdit.kt`); Settings has no Profile heading. `SettingsIndex.kt` has one line per section; `SettingsIndexTest` checks every keyed item has one and that none of the removed settings can be found.

## 1.1.0 Library (2026-11-18, stage 6 of `docs/overhaul-1-1.md`; where this and older sections disagree, this wins)

- **The Library tab** (`ExercisesTab`, `ExerciseList` in `Exercises.kt`): title "Library", a search field whose placeholder has the real count ("Search 1,580 exercises"), and four chips on one scrolling line: **Muscle** and **Equipment** (a tap opens a `Sheet` of choices, "Any ..." first, the chosen one ticked; the chip then shows the choice), **Performed** and **Favourites** (toggles), and **Clear all** once any is on. They combine. Performed is the old All / Performed choice and is remembered under the same kv key `exercises.show` (`all` / `performed`); a picker keeps its own chips in memory only. Nothing fits the filters: one grey line.
- **Rows**: the name wraps and is never cut (`MorphText`, body size), under it in small grey "Sun 15 Nov · 100 × 6" (last day and best working set, `bestSetLine`: heaviest by the Epley figure, shown in the exercise's unit; reps only "12 reps", a hold "45s") or, never done, "Chest · Dumbbell" (main muscle, equipment; "Other" is left out); a small accent star only on a favourite; the animation thumbnail, the checkbox of a multi-pick and the morph key `exercises:<row key>` are kept. A muscle under a name no longer opens that muscle's page.
- **At rest** (nothing typed): small uppercase grey headings (`Caption`), rows hairline separated, no cards: **Favourites**; **Recent**, the five most recently performed, then a "Show all N" row that opens every performed exercise newest first in place and "Show fewer" that folds it (the state is kept while the app runs); then A to Z under letters with the letter rail. Built-in exercises never used stay out of A to Z until searched for, filtered or listed with the note at the foot, as before.
- **Searching** (`rankLibrary` in `core/LibraryRanking.kt`, `LibraryRankingTest`): one list in two groups under quiet headings, "You've done these" (favourites, then performed exercises) and "More in the library" (shown only when the first group has something). Order in the first group: favourites first; then the closer match (`SearchQuery.rank` tier, 0 to 6); then more sessions in the last 12 weeks; then the newest day; then more sessions in all; then A to Z. A typing slip (tier 5) on a performed exercise is therefore above an exact match on a never-done one. In the second: the closer match, then A to Z. The chips filter before ranking. Nothing under a result says why it matched. The ranking runs off the main thread as before (`listRows` on `Dispatchers.Default`, `ensureActive` every 128 exercises); the entries (`LibraryEntry`: search words plus favourite, sessions, sessions in the last 12 weeks, last day) are built once per list.
- **Nothing found**: "No exercise called “...”" in grey, then a tappable "Did you mean <name>?" when one is close (`nearestName`: what was typed run together against the whole name, each word and each start of whole words, at most one edit in four letters and three at most, with swaps; of equal distance the exercise done most, then the shorter name; a tap puts it in the search field), then "Create “...”" (the old create flow). The pickers offer Create as before (at the top while something matches, at the end here).
- **The pickers** (Add exercise, Replace exercise, the template editor, a goal's exercise) are the same `ExerciseList`: same search, ranking, chips and rows; they keep their own tick boxes.

## 1.1.0 every other page (2026-11-18, stage 7a of `docs/overhaul-1-1.md`; F6, F7, B1, B2, B9, B10, C7; where this and older sections disagree, this wins)

- **D2 in one place.** `Section(title, ..., card = hero || onClick != null)` in `Common.kt` is a raised card only when it opens something (or is the hero); otherwise it is a section on the page: a small uppercase grey heading (up to two lines) over its content, no fill, no shadow, air around it. So the exercise page's Info, PRs (label and value rows, best reps and longest hold per weight, rep maxes), Charts and the template, summary, muscle, head, week, month, routine, PR history and BMI pages have no boxes; what stays a card is one thing you open: a past session on an exercise's History tab, a workout on the History page, the summary's celebration and Trophies cards, Home's Up next, the first-run card (`card = true`). `LocalHalfCard`, `LocalEdgeCharts`, `ActivityCard`, `BANNER_CARD` and the Activity tip are gone.
- **B1.** `typography()` (`ui/theme/Type.kt`) gives the headline roles and `titleLarge` letter spacing -2 % and line height 110 %, so every page title follows. The back bar's title may take two lines (B9); a name is never cut to one.
- **B9.** Names wrap once: the back bar title, the exercise page's session cards and the share card's exercise lines no longer stop at one line.
- **B10.** One outline family: `ShieldLine`, `TimerLine` and `DumbbellLine` replaced the filled Shield, Timer and Dumbbell; the favourite star in the Library and the PR star in the History card are `StarLine`. A filled star stays only where it is a state (favourite on, trophy earned) or a celebration.
- **Range switches** (`RangeChoice`: 3M / 1Y / All on charts, Week / 30d / 90d / All on muscle and head pages) are `Segmented`; chips stay for filters. The Library's filter chips wrap onto a second line instead of running off the edge.
- **Template page**: sections (Plan, Exercises, What it trains, History, ...) and Start is the page's one main button, in the bottom bar.
- **Glass on the workout page** (C7, the page itself stays as it was): the list is the blur source (`hazeSource`, one `HazeState` in `ActiveScreen`, provided as `LocalHaze`). The number pad is no longer a Scaffold bottom bar but floats over the bottom of the list (`Pad.height`; the list adds that much room at its end, and a box typed into is brought clear of the pad, `PadField`), as glass (`SheetTopShape`, keys stay solid). The rest panel (`RestFull`) and the rest pill are glass over the list; the Undo bar is the solid form (no source behind it at the top). A finished workout's floating totals bar is glass over its list. Where nothing can blur (Android below 12, Reduce transparency) the same material is solid.
- **Dialogs and menus**: `CleanDialog` and `CleanMenu` (`Ui.kt`, used for every dropdown) draw the glass material in its solid form (they are their own windows): the same tint, lit top edge and shadow, the corner `GlassWindowShape` (24 dp, the sheets').

## 1.1.0 interactions (2026-11-18, stage 7b of `docs/overhaul-1-1.md`; B5 to B8, C2 to C4, C8, D2 hairlines; where this and older sections disagree, this wins)

- **Hairlines (D2).** A page section (`Section` without a card, `PageSection`) draws a 1 px `outlineVariant` line in the gap between two neighbouring `LabelValue` rows (`RowLines`, `rowLine`, `drawRowLines` in `Ui.kt`): the row reports its place, the section draws, so a chart or figure between rows, a lone row and the section's ends get no line; inside a lined section a row gets 6 dp more air above and below. Nothing in a card, a dialog or a chart.
- **Hold for a menu (B5, C1).** One gesture everywhere, `Modifier.holdMenu(shape, actions)` (`Hold.kt`): a long press (not a drag) on a History or Home workout, a Library exercise row, a template row in the Workout sheet or Home's Up next card. The page dims (and blurs on Android 12+ unless Reduce transparency), the held item is lifted in place (scale 1.03, a 24 dp shadow, on its own fill, from a picture of it that is drawn only from the touch to the hold, so lists pay nothing), and its actions sit in the one glass `CleanMenu` anchored to it; a tap outside, Back, or an action closes it. Taps are unchanged: after the hold the rest of the touch is swallowed. With animations off the lift is there at once. Actions: workout (Open, Start again, Save as template, Share, Delete in red; Undo as before), exercise (Open, Favourite / Unfavourite, Edit), template (Open, Start, Edit, Delete in red; the ⋮ sheet keeps Folder and Share), Up next (Open, Start, Edit). TalkBack gets the same actions as custom actions on the item.
- **Sheets (B6).** `Sheet` is the one glass material in its solid form with a grab handle; the handle and title are a drag area that follows the finger (closes past a quarter of the height or on a quick flick; the content below scrolls), a tap outside or Back closes, "Close" is a screen reader action on the handle. While any sheet is open (`SheetDepth`) the page behind shrinks 4 % and dims; the Workout sheet still shrinks it 7 %.
- **Slide to confirm (B7).** `SlideToConfirm(label, spoken, onConfirm)` (`Slide.kt`): a danger-tinted track, a danger knob dragged to the end (90 %); released early it springs back (`Motion.follow`); TalkBack gets one plain action named `spoken`. Used for Delete everything, Restore a backup ("Replace everything") and cancelling a workout that has ticked sets (which keeps its Undo as well).
- **Say what happened (B8).** `NoticeToast` (`Ui.kt`) is the one line (glass, top, 4 s, 6 s with an action) for `AppModel.notice`; `UndoBar` is the same material. A saved workout says "Push saved", then "Push saved · 3 PRs" once the records are worked out, with View (`AppModel.saidSaved`). Import and backup say what they are doing in words from real figures only ("Reading the file", "Reading 84 KB", "Adding 12 workouts", "Backing up 214 workouts and everything else"); no percent is shown because the code has none. A failed import shows the parser's own message, which names the row or record and the problem.
- **Accessibility (C2, C4).** Spoken names: the plus button ("Start a workout"; running "Back to workout Push, 24 minutes"), the round buttons, the Workout sheet (pane title) and its handle ("Workout sheet handle. Drag down to close", with a Close action), the slide. A hardware keyboard types into the open number pad (digits and numpad digits, point or comma, Backspace; Enter is Next; `padKeyOf`).
- **Scroll and back (C3).** Each tab's `LazyListState` is held by `AppModel.tabLists`, so a tab keeps its place across tab switches and page round trips. The manifest turns on predictive back (`enableOnBackInvokedCallback`); the app's own back handling is `BackHandler`s and the `AppModel` stack, which work with it.
- **The plus into the clock pill (C8).** The plus button's content is an `AnimatedContent` whose size follows `Motion.pill()`, its content crossing over on `Motion.quick()`; cut with animations off. Edge to edge is on (`enableEdgeToEdge` in `MainActivity`).

## 1.1.0 remake: themes and Lit (2026-11-18, stage 1 of `docs/remake-1-1.md`; where this and older sections disagree, this wins)

- **Eight themes** replace the six accents (`THEMES` in `ui/theme/Look.kt`; kv `look.accent` holds the name, default Graphite): Graphite, Ember, Crimson, Moss, Ocean, Plum, Ice, Rose, each a dark ground, an accent, the ink on it, a tint, an ambient light and a Light-mode accent, with the exact values of `artifacts/round3/round3.py`. `Look` gained `themeName`, `ink`, `ambient` (the light with its alpha: white 7 % for Graphite, else the accent at 14 to 16 %), `card`, `tile` and `tint`; `ground` is the page. The six old accents are migrated once (`migrateLookTheme`, kv `look.v2`): Blue to Graphite, Teal to Ice, Green to Moss, Orange to Ember, Pink to Rose, Purple to Plum, anything else to Graphite.
- **Surfaces.** Dark: the page is the ground (black with Pure black); a card is the tint at 5.5 % over it (`Look.raised` / `card`, the Material `surfaceContainer`), a tile 4.5 % (`Look.tile`), `higher` 10 %. Light: the page is white tinted 4 % toward the Light accent, cards and tiles white. The data scale is four steps of the accent (Graphite: four greys up to near-white). Text on an accent fill is `ink` (it is Material's `onPrimary` / `onSecondary`, so every filled button follows; Light's ink is white).
- **Lit** (the tab pages: Home, Progress, Library; Profile later): `Modifier.ambientLight(light, top)` draws the theme's light once, behind the tabs pager in `Tabs()`: an ellipse the screen's width plus 40 dp each side, 540 dp tall, its top 230 dp above the screen, a radial gradient to nothing; it stays put while a page scrolls. A card is its fill, a 1 dp lit top edge (`Modifier.litEdge`, white 9 %; in Light a faint dark hairline) and a soft wide shadow (`cardDepth`, now in Dark too). A tile is `Modifier.tile(shape)`: the tile fill and a lit edge of 8 %, no shadow (use it for the tiles of Home and Profile). `Modifier.glass` takes its base colour from the theme's ground (a touch of the tint on it) instead of the fixed grey; its contrast rules, Reduce transparency path and running tint are as before.
- **Quiet** (`ActiveScreen`, its number pad and rest panel): no ambient light, `LocalSolidGlass` is true there so the glass has no blur; a done set row is the accent at 8 %, the current row one step raised (`Look.raised`), the tick a filled accent circle with `ink` (`WorkoutSkin`).
- **Crimson**: red is its accent, so what deletes is drawn in the text colour (`Look.meaning().danger`) and `DangerButton` carries a bin icon. Every other theme keeps red for deletion.
- **Appearance page**: Mode (Dark / Light / Phone) as the segmented control, a 4 x 2 grid of theme swatches (`ThemeSwatch` in `Settings.kt`: the ground with its light, a faint bar, the accent as a pill; the chosen one ringed in its accent with a bold name), Pure black, Reduce transparency, Text size, Colour-blind safe. Search finds "Theme" by the eight names.

## 1.1.0 remake: the dock (2026-11-18, stage 2 of `docs/remake-1-1.md`; where this and older sections disagree, this wins)

- **One glass bar with fully round ends** (`PillShape`, 64 dp tall) holds four tabs: Home, Progress, Library, Profile (`TAB_ORDER`; `Tab.Profile` is new), each an outline icon of 24 dp (`CleanIcons.TabPerson` for Profile) with its word under it. The icon sits at a fixed height (centre 22 dp from the bar's top with its word, the bar's middle without), so the indicator can be drawn behind it whatever the text size. The word is laid out at its bold width, so it does not shift when it turns bold.
- **The selected tab is a circle** (42 dp, never wider than a slot; white 12 % with a 1 dp lit top edge in Dark, black 7 % in Light) behind its icon. It is `rememberStretch`'s span as before, now drawn between slot centres: it glides on `Motion.pill`, leading edge first, so mid-glide it is a short capsule and it settles as a circle; a swipe moves it with the finger. The selected word is bold.
- **The plus** is a separate circle to the right of the bar, as tall as it, filled with the accent (`Look.orange`) with the plus in `Look.ink` and a soft accent shadow; press scale as before. With a workout running the accent fill fades out on `Motion.quick()` as the plus grows into the glass clock pill (`AnimatedContent`, `Motion.pill()`), unchanged otherwise. The bar's words hide while it runs, and the circle shrinks to fit a slot.
- **Profile is the fourth tab** (`ProfileTab` in `Profile.kt`): the old page's content inside the pager with room for the dock, its list kept in `tabLists`. Its header is the large title "Profile" with the round gear (Settings) at the right, a `GlassButton`. Home's header lost both round buttons. `Screen.Profile` is removed (nothing else opened it); a saved back stack that still names it drops that page (the codec leaves out what it cannot read). `clean://open?tab=profile` selects the tab; Back from Profile goes to Home like the other tabs.

## 1.1.0 remake: Home and Progress restored (2026-11-18, stage 3 of `docs/remake-1-1.md`; where this and older sections disagree, this wins)

- **Home**, top to bottom: the date (weekday small and grey, "18 November" large; nothing at the right), the Up next / In progress / Quick start card as before, **Activity** on the page (no card), a two-column grid of six **tiles**, room for the dock. 24 dp or so between the sections, 12 dp between tiles. An empty profile keeps the first-run card.
- **Activity** (`ActivityBody` in `Stats.kt`, one composable for Home and Progress): the title "Activity" with "since Apr 2025" at its right, a `Segmented` Time / Volume, the calendar scrolling back to the first workout (it opens at today, today ringed, month labels, 26 weeks across the width, never fewer than that), under it "‹ drag back to Apr 2025" at the left (only when the history is longer than the screen) and Less, the four `look.scale` squares, More at the right. A day opens the calendar at that day; the title brings the chart back to today when it was dragged away and otherwise opens the full calendar; three seconds after the last touch the chart drifts back. The first view of each calendar plays the wave once per session (`home-activity` on Home, `activity` on Progress, `calendar` on the page). `Calendar(span)` (Home's fixed 17 weeks) is gone.
- **Progress** has the same Activity as a card (the card opens the calendar) under History and Body, above the statistics; it is left out with no workouts.
- **Tiles** (`Tile`, `TileNumber`, `TileLine`, `TileAdd` in `Stats.kt`; the tiles themselves in `Home.kt`): `Modifier.tile(cardShape())` (the tint at 4.5 %, a lit edge of 8 %, no shadow), 16 dp inside, at least 132 dp tall, a row's two tiles as tall as each other; a small grey uppercase caption, the number at 28 sp with its unit small and grey (it steps down rather than wrapping, and rolls when it changes), a grey line, an optional `Sparkline(wash = true)` (the accent's soft wash under the line and a dot at its end). Each tile is a card with a key (`home:weight`, `home:week`, `home:onerm0`, `home:onerm1`, `home:volume`, `home:last`) and opens its page the way a card does:
  - **Weight**: latest weigh-in, "+0.4 since 19 Oct" (from the first weigh-in of the last 30 days, else from the one before), a chart of weekly means (`chart:weight`), a round "+" that opens the ruler sheet on Home; the tile opens the weight page. No weigh-in: "Log your first weigh-in".
  - **This week**: the ring (workouts against the weekly target), "1 of 3", "30-week streak" (or "No streak yet"); opens Trophies.
  - **1RM of the most logged lift** and **of the second** (the exercise's name as the caption; the number with "kg 1RM"; "+2.5 in 8 weeks", or "since 12 Oct" when the lift has less history; a chart of its estimates, `chart:1rm`): the lifts are the most done in the last twelve weeks that have at least two sessions with an estimate; they open that exercise's Charts tab. With no such lift one quiet tile says "Log 2 sessions of a lift"; with only one, the second is left out and the grid closes up.
  - **Volume this week**: the number with its unit, "+12 % on last week" in grey. It sets this week so far against the same days of last week (set against the whole of last week it would read as a fall early in every week); with nothing last week it says so; opens Trends.
  - **Last workout**: its name, "Sun 15 Nov · 1 h 05" in grey, "★ 3 PRs" in the theme's green when it set any; opens the workout, and a hold on it gives the workout menu.
- Gone: the 1.1.0 Weight row, the fixed 17-week calendar with the week and streak in its heading, and the last-workout row.

## 1.1.0 remake: Profile (2026-11-18, stage 4 of `docs/remake-1-1.md`; where this and older sections disagree, this wins)

- **The tab** (`Profile.kt`), top to bottom: the round gear (Settings) alone at the top right (no "Profile" title: the identity is the heading); the **identity** (a 76 dp circle with the initial on a gradient of the accent from 55 % to 18 % with a lit top edge, the name large, "Training since Apr 2025 · kg · Edit", Edit opening `ProfileEdit`); **stat chips** in a row that scrolls sideways (workouts, time trained in hours, total lifted, best streak in weeks; each a small `Modifier.tile` with the number and a grey word, each opening what explains it: History, Trends, Trophies; with no workout one quiet "Start a workout" line); then **Body**, **Strength**, **Goals and milestones** (titles large, 28 dp apart, each with a grey link at the right: Measurements, Personal bests, Trophies) and a quiet **Switch profile** row, room for the dock.
- **Body**: the weight card (a `Tile`: number, "+0.4 since 19 Oct · goal 80", `Sparkline(wash = true, goal = ...)` which draws the body-weight goal as a faint dashed line and keeps it in the chart's range, the round "+" for the ruler; the card opens the weight page); a row per measurement (not weight) with its change since the previous entry and its latest value; "Height · BMI" ("184 cm · 24.2", opens BMI; height only: "Height"; none: "Add your height"); **Photos** (the caption opens all photos, "Compare" opens the compare page, the newest three with their days under them).
- **Strength**: the four most logged lifts of the last twelve weeks (`liftsByFrequency`, shared with Home) that have an estimated 1RM, each with its latest estimate, "1.37 × bw" (that over the latest weigh-in, two decimals; hidden without one) and a thin bar: how far it is to the lift's goal when it has one, else its share of the strongest of the four (by × bw, or by kg without a weigh-in). A row opens the exercise's Charts tab. Then two chips: "21 PRs this month" (`recordCount` over this calendar month; opens Personal bests) and the split of the last four weeks' working sets, led by the biggest group ("Push 38 %", "pull 34 · legs 28"; opens the body figure). The split is `trainingSplit` in `core/Split.kt`: a set counts once, shared equally among the groups of its exercise's **main** muscles (`splitGroupOf`: chest, shoulders, triceps, serratus push; lats, upper back, traps, biceps, forearms, lower back pull; glutes, quads, hamstrings, adductors, calves, tibialis, hip flexors legs; abs, obliques, neck and rotator cuff none), warm-ups left out, percents by largest remainder so they add to 100.
- **Goals and milestones**: one tile card with each goal's bar (`GoalLine`: name, percent, "Goal 100 × 5 · On track for about 12 Feb") and the next milestone (`nextMilestone` over `milestones`, as 1.0.4's card) with its bar; "Set a goal" as a quiet line when there is no exercise or body goal; under the card the latest earned trophies (up to five, star tiles) with the total at the right, opening Trophies.
- Everything is optional: an empty part is one quiet "Add ..." line with a chevron (weight, measurement, height, photo, goal, first workout), never an empty box. Gone: the Lifetime lines, the Measurements list with its "Add", "All measurements" and "All photos" rows (the Measurements link and the Photos caption open them), the "Goals and trophies" list.

## 1.1.0 remake: adding to a running workout, sounds, moments

- **Library while a workout runs**: a glass band under the title ("● Push is running · 20:15 · Back to workout ›", dot in the accent, the clock ticks, a tap is `resumeWorkout()`), and a round glass **+** (48 dp touch) at the right of every Library row and search result, spoken "Add <name> to <workout>". A tap adds the card (`AppModel.addToRunning`), the + becomes an accent "Added" chip with a tick (16 % accent fill) and the one-line toast "<Exercise> added to Push" with Undo appears (the app's `Notice`, at the top, 6 s; a second add replaces it; Undo removes only its own card). The Library stays open. Pickers never get the +; with no workout nothing changes.
- **Exercise page** while a workout runs, not opened from it (a page opened with `WORKOUT_EXERCISE_TAB` is the workout's own): a full-width accent "Add to Push" button in the page's bottom bar (the content ends above it), or a quiet "In Push" with a tick once the card is there. Hidden while editing.
- **Rest sounds**: Deep bell (default), Bell, Gong, Soft tone, Wood block (`RestTone`; kv `rest.tone` holds `deep`, `bright`, `gong`, `soft`, `wood`, absent means Deep bell). Settings, Workout, "Rest sound": a row per sound (a tap picks and plays it) with a play button. The rest end (sound or both), the stopwatch's bell and the rest notification's channel use the chosen one. **`rest.tone` is read again**: it is no longer among the removed kv keys of 1.1.0 stage 5, and the four channels that stage deleted are made again.
- **Moments** (all off with Animations): a tick (tint sweeps from the tick, one light haptic), a PR (badge with one light sweep, double tick, chime), starting (plus into the clock pill), a rest ending (the ring has closed, the panel's edge lights once in the accent for about 0.6 s: 150 ms in, 450 ms out, only when the rest ran out with the full panel showing; the sound plays whatever). No other motion was added.

## Round 4a: the dock, the soft top edge, segmented controls (2026-11-19)

From the user's notes on test APK 3 (`docs/remake-1-1.md`, "Round 4"). Built and unit-tested, NOT yet run on a device.

- **Dock**: four icons from `artifacts/round4/icons.html` (`CleanIcons.DockHome` set A, `DockProgress` set D, `DockLibrary` set B, `DockProfile` set B), 27 dp, outline, centred in their slots, no word at rest (each slot still speaks its tab's name). The current tab's icon is in the accent (cross-faded by the stretch's `cover`), the others `onSurfaceVariant`.
- **Highlight**: none at rest. While the bar moves between tabs (a tap's glide or a swipe) a capsule the full height of the bar's inside stretches from the old slot to the new, with the same `rememberStretch` span; its opacity follows how far an edge is from lying on a slot edge (0 to 1 over the first and last 12 % of a slot), so it fades in as the move starts and out as it settles. Animations off: a cut, so none.
- **Dock material**: `glass(shape, accent = look.orange)`: the ground itself mixed 8 % toward the accent (no white light from above), 90 % tint over the blur (97 % solid), a 1 dp accent outline at 35 %, the lit top edge kept. Real blur on Android 12+ (the bar and the plus now get `LocalHaze` explicitly; they sit outside the pager's provider), solid below 12 / Reduce transparency. A 96 dp band from clear to the ground at 70 % sits under the bar over the page.
- **Scroll down (idea 11)**: while the current tab's list scrolls down the dock lowers 8 dp and its height shrinks 12 % (bar and plus together, 220 ms); scrolling up, the top or the bottom restores it. Read from `model.tabLists`; the Library's list counts.
- **Top edge**: a tab page's content (Home, Progress, Profile) fades out over 40 dp under the status bar (`topFadeMask`, a DstIn mask on the page, so the ground and its light show through). It grows with the scroll (0 at rest, full after 24 dp), so a title is never covered at rest. Pages in the back stack: the fade is drawn below `BackBar` once the page has scrolled (`TopFade`, a nested-scroll connection on the page; `LocalTopFade`). The Library tab keeps its title and search fixed, so it has none; pages with a custom top bar (not `BackBar`) have none.
- **Less empty space at the top**: `Title` is one row (title left, actions right, top spacing as Home's); Profile's gear sits on the name's line.
- **Segmented control**: thumb corner = track corner minus the 3 dp inset (15 dp in the 18 dp track), colour `Look.higher` with a lit top edge (white 10 % in Dark), track `onSurface` 5 %, selected label semibold `onSurface`, others `onSurfaceVariant`. `Choice` (chips) is a different control and unchanged.
- **Weight tile / Profile weight card**: the round "+" (36 dp disc, 48 dp touch, icon 20 dp) is on the number's line at the right (`TileNumberAdd`).
- **Up next**: "Not this one" is gone; a 44 dp round button (`PickAnother`, the tile fill with its lit edge, `CleanIcons.Cycle`, spoken "Pick another") sits right of Start on the same row.
- **Press (idea 3)**: tiles and cards that open something dip to 0.98 and brighten their lit edge while pressed (`pressable(dip, pressed)`, `litEdge(bright)`).
- **Haptic ticks (idea 4)**: `rememberTick()` (`SegmentFrequentTick`) on a segmented control's change and on a calendar day tap; Settings, Appearance, "Haptic ticks", kv `haptic.ticks`, absent = on (`LocalHapticTicks`).

## Round 4b: Activity as one constant, holds and the letter rail (2026-11-19)

- **Activity is one element under Home and Progress** (`ActivityBody`, `ActivityItem` in `Stats.kt`): no card on Progress, the same padding and content on both. Its calendar `ScrollState` and the Time / Volume choice are in `AppModel` (`activityScroll`, `activityByVolume`), so scrolling or switching on one page shows on the other. One wave key, `wave:activity`, for both pages.
- **Same height**: each page reports where its Activity item would start with no gap (`model.activityNat`, root px, kept from when the page was last at its top); the page that starts it higher gets a spacer above Activity (`ActivityItem`) so both start at the same y, whichever is taller (at default text size Home's header and Up next card and Progress's title and twins differ by about 2 px; the tour asserts the same y within 1 px). `model.activityY` is where the block itself starts, at rest at the top; if the two ever differ the overlay below slides between them, so it still never jumps.
- **Fixed between the pages**: while the pager is strictly between Home and Progress, both lists at scroll 0, no workout page open and no tap blink running, `Tabs` draws Activity once above the pager (`overlay = true`: no touch of its own, it takes touches that land on it) at the y between the two pages' (linear in the pager's position), and the pages' copies are hidden (`graphicsLayer alpha 0`, keeping their layout) by the same derived state, so the hand-off at either end is the same pixels. Any other move (another tab, a scrolled page, a tap on the bar) shows the pages' own copies, which move with their page.
- **Swipe isolation**: the whole block (title, switch, calendar, legend) is a horizontal `scrollable` on the shared scroll state under a nested-scroll connection that takes what is left over, so a drag that starts on it, or runs past the calendar's end, never reaches the pager. Vertical drags still scroll the page.
- **Gentle fling**: `GentleFling` (`exponentialDecay(frictionMultiplier = 2.8)`) on both the calendar and the block.
- **Months pill**: while the calendar is dragged, flung or drifting back, a small glass pill at the right above it names the months in view ("Mar – Jul 2025", "Nov 2024 – Mar 2025", "Mar 2025"); it fades in 180 ms and out 600 ms after the movement stops.
- **Holding a day (idea 5)**: `holdAndSlide` (Initial pass, nothing consumed before the hold, long-press time, no move beyond the touch slop) shows `DayPeek`, a small glass card above the finger: the day, and per workout name, length, volume, PR count and its exercises on one line (two workouts at most, then "+n more"); sliding on to another day switches it, releasing closes it, a day with no workout shows nothing. A tap is as before (Activity: opens the Calendar page at the day; Calendar page: the day's card). Ticks on the hold and on each new day.
- **Calendar page**: the day's card is drawn in the page (no popup window), by its square, so a tap on another day opens that day's card in the same tap, and a tap that nothing took (not a day, not the card) or a drag closes it. Holding used to add a past workout to the day; that is now the card's last row, "Log a workout".
- **Today fills in (idea 7)**: after a workout finished today (`AppModel.freshDay`, memory only), when Home is shown with no page above it today's square grows from 60 % and its colour eases in from the empty square's, 400 ms after a 250 ms wait, once.
- **Letter rail (idea 15)**: the letter under the finger is shown in a 44 dp accent bubble left of the rail, following the finger's height, and each change of letter ticks (`rememberTick`, Haptic ticks).

## Round 4c: the workout's open and close, the rest ring, bars, glow, count-up (2026-11-19)

- **The running workout is a shared text transition** (the user: "the same common text transition effect for moving between the current workout and back, currently it is just a blink"). The dock's clock pill (`PlusButton` while a workout runs; card key `DOCK_WORKOUT` = `dock:workout`, a card of the tabs page on every tab) and Home's In progress card (`home:today`, its Resume button runs through `cardTap`) are cards that open the workout by `resumeWorkout()`: the same container transform as any card (`CardMorph.kt`: `opensInto(round = true)` for the pill, `cardTarget` on the workout page), 500 ms on the emphasized curve both ways. The workout's name and clock are `MorphText` on both sides (roles `name`, `clock`; the page's are `WorkoutTitle`), so they travel from the pill's or card's place onto the page's title and clock, and back on Back or the down chevron, the words crossing over. While the pill is the source it stays where it is (the bar slides away, the pill does not) and its container is not cut at the bar (`MorphClip.barTop`). While a rest runs the pill shows only the clock (the rest's), so only the clock travels. Only a resume grows: starting a workout (the Workout sheet, a template, Start again) and every other way in is the blink as before (`AppModel.open(grows)`); a card that is gone when the workout closes (finished, discarded, another tab for a Home card) is the blink; animations off is a cut.
- **The pill's rest ring (idea 8)**: a 2 dp accent ring round the pulsing dot (18 dp box) counts the rest down, full at its start and empty at its end (linear, `Motion.clock()`); no ring when no rest runs.
- **Home's 1RM tile (idea 10)** already travelled: its sparkline (`chart:1rm`) becomes the exercise's Charts tab line (the All view's 1RM line, or the Est. 1RM chart); `MotionFramesTest.oneRmTileChartTravelsToTheExercisePage` checks it both ways.
- **Statistics bars (idea 14)** reshape between Week / Month / Year / All in `Motion.BARS` (300 ms, emphasized): each bar goes from its old height and slot to the new, added bars grow from 0, removed shrink to 0 (`VolumeBars`).
- **The next set's glow (idea 16)**: when a rest runs out by itself (`AppModel.restRanOut`, not when skipped) the next set's row (the `Glide` highlight) gets a soft accent fill and a thin accent edge that fade in over 150 ms and out over 600 ms (`Motion.GLOW_IN`, `GLOW_OUT`), once. The rest panel's edge light stays as it was.
- **The summary's numbers count up (idea 20)**: Time, Volume, Working sets and PRs count from 0 to their figure over 250 ms each, 120 ms apart (`Motion.countUp`), the first time that summary is shown in a session (`AppModel.seen`), then one light tick (`rememberTick`, so the Haptic ticks switch rules) after the last. The "(+3 min)" changes are not counted; animations off shows the figures at once.

## Round 4d: the weigh-in sheet, a profile picture, theme cross-fade, the week pill (2026-11-19)

- **The sheet grows out of its "+"** (idea 9). `Sheet(origin = Rect?)` (`Ui.kt`): with the window bounds of the tapped "+" (Home's Weight tile and Profile's weight card report them through `RoundAdd(onBounds)` / `TileNumberAdd`) and `LocalMotion` on, the sheet's clip is that round button's rectangle opening to the whole sheet (`GrowShape`, 300 ms, `Motion.Emphasized`), the scrim and the content fading in with it; closing (drag, scrim, Back, Close) runs it backwards into the "+" before the window goes (200 ms). The window has no animation of its own then (`CutWindow`). With no origin, or animations off, it is the plain blinking sheet as before. Save closes at once.
- **The ruler**: a light tick (`rememberTick`, so kv `haptic.ticks` and Animations rule it) for every step passed, at most one every 25 ms; a release below 700 px/s stays where it is and settles on the nearest step, a faster swipe coasts with exponential decay at friction 1 (about 3 kg at a medium flick, 7 at a hard one) and then settles on a step.
- **Typing** (`TypePad.kt`): tapping the big number swaps the sheet's body for the typed number and the app's own pad (the workout pad's keys, digits, point, delete and a Done key, on the sheet's glass; a hardware keyboard types, Backspace deletes, Enter is Done). The first key replaces the number. Done sets the ruler, clamped to its range and rounded to its 0.05 step (`padValue` in `core/WeighIn.kt`). The same pad types the hour or minute of the time.
- **When** (`WeighInDate.kt`, `WhenCard`): a card in the sheet with the last seven days as chips (Today, Yesterday, "Tue 17"; they scroll), an Earlier chip that stays in view and opens a month (weeks from Monday, arrows, days after today dimmed; the chip then reads the day picked), and the time as an hour and a minute that scroll (32 dp a step, a flick carries on, a tick each; tap the middle number to type it). Untouched, the entry is dated when Save is pressed; once anything is chosen it is that day and time with the seconds the sheet opened at, as the old dialogs gave.
- **Profile picture** (`Avatar.kt`): the 76 dp circle opens a small sheet, "Choose photo" (the system photo picker, no permission) and "Remove photo" when there is one. The picture is turned upright, cropped to the centred square, scaled to 512 px and kept as `files/avatars/<profileId>.jpg`; the circle shows it in place of the initial. It is in the backup zip and not in a CSV (`docs/data-format.md`).
- **Cross-fade** (idea 13): `CleanTheme(crossFade)` shows `lerpLook` of the look that was on screen and the new one over 250 ms whenever the look changes (a theme, Dark or Light, Pure black); off with Animations off and while the app is still loading.
- **Week pill** (idea 18): on the first open of a week the glass pill above the dock says "Last week: 3 of 3 ✓" (no tick when the target was missed), for four seconds or until tapped, over the tabs only. It shows once per profile per week (kv `weekpill:<profileId>` holds the Monday); nothing when last week is before the first workout, or had no workout and no target was ever set (`core/WeighIn.kt`: `weekPillDue`, `lastWeekLine`).

## Snapshots and Moments (2026-11-19, round 5d; idea from another app's profile photos, nothing copied)

- **Snapshots tile** on Profile, its own section after Body (card key `profile:moments`): a Lit `Tile` with the title "Snapshots", the count and a chevron ("12 ›") at the right, and the newest four pictures as framed cards in a fan (76 x 100 dp, turned -6, -2, 3 and 7 degrees, lifted -4, 5, -3 and 4 dp, a 2 dp frame in the surface colour, the newest on top at the left). Fewer than four leaves faint outlined frames. With none, one quiet pill with a plus, "Add photo", opens the system photo picker (several at once, images only, no permission) and the tile itself opens nothing. Otherwise the tile has the standard press and opens the Moments page, growing out of the card.
- **Moments page** (`Screen.Moments`, title "Moments"): a stats row (Photos, This month, Days with photos), then a three-column grid of square thumbnails under "Today", "This week" (the six days before today), then month names ("November", with the year for an earlier year). A plus in the top bar opens the picker. A tap opens the picture over a dimmed ground, growing from its tile (scale and move on `Motion.short`, a cut with animations off); a tap or Back closes it the same way. A long hold starts selection (light haptic, rings and check marks, the top bar shows the count and a Delete); further taps toggle; Back leaves selection. Delete asks first (`Confirm`) and has the app's Undo bar.
- Thumbnails are decoded off the main thread at the tile's size and kept in a small cache. Logic (names, headers, stats) is in `core/Moments.kt`.
- Not a progress record: no pose, no compare, no blur setting. Storage and backup: docs/data-format.md, "Moments".

## Round 6a: delight inside the workout (2026-11-19, from the round 5 approvals)
Everything here is small, checks `LocalMotion` and (for buzzes) the haptic switches. Values live in `Motion.kt`.
- **D2** A set that becomes a PR gets one accent shimmer along its top and bottom edges (`Modifier.prEdge`, `Motion.PR_EDGE` 600 ms), once per landing, beside the badge's own landing; the double tick and chime were already there.
- **D6** A rest that runs out (not one skipped) lights the screen's four edges in a soft accent gradient: in 400 ms, out 800 ms (`restGlowIn/Out`), panel up or not.
- **D7** A set's swipe is a rubber band (`Motion.rubber`: 1:1 at first, heavier toward 2.5 x the 110 dp threshold). Removal already had the Undo bar ("Set removed"); it is the undo pill.
- **D10** After a tick the next exercise eases to the top on the glide curve (`animateScrollBy`, not the stock scroll); a finger on the list interrupts it. The finished card's fold was already there.
- **D13** Number-pad keys (workout pad and `TypePad`) dip to 0.92 with the light tick.
- **D16** Settings, Workout, Rest sound: "Finish chime" (`finish.chime`, off by default) plays the PR chime at half volume when a workout is saved.
- **D19** The cards parting around a dragged exercise move on `Motion.part()`, a light spring (0.75 damping).
- **E2** In the last 3 s of a rest a soft tick each second (`Feedback.softTick`), unless "Buzz" is chosen (its pulses cover those seconds) or Haptic ticks is off.
- **E3** The weight typed in an ordinary set tints in the accent while it is above the exercise's best weight (`records.weight`, normal and bodyweight loads, not yet ticked). Left and right halves are not tinted.
- **E14** (the full rest panel it was written for is gone, see Round 13) The rest bar follows a downward swipe with resistance; past 0.4 of the limit it hides, else springs back.
- **E19** When a workout ends the plus springs once, 1 to 1.08 and back (`plusUp`, `plusBack`), as the pill has folded.

While the gesture runs the top page scales to 0.92 and rounds its corners; commit runs the usual close, cancel springs back. A uniform scale of the top page, not a shrink toward its card (too invasive with the card morph). The other BackHandlers are unchanged.

## Round 6c: charts, numbers and lists (2026-11-19)

All of it is quiet: nothing moves at rest, everything that moves checks `LocalMotion`, and ticks respect the Haptic ticks switch.

- **D8** The weigh-in ruler marks the last weigh-in with a small faint accent dot under the ticks (shown when it is in view).
- **E1** On the weigh-in ruler a whole kilo (or pound) gives a firmer detent (`rememberDetent`, `SegmentTick`) and is never held back by the 25 ms pause; the steps between keep the light tick.

A tap still opens the card with "Open". On an exercise's chart the held card also names the workout (`names`).
- **D18** The weight card's line on Home and Profile draws its last segment in over 500 ms when a weigh-in was added since the card last showed (`rememberNewPoint`, remembered per card in the model; not after an app restart).

- **E4** The week ring (`ProgressRing(closeSpring)`) closes with one soft spring overshoot, when the week becomes complete or the first time Home shows it complete (kv `ring.closed:<profileId>`).
- **E13** The Activity months pill leaves the year out when the view is inside this year and does not take in January, and names it otherwise (`monthsInView`, core).
- **E8** Library search results slide to their new places (`animateItem`).
- **E10** Starring a favourite on the exercise page bursts four dots 8 dp out in 350 ms (`Modifier.starBurst`).
- **E11** History ends with "Your first workout, <date>" (not while searching).
- **E12** The summary says "Your 50th workout" for the 10th, 25th, 50th, 100th, 150th, 200th, then every 100th workout of the profile (`roundWorkoutLine`, core).
- **E15** `AppModel.today` changes at midnight and when the app comes back to the front; Home's header and tiles, the Today card, Templates' "Today/Yesterday", Activity, Progress and the weigh-in day chips read it, and the consistency, targets and program flows are worked out again when it changes.
- **S3** Activity keeps "since <month>" on its title; the "drag back to" hint is gone from the legend row.

## Round 7: features (2026-11-19, from the round 5 approvals N10, N11, N14, N15, N16, N17)

Quiet by default: nothing here asks anything of the user unprompted, and nothing shows unless it was switched on or has something to say.

- **N10 Workout tags.** A workout has no tag unless the user adds one, and nothing ever prompts. A quiet line under the note, "Add tag" (grey italic, like "Add note"), on a finished workout's page, on the running workout (it has no menu, so it sits under the note) and on the Correct page. A tap opens a sheet: a field for a new tag, chips of the tags already used (most used first), and, once a tag is set, the switch "Leave out of stats" (default off) and "Remove tag". With a tag in use, History has a small row of chips above the list (hidden when no workout has a tag) that narrows it to one tag. Left-out workouts drop out of every statistic and record but stay in History; their line reads "<tag>  ·  left out of stats". Storage: docs/data-format.md, "Workout tags". `Tags.kt`, core `Tags.kt`.
- **N11 Steps.** Settings, Data, Health Connect has a second switch, "Show today's steps on Home" (default off); turning it on asks for READ_STEPS and stays off if refused. Home shows "8,214 steps" in the header's right corner, small and grey, and nothing when off, not allowed, unavailable or zero. Read again whenever the app comes to the front (`AppModel.refreshSteps`, `Health.stepsToday`). The offline rule now allows this one read (see README.md).
- **N14 Year in review.** `Screen.YearReview(year)`: the year large, then four figures that count up (workouts, time trained, volume, sets), "Strongest gains" (the three lifts whose estimated 1RM rose most over the year, from the best before the year, or the first session in it, to the best in it), then the best month, the longest streak (weeks in a row with a workout), the PRs and the most trained exercise; each opens what it names. Other years with workouts are chips at the top. From Progress's rows of statistics ("Year in review", newest year) for any year; on Profile, from 1 December to 31 January, a quiet line "Your 2026 ›" under the stat chips. Core: `yearReview`.
- **N15 Automatic backup.** Settings, Data, Backup: Folder (picker), Off / Daily / Weekly, "Last backup: <date>" and the last error in the danger colour only when there is one. Keeps the newest five. `AutoBackup.kt`; format and keys in docs/data-format.md.
- **N16 Set done.** The workout notification (lock screen) has a "Set done" button while a set is left. It ticks the next unticked set of the card whose turn it is as its check would (an empty box takes the grey hint: the template's planned value, else the same set of the last session, else the set above; the progression scheme's aim is not looked at there) and starts the rest, through `AppModel.blockChanged`, which moved there from the workout screen so both paths are one. The notice is posted again with the set that follows. If the app process is gone, the tap opens the app with the action `setdone`, which ticks once the workout is read back and shows the workout.
- **N17 Replace exercise.** The picker opened by Replace exercise starts with a "Suggested" section (nothing is typed or filtered): exercises that train a main muscle the exercise trains, ranked by how much of the main muscles they share, then done before, then equipment used on anything the user has logged (`swapSuggestions`, core, from the stored muscles and the history).

## Round 8: two fixes from the phone (2026-11-19; where this and older sections disagree, this wins)
- **E17 corrected.** Pulling down at the top of Home moves the whole list down together (`graphicsLayer { translationY = pull.shown }` on the `LazyColumn`), with the same rubber band, one tick at 72 dp and the spring back. The header no longer scales or moves on its own, so it can never overlap the Today card.
- **Measurement pages** (Weight, Chest, Waist and the rest of `MeasurementDetailScreen`). "Log weight" and "Set a goal" as two stacked pills are gone. The "+" is an action of the top bar: on Weight it opens the weigh-in sheet growing out of it (`origin`); on the other files it opens a "New entry" sheet (value, date, Add) in place of the inline field. Under the bar: the newest value large (`headlineLarge`, with its unit) and how far it moved since the first entry of the last 30 days ("+0.4 since 22 Oct", as the Home tile). The value is the page's `"number"` morph, so the Weight card's number lands on it; `"title"` and `"chart:weight"` are unchanged, and the latest list row is a plain text now. Weight only has a goal: one quiet grey line, "Set a goal ›" or "Goal 78 kg ›" (a tap changes it), a small "Remove" at its right, then a 4 dp bar and the percent and projected day as a grey line. The period control and the chart follow directly.
- The title row was checked against the bar: `BackBar` is a `TopAppBar`, which already keeps clear of the status bar; the clipped title on the phone was not reproduced in the tour (the tour has no status bar), so it is unverified there.
- Settings search: "Dock" (`ap-dock`, the round 6b section) added to `SettingsIndex`.

## Round 11: consistency rules (2026-11-19; from a pass with the apple-design skill; where this and older sections disagree, this wins)

One rule for each of the things that had drifted, in the app's own pieces (`Ui.kt`):

- **Headings.** A tab's title is `Title` (large, bold); an opened page's is `BackBar`. A **section of a tab page** (Activity, Statistics, Snapshots, Body, Strength, Goals and milestones) is a `SectionHeading`: `titleLarge`, semibold, at the left, with a grey `SectionLink` or a quiet note at the right; `PageSection(big = true)` is the same heading over rows. **Small grey capitals** (`Caption`, `PageSection`'s default) are only a label inside a tile or card (WEIGHT, TODAY, SETS) or over a group of rows or a chart on a page that was opened (Settings, Trophies, Trends, Year in review, an exercise's Info). Progress and Profile follow the same rule: Profile's Snapshots, Body, Strength and Goals and Progress's Statistics are `SectionHeading`s, and Activity (one tap target with the calendar, so its own row) uses the same style. A tile that a heading already names has no caption of its own (Snapshots).
- **Actions in a bar.** Adding is a "+" icon at the right of the bar (Weight and the other measurements, Moments, Library); looking is an icon (search, calendar); editing is the plain text "Edit" / "Done" (templates, exercises, no longer a tonal pill on exercises). One primary filled button per page, at the bottom or in its card. A quiet grey text link is how a section or a chart offers the way on ("Measurements", "Full screen"); outlined pills are not used for actions.
- **Quiet lines under a workout's name.** "Add note" and "Add tag" are one row (the tag at the note's right); while the note is typed the field takes the width. "Variation" is not a button on an exercise's card any more: with one variation done the card shows nothing, with several the chips (and a "+") stay, and "Switch variation" in the exercise's ⋮ menu is always there.
- **Goal on the Weight page.** One line (the goal, then how far along at the right), a 3 dp bar and, when there is one, the projected day in grey; no repeated "Goal 78 kg" under the chart (the chart's goal line is labelled on its axis). Removing the goal is the button "Remove goal" in the goal's own sheet. A chart's last line is the trend at the left and "Full screen" at the right, both grey.
- **Spacing.** Sections of a tab page start 24 to 28 dp below the one above (`Space.xxl` for `PageSection(big)`, Profile's 28 dp); groups on an opened page 12 dp above and below as before; a heading is 8 to 12 dp above its content. Figures above a group get 8 dp of air before the label.

## Platform: what differs on the iPhone (2026-11-20, iOS stage 3)

The screens are the same Compose code on both platforms; only what `Platform` hides differs. Written without a Mac to run it: compiled, not seen on a phone.

- **Dialogs and pickers.** Date and time are one wheel sheet (`UIDatePicker`, wheels, a medium sheet with Cancel and Done) instead of Android's two dialogs; files, folders and saving are the system document picker; pictures the system photo picker (no permission) and the camera. Share is the share sheet; the window animation of a dialog is Compose's own, so `BlinkWindow` and `CutWindow` do nothing.
- **Notifications.** iOS cannot count a notification down, keep one ongoing, or buzz from the background. A rest is a notification scheduled for its end (with the chosen sound, silent when sound is off) plus a quiet one in the Notification Center with "+15 s" and "Skip"; the running workout is a quiet notification with "Set done" and no running clock; the workout-day reminder is a repeating notification a week for each chosen day, and it is not skipped on a day already trained (Android's alarm checks at the time). Buzzes are the Taptic Engine: a tick, and a light, medium or heavy tap by how long Android's would run. Live Activities would do better and need a widget extension, left out.
- **What is not there.** Health (HealthKit needs a paid developer account): Settings hides the whole Health section (`Platform.hasHealth`). Widgets (WidgetKit shares data through an App Group, also paid): none; the quick actions on the home-screen icon stand in (Start, Resume while a workout runs, Log bodyweight; they do not name the next template). The launcher icon per theme is an alternate icon, which iOS announces with its own alert.
- **Status bar and orientation.** The status bar's icons follow the window's light or dark style, so the window is overridden to the app's theme (and left to the phone for the "phone" theme). The app is portrait; a full-screen chart turns it to landscape by asking the scene.
- **Back and restart.** The page stack is kept in a small file when the app goes to the background and read at launch, as Android's saved state is. A restore or "Delete everything" cannot restart a process, so the root builds a new model.
- **Data.** Everything is under the app's Documents folder (`clean.db`, `photos/`, `moments/`, `avatars/`, `animations/`); the backup zip has the same layout as Android's, so a backup moves data either way. The scheduled backup writes into the folder the user picked, when iOS runs a background refresh and when the app goes to the background with one due.

## Round 13: one day card, the rest bar, undo pills (2026-11-20; where this and older sections disagree, this wins)

From the user's three notes on the phone (the calendar's tap and hold said different things, no full-screen rest timer, undo pills should swipe away and be less in the way). Not run on a device.

- **One day card.** `DayWorkoutLines` (`Stats.kt`) is the one description of a workout on a day: its name, then "HH:mm · length · volume · N PRs", then its exercises on one line. `DayPeek` (a hold, no actions) and the Calendar page's tap card (each workout a row that opens it, then "Log a workout") both build from it, at the one width `DAY_CARD_WIDTH`, so they cannot drift apart. The Calendar page has no hold peek any more (a slow tap is a tap); the Activity card on Home and Progress keeps its hold-and-slide peek and a tap there opens the Calendar page. Progress's History card has the same hold-and-slide on its rows: the workout above the finger as the same card (above, or below the finger when there is no room above), sliding on switches row; a tap still opens the History page, a scroll still scrolls. History's own list is not given it: a hold on its cards already opens the workout menu (`holdMenu`), so the two would fight.
- **The rest bar.** While a rest runs, `RestDisplay` (`RestPanel.kt`) floats a glass bar at the bottom of the workout page, above the system bar and above the number pad when it is up: the time (tap it for the rest's options, which hold the preset lengths and Skip rest), the next set in words on one line, −15 s, +15 s and Skip as compact buttons, and a 3 dp line draining along the bottom edge. The list gets bottom room equal to the bar's height while it shows, so the last row is never under it. It never covers the workout: there is no full-screen panel, no ring and no "Hide" any more. Swiping it down follows the finger with `Motion.rubber`, then hides it for this rest (or springs back); a small "Rest m:ss" pill takes its place at the bottom and brings it back. The glow on the edges when a rest runs out, the soft ticks of the last seconds, the top bar's own time and line and the dock pill's ring are unchanged. `restShowsFull`, `DEFAULT_REST_HIDE` and `REST_RETURN_SECONDS` are gone from core; Settings never had an option for the display.
- **The Undo pill.** `UndoBar` is a glass pill that hugs its words (cut with an ellipsis), with Undo and a thin line along its bottom edge that drains over the 8 s. It sits just under a top bar's row (56 dp below the status bar) so its back, Finish and rest buttons stay free, or 72 dp down on a tab page, under its heading, and only the pill takes touches. Swiping it sideways either way or up follows the finger with resistance; past about 50 dp it leaves and `AppModel.dismissUndo()` lets the chance go (what was kept for an Undo is dropped as when the time is up) without taking anything back; short of that it springs back. With animations off it just goes. Undo is a button; the pill is a polite live region.

## Round 14: Settings readability (2026-11-20; where this and older sections disagree, this wins)

From the user: "make the settings more readable, better gaps to understand heading vs text". Not run on a device. What was wrong (looked at in `ScreensTourTest.settingsPages`, `s-*` shots): the heading was 11 sp grey capitals, smaller and no darker than the row titles under it, with as much air above as below, so it did not belong to anything; row titles were grey and values white (the wrong way round); every switch carried a three-line grey explanation that ran under the switch; every button was a full-width pill; nothing was grouped, so rows, notes and buttons were one stream of equal weight.

- **Pieces (`Ui.kt`).** `SettingsGroup(title, footer)`: a `SectionTitle` (title-medium bold, the type scale's section title, read as a heading, inset to the rows' text), 8 dp under it, then every row in one card (`cardFill`, `cardShape`, `cardDepth`) with a 1 dp hairline inset 16 dp from the left between rows, then the `GroupFooter`. 24 dp (`Space.xxl`) above every group, so a heading is three times nearer to its card than to the group above. Rows: `SettingsRow` (title in `bodyLarge`, an optional short `bodySmall` summary, a muted value, trailing switch or chevron, at least 56 dp, 12 dp between words and switch), `SettingsSwitch` (the whole row is the switch), `ActionRow` (a button in a group: accent semibold words, red for `danger`; a tinted button has nothing to stand out from on a card), `ChoiceRow` (title and value, the chips or segmented control under), `NoteRow` (plain words). `RestChoice(grouped = true)` is the chip row in a group.
- **Words.** A summary is one or two short lines under a row. An explanation longer than that, or one that belongs to a whole group, is the group's footer (`bodySmall`, muted, 8 dp under the card). A group with one switch keeps its explanation in the footer. Nothing was dropped; wording was shortened only where it was wordy.
- **Settings only.** Every Settings page (the list of pages, search results, the four pages, `AutoBackupSection`, `RenamedExercises`) uses the pieces. This replaces round 11's small-capitals headings for Settings; Trophies, Trends and the rest keep `PageSection`.


## Round 15: delights (2026-11-20, from the round 13 approvals; not run on a device)

Each happens once per occasion, is cut or absent with animations off, moves nothing in the layout (drawn in `graphicsLayer` / draw phases) and is gone at rest, so a resting screen is unchanged. Timings are named in `Motion.kt`.

- **Up next slides on** (`rememberUpNextSlide`, `Program.kt`; 400 ms, `Motion.UP_NEXT`): when a workout was logged since the name was last on screen and the template Up next names changed, the old name stays until Home is shown again, then slides up out and the new one up from below, 250 ms after Home is back. Not on a cold start, not for a template picked by hand (no workout logged since), not while a page covers Home. What was last seen is kept for the session (`LocalSeen`, `upnext:name`, `upnext:count`).
- **The week ring draws its new segment** (`ThisWeekTile`, `Home.kt`; the ring's own `Motion.slow()` 600 ms, starting `Motion.RING_AFTER` 250 ms after Home is shown, with the calendar square's fill): the ring keeps the count last seen until Home is on screen, then sweeps from the old fraction to the new; E4's closing overshoot still applies when it closes.
- **Anniversary line** (`anniversaryLine`, core `Delight.kt`): on the day a year or more on from the first logged workout (same month and day; 29 February is 28 February in years without one) Home's weekday line reads "Friday · 1 year of training today". Only that day.
- **All-time-high bar glint** (`allTimeHigh`, core `StatsPeriod.kt`; `rememberHighGlint`, `Progress.kt`; 600 ms, `Motion.CAP_GLINT`): a Statistics bar that is the biggest day, month or year of the whole log (by what the bars cover) gets a thin accent cap with a sheen across it (`drawCapGlint`) the first time the bars are in view after its volume passed the high last seen (`high:<size>` in `LocalSeen`). Nothing at rest.
- **Double tap a chart** (`SplitLineChart`, `Stats.kt`; the existing 400 ms stretch): switches between all the chart's points and the last three months, on a chart that runs back further; "Last 3 months" in grey-blue under the chart's middle while on. Tap, hold-and-scrub and scrolling are unchanged (the double-tap wait only exists on such charts); kept per chart while its page stays (`rememberSaveable`); a screen reader gets a custom action. Full screen always shows everything.
- **Muscles trained today breathe** (`rememberBreath`, `Body.kt`; 800 ms, `Motion.BREATH`): on Progress's Body card they brighten 40 % toward white and settle back once, the first time the card is in view each day (kv `breath.day:<profileId>`); nothing when none trained today.

## Round 16: one floating material (2026-11-20; where this and older sections disagree, this wins)

From the user on his phone: floating things were drawn in different materials (the dock blurred and dark, the day card, toasts and pills a flat mid-grey that let the page ghost through, cards opaque near-black tiles). Apple's rule is two layers, not three: **content is opaque; everything that floats is one material.** Not run on a device (the blur itself is only visible on the phone).

- **The material** is `Modifier.glass(shape, haze, lift, accent)` in `Ui.kt` and nothing else; there are no ad-hoc `Surface(color = surfaceContainerHigh...)` floating surfaces left. Tone: the page ground with 8 % of the accent mixed in (the dock's own tone), `glassTone`. **Blurred form** (a blur source behind it, Android 12+, Reduce transparency off): 22 dp blur under that tone at 90 % (a regular, text-heavy variant; never more transparent). **Opaque form** (no source, a window of its own, Reduce transparency, the phone's high-contrast setting): the tone the blur reads on average over the ground (the same tone at 90 % over the page), 100 % opaque, so nothing ghosts through. Both forms have the same lit top edge, darker bottom edge, inner hairline and wide soft shadow (`lift`). The old white light from above and the 97 % mid-grey are gone. Light: the same logic (the page-tinted white); the opaque form is the same light surface. While a workout runs the tone takes the accent a little, as before. `accent` only draws the dock's thin accent outline: that stays the dock's identity.
- **Blurred where they can sample the page** (a source that is a sibling drawn before them, only recorded while the card is up, half input scale): the Activity hold card (`DayPeek`; the calendar chart is its source), Progress's History-row hold card (the page list), the Calendar page's day card (the months list), the Undo pill and the toast (the app's root, `sheetHaze`), the rest bar and rest pill (the workout list; they follow the app's Reduce transparency, not the workout page's quiet no-blur), the dock, the Workout sheet and the History totals bar as before. Haze cannot blur from inside its own source, hence the sibling sources. The months pill over Activity has nothing it can sample (it floats over the heading, outside the chart), so it is the opaque form.
- **Opaque form always** (windows of their own, which cannot see the page): menus (`CleanMenu`), dialogs (`CleanDialog`), sheets (`Sheet`), the exercise picker, the set-row dialogs of the workout (`RowDialogHost`), What's new, the chart point cards and the body figure's muscle card (popups).
- **Not glass: a button inside the content layer.** `GlassButton` (the Library row's + and Profile's gear) is a quiet filled disc in the tile tone with its lit edge, like the cards around it.
- **Two defects.** The Undo pill's draining line is drawn inside the pill's shape, on its straight bottom edge (inset from the round ends), so it can no longer reach past it. The soft top edge fades only the band that content has actually scrolled into: it is as tall as the page has scrolled (up to 40 dp) instead of at full strength after 24 dp, so Home's date title looks normal until it slides under the status bar (`BackBar`'s fade the same).
- **Hold peeks stay on screen.** `placeAbove` and `placeBy` keep the card inside the window with the page's side margin (`Space.l`) on both sides, above the finger, below it when there is no room above.
- **A double tap on a chart drives the page's range control** where there is one (`RangeSelector`: the exercise page, Trends, the weight page and other measurements, a muscle's sets per week, BMI): it sets the control to its 3 months and, tapped again, back to the option that was chosen before, so control and chart always agree and the control's thumb glides as on a tap; no "Last 3 months" label then. Only a chart with no range control (Month, Week, template and similar lines) keeps its own switch and its label.

## Round 17: eight more small delights (2026-11-20; not run on a device)

Same rules as round 15: once per occasion, cut with animations off (`LocalMotion`), drawn in graphics layers or draw phases so nothing moves in the layout, nothing at rest. Timings are named in `Motion.kt`; the pure parts are in `Delights.kt`.

- **Library hold peeks at the last session** (9; `Hold.peek`, `holdMenu(peek = ...)`, `lastSessionPeek`): the Library row's hold already opened the lift-and-menu (Open, Favourite, Edit) and those three have no other route, so the peek is not a separate card that a release dismisses.
