It is the head of that menu's glass card: the day of the exercise's last session, then its working sets as "60 kg × 8" (at most six, "+n more"), over a hairline and the choices. An exercise never done shows no head. Pickers have no hold, so none there.
- **"Did you mean"** (10) was already there (`nearestName`, `Exercises.kt`, round 14 era): a search that finds nothing shows the nearest name as a tappable accent line that fills the search. Only a test was added.
- **A new weight PR counts up** (11; `PrLine(rollFrom)`, `rolledWeight`, `previousBestWeight`, `Motion.PR_ROLL` 700 ms): on the finish summary's "PRs" list a weight PR (ordinary load type, with reps, a heavier weight than any earlier working set) counts in half-unit steps from the previous best to the new weight, starting once the summary's own numbers have counted. Once per PR: `reward.seen` id `prroll:<day>:<exercise>:<weight>x<reps>`, written when the summary decides what to play. The exercise page's record is not animated.
- **Pull the Library down to search** (12; `ExerciseList`, `PULL_TO_SEARCH_DP` 72 dp): Library only (a nested-scroll connection; pickers keep their sheets' own drag). The list follows the finger through `Motion.rubber` (heavier the further), "Pull to search" fades in above it and becomes "Release to search" past the threshold with one light tick; letting go there focuses the search box (which brings up the keyboard), letting go before it just settles back (`Motion.PULL_BACK`). With animations off the list does not move, the hint and the focus remain.
- **Hold the avatar** (13; `ProfileTab`, `trainedLine`): a small glass card "412 h trained since Apr 2026" (all workouts' lengths added; minutes under an hour) beside the circle, blurred over the page like the History peek (`placeBy`, inside the `Space.l` margins); release dismisses. A tap still opens the picture menu.
- **Snapshots cards lean** (15; `rememberScrollTilt` in `Moments.kt`, `tiltStep`): on Profile, each fan card gets up to 3 degrees of `rotationX` and a smaller `rotationY` (by its place in the fan) following the page's scroll speed frame by frame, and settles flat 90 ms after the scroll stops (`Motion.TILT_SETTLE`), also with a finger held still. Nothing with animations off.
- **▲ from a set that beat last time** (17; `SetRow.beatsLast`, `beatsLastReps`, `Motion.BEAT_RISE` 700 ms): as a set is ticked, if its reps beat the same set of last time's session at the same weight or more (ordinary and bodyweight loads, not warm-ups) a green ▲ rises from the reps box and fades. Once per tick (unticking and ticking again repeats it). Left and right pairs are not covered yet.
- **Finish breathes** (19; `Modifier.breathing`, `everySetDone`, `Motion.FINISH_BREATH`): once the workout has sets and all are ticked the Finish button slowly pulses (5 % scale, 18 % fade); it stops as soon as a set is unticked or added.

## Round 18: hold the body, a keyboard that comes, an accent in History (2026-11-20; not run on a device)

- **Hold a muscle** (`BodyFigure`, `Body.kt`; `holdAndSlide`, `peekMore`): on any interactive figure (the Body page, a muscle's page, template stats, Home/Progress's fatigue and strength figures; not the small Progress card) a press-and-hold on a muscle shows a card above the finger with its name (a limb by its side, "Left biceps"), the line the tap tip already says (sets, fatigue word, % retained, left/right) and "Last trained 17 Nov" (or "Never trained"; `peekMore`, given by the Body page and Home's figures). Sliding on to another muscle updates it with a light tick, off the body hides it, release dismisses. A hold is not a tap: the touch is the hold's alone once it fires, the tap tip and double tap to unzoom work as before. The card is a popup (a window of its own, so it may leave the figure's card): the opaque form of the glass, no blur. Top exercises were not added (not cheap on the figure's data).
- **Both popups stay inside the window** (`InsideWindow`): the hold card centred above the finger (below it when there is no room), the tap tip at its old corner (finger less 56 dp, 84 dp), both kept inside the window with the `Space.l` margin on every side.
- **Pull to search raises the keyboard** (`ExerciseList`, `Exercises.kt`): letting go past the threshold focuses the search box on the next frame and asks the software keyboard to show a frame after that, not inside the fling. A release that never reports a fling (a slow lift) is caught by the list's scroll ending with the pull still out; both go through one `release()`.
- **PR marks follow the accent** (`WorkoutCard`, `Workouts.kt`): the "3 PRs" chip on History's cards (and on the page it grows into) is the accent in its 16 % tint instead of the meaning green; Progress's History rows already used the accent for the star and count. Graphite's chip is therefore a pale grey, Ember's amber, Ocean's blue. Green still means a PR elsewhere (finish summary, exercise page); the user asked for History only.

## Round 19: more hold peeks (2026-11-20; not run on a device)

The hold peek of rounds 4 and 18 (`holdAndSlide`, a card above the finger, a light tick when the slide reaches a new item, release dismisses) on eight more places. One mechanism: every new card is `PeekPopup` (`Stats.kt`), the opaque glass in a window of its own, kept inside the window with the `Space.l` margins by `InsideWindow` (above the finger, below it when there is no room above); only the Activity and History cards, which float over a sibling of the page, are blurred. `HoldPeekBox` wraps one row or block that has a single card; a hold is the touch's alone, so the tap under it does not fire, and taps and scrolls are unchanged.

- **Multi-line chart** (`MultiLineChart`, the exercise page's "All", a muscle's sets per week, the full screen): hold and slide scrubs the dates; a line across the chart, a dot on each line, and one card with the date and each line's value at its nearest date, each with its line's colour dot. The exercise page's two `SplitLineChart` calls already pass the page's `RangeSelector`.
- **Volume bars** (`VolumeBars`, Progress): the bucket under the finger (not days to come): its label, the volume, workouts and sets ("No workouts" for an empty one).
- **This week tile** (`Tile(peek = ...)`, Home): the seven days of the week as dots, the days trained, the streak. A tap still opens Trophies.
- **Trophies grids** (`HoldOverCells`): hold and slide over the 26 weeks for "Week of 10 Nov", "2 of 3 workouts" (or "program days") and the state words the cell's description already had; over the week on program for the day, its template and its state.
- **Profile Strength** (`StrengthLift.peek`): the lift, "Best set 100 kg × 5 · 12 Nov" (the set behind the latest estimate), and the change against the session before ("+2.5 kg since 3 Nov"). A tap opens the exercise.
- **Personal bests** (both views): the History peek's content (`WorkoutPeek`: the day and `DayWorkoutLines`) for the workout the record or PR was set in.
- **Weigh-in and measurement entries**: the value, the change since the entry before it, and for body weight with a goal the distance to it ("Goal reached" past it). Tap-to-edit and the trash button as before.
- **Template cards' menu** (`templatePeek`): like the Library's head, the day it was last done and "5 exercises · 18 sets" over the choices; a template never done has no head (as the Library).

## Round 20: one PR colour, a colour family per theme, meaning colours clear of the accent, search that opens from an icon (2026-11-20; not run on a device)

- **One PR colour** (`prColor()`, `PrChip`, `Badge`, `prTint`, `Ui.kt`; **replaced in round 28: a PR colour of each theme's own, never the accent**): every PR mark was the accent (`primary`): Home's last-workout chip (`PrChip`, star and count), History's chip, the badges on the finish summary's and the workout page's sets (`PrBadges`; a *matched* best stays amber `tertiary`), the share card's badge, the Progress and Profile stars. Chips fill with the accent at 12 % in Dark and 8 % in Light (`prTint`), so the text stays within a hair of the accent's own contrast (a test asserts 4.2 : 1 over every theme; Crimson Dark's accent is only 4.4 : 1 on its card). Graphite's Light accent is near-black, so its chip is dark on pale grey and reads well.
- **Goals use the accent** (`goalColor()`): the goal line (`SplitLineChart`), the sets-per-week target band (`MuscleDetail`), "within the weekly target" on the Week page and its tick on the Body page's week view, and a hit day on the Trophies week. `done` is left to ticked sets, "beat last time" and the finish summary's good news.
- **Per-theme chart colours** (`SERIES`, `Look.series`, `Look.kt`): the first line of every chart is the accent, followed by four colours chosen in Oklch at one chroma (0.12 Dark, 0.11 Light; Graphite 0.075) from the accent's split-complement and triad, the lightness of each picked from three steps so no two lines are alike with deuteranopia or protanopia (pairwise at least 6 in 100 x Oklab, 9 with normal sight, 4 with tritanopia) and each keeps 3 : 1 against the page, card, tile and white. Used by `MultiLineChart` (lines, legend dots, the hold card's dots), the exercise page's All chart and the muscle's left and right chart (Left is the accent, Right the second colour). The colour-blind-safe switch changes the meaning colours only; the series already pass the colour-vision checks. Dark, then Light, extras after the accent:
  - Graphite: 7BA2CC D1B380 98E2D7 C88B94 / 5A7EA3 674E21 165F58 9F6971
  - Ember: 61A3E6 44D0C1 DC7D94 DEC3FF / 16558C 018D82 82344A 8A68AE
  - Crimson: A9D7FF D5B455 44D0C1 B08ADA / 3A80B7 94771A 056058 614083
  - Moss: A9D7FF C5953B B08ADA FEBFB9 / 02578B 855E05 8A68AE 853532
  - Ocean: FAC871 60EAE2 F896AD 75B168 / 9C721C 018D87 99485E 2D6121
  - Plum: DBB155 3ED0C8 DF7E86 96E7A7 / 816002 007671 84343F 448C56
  - Ice: FB9989 9E91E4 EFCE6F D67EA9 / 9B4B3F 53458A 94771A 7D355B
  - Rose: 61A3E6 85E9B6 C1983A DEC3FF / 437EB9 04623F 98741A 755498
- **Meaning colours never sit on the accent** (`Look.meaning()`, `Theme.kt`, `ColourMath.kt`): a meaning colour whose Oklch hue is less than `MEANING_HUE_GAP` (36 degrees) from a chromatic accent's is turned to `MEANING_HUE_TARGET` (38) degrees from it, on the side it already sat on (done and ready, the good colours, always to the cool side: Moss's good turns teal, Ember's recovering, warm-up and full turn toward yellow, Ocean's underweight and Plum's drop step aside, Crimson's failure and fatigued go vermillion); lightness and chroma are kept, then the lightness is moved until the colour has the contrast it had against the look's card (at most 4.5 : 1). Graphite, with no hue, shifts nothing; Crimson's `danger` is still the text colour. `Round20ColoursTest` asserts the gap and 3 : 1 (4 : 1 Light) over every theme, Dark, Light, Pure black and colour-blind-safe. Not covered here: `tertiary` (the amber of a matched best and the favourite star), done in round 22.
- **Search from an icon** (`Search.kt`: `SearchOpener`, `SearchBox`, `rememberPullToSearch`; used by `ExercisesTab`/`ExerciseList(search = ...)` and `HistoryScreen`): the Library and History headers carry a search icon; tapping it, or pulling the list down past 72 dp (one helper, the round 17 and 18 behaviour: rubber band, hint, tick, keyboard a frame after focus), grows a pill (tile tone with the lit edge, glyph, placeholder, close control; `Motion.search()` 220 ms, none with animations off) under the header and focuses it. The close control, Back, or leaving the field empty collapse it and forget the query. Filter chips, "Did you mean" and the create row are unchanged. The exercise pickers keep the plain outlined field (`search = null`). History's field is the same component; its old toggle (search or close icon) is now the search icon only.
- **Haptic tick at swipe thresholds**: the Undo pill (`dismissUndo` distance) and the rest bar (`limit * 0.4`) give `rememberTick()` once as the swipe passes the point where letting go acts.
- **Count-up** (`countUpOnce`, `Pages.kt`, 250 ms, the Summary's `countUp`): once per page open, off with animations off, on the Week and Month totals, the template page's Exercises, Working sets, PRs set, Total time and Total volume, Profile's Strength figures (estimate and x bodyweight) and Personal bests (each lift's figure, each month's PR count).

## Round 21: nothing changes when a transition ends (2026-11-21; not run on a device)

**The rule (the user: "a lot of after-transition jumps").** The last frame of a card-to-page move (and of a page-to-card Back) is the frame at rest, in every area: nothing may be drawn, shaped, sized, shifted, tinted or counted differently in the frame the clock ends than in the one after it. What is on both sides morphs (`morphs`, `MorphText`, the travelling chart line); what is on one side only fades in place, with the card's content or the page's, and never slides. `Round21Test` renders every pair frame by frame (`pair`) and compares the last frame of the move with the settled frame inside the pair's area (a few pixels of anti-aliasing at a line's turns or a tile's top edge are allowed, set per pair); strips are in `app/build/screens/default/round21-*.png` (`-open`, `-back`: the last two frames of the move, the next one, rest and the difference in red; `-move`: the whole way).

What was jumping and how it is fixed:
1. **The weight line (Home's tile, Profile's card, to the weight page and back).** The travelling line was resampled to 240 points, which smoothed a page of hundreds of daily weigh-ins and drew thick, jagged data only when the real line took over. The travelling line is now `curveThrough` over the denser side's own points (`ChartLine.rawX/rawY`), the other side read at the same places along its length from its finely resampled curve (`resampleLine` takes 16 samples per point, up to 8000), the mix `from * (1 - q) + to * q`; at the denser end it is that chart's own path, point for point. Same scale ends: the y of each point is interpolated, so are the x.
2. **Closing onto a card with a tint (every Sparkline with `wash`: both weight cards, the 1RM tiles).** The travelling line carried a tint of alpha 0 and no dot, so the card's wash and the latest-value dot appeared in the last frame. The Sparkline now tells the move its real wash (`ChartWash(height, 0, 0.26, 0, ...)`) and its dot, so they fade and reshape with the line from the page's none.
3. **Progress's History card and the History page: the date.** It no longer travels (the `row:date` role is gone on both sides while the page's card is `progress:history`): the card's date fades with the card's content and the page's fades in with the page, in place. The name and the PR count still travel; Home's Recent workouts rows keep their date morph.
4. **The This week ring.** The tile's ring and the Trophies page's ring (the current week's cell of the last 26 weeks, a ring or, when the week is met, a star) share the role `ring`: it travels and resizes between them both ways, one box on the container's clock, the two drawings crossing over inside it.
5. **Statistics rows on Progress (Personal bests, Trends, Trophies, Calendar, Year in review).** They are laid out on the page with no card, but the row's container started and ended in the card tone, so on Back the last frame was a grey bar that vanished when the clock ended; the container's fill is the page's own now (`NavRow`).
6. Checked and found at rest: Home's Last workout, Volume and 1RM tiles (the 1RM line is the same travelling line as 1), Progress's Body card, the Trends, Personal bests and Trophies rows, a Library row to its exercise (no pixel differs). Left alone: a difference of a pixel or so in the glyphs of a PR badge (History) and in the top edge row of a tile on Back, which are anti-aliasing and not visible; Activity (one constant, no card), and the template cards (in the Workout sheet, not a card morph).

## Round 22: Graphite in Light, contrast audit; the amber off the accent (2026-11-21; not run on a device)

**The audit.** `Round22Test` renders every main screen and the important subpages in Graphite Light (`app/build/screens/default/round22-graphite-light-*.png`, 42 pictures); `Round22ContrastTest` checks each foreground token on every surface it is drawn on (text 4.5 : 1, quiet text 4.5 : 1, accent as text 4.5 : 1, a control's outline 3 : 1, the four-step scale 3 : 1 at its lowest) for Graphite Light, and asserts Graphite Dark is as it was. Graphite's near-black Light accent makes every surface made by tinting with it (page, raised step, set boxes) a flat dark grey, which is where it fell short. Ratios before, then after:

| Issue | Before | After | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|
| Quiet text on the raised step and set boxes | 4.43 and 3.89 | 5.23 and 4.59 | Graphite Light `muted` 6A6A66 to 5F5F5B (`GRAPHITE_LIGHT_MUTED`) |
| Ghost value in an empty set box, and the aimed value after Previous | 1.85 | 3.83 | placeholder alpha 0.5 and 0.7 to 0.9 in Light (`lightAlpha(dark, light)`, Ui.kt; Dark keeps its alphas) |
| Disabled number-pad key ("Same") | 1.71 | 3.36 | alpha 0.25 to 0.5 in Light; disabled sheet and list rows 0.4 and 0.6 to 0.75 |
| Segmented thumb against its track (Time / Volume, Balance / Fatigue, 30d) | 1.02 | 1.32 | Light thumb is white on a track of 8 % ink (was `higher`, as dark as the track) |
| Outlined chip edge against the page | 1.40 | 4.08 | `CleanChip` border is `outline`, not `outlineVariant`; Graphite's outline and hairline are neutral greys (757575, C8C8C8; they were blue-grey) |
| Search pill against the page | 1.00 | 4.12 | Light draws a 1 dp `outline` edge on the pill |
| Lowest calendar / body / bar step against page and card | 1.94 and 2.10 | 3.09 and 3.45 | Graphite Light steps 8A8A8A, 626262, 3E3E3F, 1A1A1C (`GRAPHITE_LIGHT_SCALE`; steps keep 1.6 or more from each other) |
| Lowest step against the untrained figure's grey | 1.2 | 1.49 | same |
| Card against page | 1.08 | 1.12 | Graphite Light page takes 5.5 % of the accent, not 4 % (`GRAPHITE_LIGHT_PAGE_WASH`) |
| Dock edge against the page (the accent outline, 35 %) | 2.64 sides, 1.34 top | 3.39 sides, 2.87 top | outline 45 % in Light and drawn over the lit edge, which had painted the top out |

Checked and fine: text and bold numbers (13 to 19 : 1), accent as text or icon (12 to 17 : 1), dock icons (6A6A66 on the dock 4.27 before, now darker still on its tone), selected chip text (14 : 1), PR chip (grey tint, dark text), toggles (outline), the Active screen's rows, the undo pill and rest bar text. Left as they are, on purpose: the floating panels' own fill and edge (the toast, undo pill, rest bar, sheets: 1.08 fill, 1.2 lit edge; they are separated by their 14 dp shadow, which the screenshots' software canvas does not draw, and carry no control edge), row hairlines and chart grid (decoration; every value is also written), empty calendar cells (1.1 : 1: they only show the grid), the Active screen's warm-up "W" (A86A00 on the current row 3.63, bold large text). Graphite Dark was glanced at and not changed: its control outline is 2.97 on the raised step, the ghost value 2.28 and the lowest step against the untrained figure 1.33; reported, not asserted tighter.

**Tertiary off the accent.** `Look.tertiary()` (Theme.kt) is the amber (FFC53D Dark, 9A5B00 Light) put through the same `apartFrom` rule as the meaning colours: within `MEANING_HUE_GAP` (36 degrees) of a chromatic accent it is turned to 38 degrees from it on the side it sat on, lightness moved back to the contrast it had on the card. `Look.colorScheme()` sets `tertiary` (and its container) from it, so every reader of `colorScheme.tertiary` follows (the matched-best badge in `PrBadges`, the favourite star, the "no new best" line, the linked-set mark in Active, the latest-value colour in Stats). Only Ember moved: Dark FFC53D (21 degrees from the orange) to E6D142 (a yellow, 38 degrees), Light 9A5B00 (12 degrees) to 836905 (a dark gold, 38 degrees, 5.27 : 1 on the card). Crimson Light is 41 degrees away, every other theme 62 or more; nothing else changed. `Round20ColoursTest` asserts the gap, 3 : 1 (4 : 1 Light) and a warm hue for every theme, Dark, Light, Pure black and colour-blind-safe.


## Round 23: the nooks (2026-11-21; not run on a device)

The rarely visited pages looked plain beside the main tabs. Each now has one hero, small-caps section labels (`Section("...")`), the `Space` scale and the accent for meaning only. The shared pieces are in `Nooks.kt`; pictures are `app/build/screens/default/round23-graphite-{dark,light}-*.png` (`Round23Test`).

- **Exercise PRs tab** (`Exercises.kt`): a hero Est. 1RM (`countUpOnce`, a `Sparkline` of its history, a `PrChip` with the day it was set, hold peeks the workout); the records (heaviest weight or its variants, most reps, longest hold, est. 1RM, best set and session volume) as a 2-column grid of `RecordTile`s (a `Tile`; tap opens the workout, hold peeks it with `WorkoutPeek`; each side's own figure under it for left and right exercises); a "PR history" card with the last three PRs (`PrLine`) that opens PrTimeline; "Best reps at each weight" as a ladder (`LadderRow`: weight, bar, reps, day; ten rungs, "Show all n"; each rung holds to a peek); "Rep maxes" as chips. Est. 1RM and volumes are shown to one decimal (display only).
- **History tab**: a strip of three tiles (sessions, last done "N days ago", best 1RM) and, when nothing is logged, an `EmptyState` with "Add to workout" (into the running workout, or a new one).
- **Measurements** (`Body.kt`): a BMI card (value, band word in its colour, `BmiBandBar`), then "Body" (Strong's files) and "Your own" as cards (label and day, big latest value, change since the entry before, a sparkline; tap opens MeasurementDetail), a quiet "Not logged yet" list, an `EmptyState` when nothing is logged and a + in the bar. The cards keep the `m:label`, `m:value` and `m:date` morph roles; no card-to-page chart morph was added (Round 21).
- **MeasurementDetail**: the chart's y range is padded (`paddedRange`: at least 5 % of the span either side, never narrower than 3 % of the value, 0.5 at least); entry dates are `prettyDay` with the time grey; lowest, highest and average tiles; delete already shows the undo pill (`deleteMeasurement`); the line's morph key is per file (`measurementMorphKey`: `chart:weight` for weight, `chart:m:<file>` for the others) so it cannot travel to another file's card.
- **BMI** (`Bmi.kt`): a hero number with count-up, a segmented band bar with a marker, "About BMI" with one line and "Why this may mislead" expanding the rest, "Over time" on the shared `measurementRangeSelector`, no empty section title. The height row is feet and inches for a pound profile (`heightText`; storage stays cm).
- **Month and Week** (`Pages.kt`): "‹ Month before / Month after ›" and the Week's are one `PeriodNav` (after is off past today); `PeriodHero` (volume large with a delta chip in the accent, then workouts, time and PRs with theirs; zero changes show no chip), against the month before or, on the Week, the eight-week average; Month adds a small calendar (`MonthGrid`, the Calendar's scale; a day opens the Calendar on it) and `ThinBar` bars; Week's sets per muscle are `SetsBar`s with the shaded weekly target (`setRanges`), the tick for the usual week and amber, accent or quiet as before (the "(n)" brackets are gone); PRs are `PrChip`s (`PrChipRow`).
- **Trends**: the week still running (`isRunningWeek`) is a dashed last segment ending in a hollow dot ("so far"), kept out of the trend (`trendOf(points, partialLast)`), the average and the last four weeks, and out of the "latest" line. "Trend: −0.03 workouts a week, per month" (`trendLine`): one space, the app's minus, a named unit. No morph for a chart with a running week.
- **Muscle page**: a muscle never trained shows "–" for Retained strength and a grey "not trained yet" and "never" (`LabelValue(valueColor)`).

## Round 24: the nooks, second half; the card's line on Back (2026-11-21; not run on a device)

Pictures: `app/build/screens/default/round24-graphite-{dark,light}-*.png` (`Round24Test`). Shared pieces are in `Nooks.kt` (`TimelineRail`, `TimelineMonth`, `TimelineRow`, `MonthBars`, `RankedRow`, `prImprovements`, `gainText`).

- **PR history** (`PrTimelineScreen`, `Pages.kt`): a timeline. A thin rail joins the PRs, a month heading (with its PR count) sits on the rail, each PR is a dot with the set and its day, a `PrChip` for each record it broke and one grey line of how far each rose over the record of that kind before it (`prImprovements`: per kind and side; 1RM, weight, set volume, reps, duration; the first record says "First record"). A tap opens the workout, a hold peeks at it. The PR chip colours are untouched.
- **Exercise Info tab**: the muscles are chips under small labels (main filled, supporting and minor outlined; a chip opens the muscle); the goal bar is a `ThinBar` in `goalColor()`; with no notes a gentle line says what a note is for. **Charts and PRs tabs** with nothing logged, and the Routine, Notes to variations and Month pages with nothing in them, show `EmptyState`.
- **History tab tiles** hug their content: `Tile(minHeight = 0.dp)` (the default stays the 132 dp Home's tiles share).
- **Year in review**: a bar for each month's volume (`MonthBars`; the best month is the full accent, the others half; a bar opens that month), "Most trained" as ranked rows with `ThinBar`s (five exercises, `topExercises` in core), "Best streak" (was "Longest streak"), and a Share button in the bar: the year as plain text (`yearSummaryText`) through the phone's share sheet (`Platform.shareText`, no new platform work).
- **Template page**: three tiles at the top (Exercises, Sets, Done), then the lines; the up and down arrows are the meaning colours (`done`, `failure`; level is quiet) and a caption plus a coloured legend say they compare the last session's estimated 1RM with the one before.
- **Personal bests**: the search field is gone; the top three have their place in the accent and a larger figure; each lift with two sessions or more has a `Sparkline` of its estimated 1RM.
- **Trophies**: every badge not yet earned is dimmed and shows "n to go" and a thin bar of how far along (`UnearnedRow`), the next of each ladder first; a week of the grid opens the Week page (as it did) and "Best streak" is the word on Trophies and Year in review.
- **About**: the changelog shows the latest release and "Show all" opens the rest.
- **The card's line on Back** (`ChartMorphLine`, `CardMorph.kt`; found on the phone, Home's Weight card with the page on 1Y): the travelling line took the page's own daily points as its grid and mixed them with the card's smooth curve linearly in progress, so on Back the last few per cent of the way (the long tail of the move, many frames) still carried a few per cent of the page's wiggle, which then vanished when the real line took over. Now the page's detail (its points less their moving average, `ChartLine.smoothY`) is let in by `pageDetailShare(q)`: none in the last 8 % of the way to the card, all of it from 40 %, a smooth step between. The line's course still goes with the whole move, and at the page's end it is the page's line to the pixel (Round 21), so only the first third of opening (where the line is moving fastest) shows the wiggle a little later than before. It applies to every card that shares the line morph (Home and Profile weight, the 1RM tiles, the Trends cards); a card denser than its page is unchanged. `MorphProbe.chartQ` and `chartDraws` are for tests.


## Round 25: the card's line holds still at the end of Back (2026-11-21)

The weight card's line still drifted after the card landed when closed by a back swipe (seen on the phone, test 37). The move's easing
(Emphasized) spends its last third of the time on its last tenth of the way. Every part of the card covers that tenth over a short
distance, but the travelling line comes from the page's chart, far above, so a tenth of its way is a visible slide. The travelling line now
follows `chartLineProgress(q)`: it is the card's own line, in the card's place, over the last 12 % of the way (`CHART_CARD_HOLD`), and
holds still there while the card finishes. Opening leaves the card the same amount later. Pinned by
`Round24Test.closingTheWeightCardByABackSwipeLeavesItsLineAsItIsWhenTheCardLands`, which closes by a predictive-back gesture.


## Round 26: performance tiers, one haptic vocabulary, a What's new page (2026-11-23; not run on a device)

Three items from the "before the 1.1.0 release" list. Pictures: `app/build/screens/default/round26-graphite-{dark,light}-*.png` (`Round26Test`). Nothing here was felt or seen on a phone.

### Performance (Settings, Appearance, Performance; `Performance.kt`)

Three tiers, kv `performance` (`full` is stored as nothing). **The tier is a floor**: `CleanRoot` provides `LocalPerformance` and sets the switches that were already there from it, so Reduce transparency (`glass.reduce`), the phone's own settings and Animations (`ui.motion`) can only take away more. `solidGlass = glass.reduce || tier.solidGlass || phone forces it`; Animations off still cuts everything at every tier; Light stays animated (the blink, press feedback, the rest ring's clock, PR badges and glows still play).

What each costs, and where it goes:

| Effect | Cost | Full | Balanced | Light |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Haze blur behind glass (`Modifier.glass`, dock, sheets, rest bar, hold cards, toast, Undo) | a blur of the page behind it every frame it is up, the heaviest thing here | blurred | opaque form | opaque form |
| `Modifier.blur(12.dp)` on the page behind a hold (`App.kt`) and the progressive blur under the dock | a second full-page blur while a hold is up, a haze strip all the time | on | off | off |
| `ChartMorphLine` (the travelling weight and 1RM line between a card and its page) | a path rebuilt every frame from hundreds of points, the tint and its mask in a layer | travels | cross-fade (the chart's own line on each side) | cross-fade |
| Card-to-page container transform (`SharedTransitionLayout`, shared bounds, `MorphText`, clip) | the whole tabs page and the page drawn together, clipped and moved for 500 ms | grows | grows | the blink |
| Theme cross-fade (`CleanTheme(crossFade)`) | the app drawn twice for a moment | on | on | off |
| Count-ups (`countUp`, `countUpOnce`) | recomposes text 250 ms per figure | on | on | the figure |
| Ring and sparkline draw-ins (`ProgressRing`, `drawnIn`), chart range stretch (`rememberStretch`) | canvas redraws for 300 to 600 ms | on | on | at once (a rest ring keeps its clock) |
| Roll of a changed number (`rememberRoll`), rows settling in (`settleIn`), the calendar wave (`rememberWave`) | per-row layers and a clock | on | on | off |
| Breathing: the finish button (endless), the muscles trained today (`rememberBreath`), the dock's running dot (endless) | a clock that never stops, wakes the screen every frame | on | on | off |

Defaults to Full. **Suggestion**: once, at start when nothing else is up (not on the welcome, not over What's new), a phone that says it is low on memory or on battery saver (`Platform.lowPowerHint`; Android: `ActivityManager.isLowRamDevice` or `PowerManager.isPowerSaveMode`; false on iOS) is offered Balanced in a toast with a Switch action; kv `performance.suggested` records it so it is never asked twice, and nothing is asked once a tier was chosen. Phone check: whether the offer comes up on a phone with battery saver on.

### Haptics (`Haptics.kt`, `Platform.haptic`)

One vocabulary, `Haptic`: **Tick** (selection, a threshold passed, a peek opening, a pad key), **Detent** (a whole kilo on the ruler), **Confirm** (a set done, a weigh-in saved, a rest over), **Heavy** (a PR, a badge, a finished workout), **Reject** (Undo). `Platform.haptic(kind)` is the one seam: Android `HapticFeedbackConstants` on the window (`AndroidHaptics.constantFor`: Tick `SEGMENT_FREQUENT_TICK` on API 34+, `TEXT_HANDLE_MOVE` on 27+, `CLOCK_TICK` below; Detent `SEGMENT_TICK` on 34+, else `CLOCK_TICK`; Confirm `CONFIRM` on 30+, else `VIRTUAL_KEY`; Heavy `LONG_PRESS`; Reject `REJECT` on 30+, else `LONG_PRESS`), the iPhone `UISelectionFeedbackGenerator`, a light impact, `UINotificationFeedbackGenerator` success, a heavy impact and an error notification. Screens use `rememberHaptic()` / `rememberTick()` / `rememberDetent()`, the model and effects `AppModel.buzz(kind)`; both honour Haptic ticks (kv `haptic.ticks`), which now covers every UI buzz (a set ticked used to ignore it; with Animations off a set still gives no buzz). **No event fires two**: `HapticGate` drops a buzz that lands within 150 ms of one at least as strong (a set ticked that is also a PR gives the PR's). Nothing buzzes on a scroll: the only buzzes near a list are the pull-to-search and Home pull thresholds (once each) and the letter rail's letter changes (a drag on the rail, not a scroll).

| Event | Before | After |
|---|---|---|
| A segmented control changes, a calendar day is tapped, a chip in the weigh-in date picks, a chart scrub changes point | `SegmentFrequentTick` | Tick |
| A hold-to-peek opens (Activity day, History row, bar, Trophies, body figure, avatar, tiles) and the peek moves to another target | `SegmentFrequentTick` | Tick, once on opening |
| A hold menu opens (`holdMenu`) | `LongPress` | Tick |
| A photo selected by a long press (Moments) | `LongPress` | Tick |
| A swipe reaches its trigger (rows with a swipe; the Undo pill; the rest bar) | `LongPress` / the set's tick / `SegmentFrequentTick` | Tick |
| Pull to search, Home's pull threshold | `SegmentFrequentTick` | Tick |
| Letter rail changes letter | `SegmentFrequentTick` | Tick |
| Weigh-in ruler step (at most one in 25 ms) | `SegmentFrequentTick` | Tick |
| Weigh-in ruler whole kilo | `SegmentTick` | Detent |
| A number-pad key (workout pad, type pad) | `TextHandleMove` | Tick |
| An exercise grabbed to reorder, and each step | `LongPress`, `TextHandleMove` | Tick, Tick |
| A set ticked (its box, or finished by a swipe) | `TextHandleMove`, even with Haptic ticks off | Confirm |
| A new PR (live in the workout) | Vibrator double tap + chime | Heavy + chime |
| A badge or week reward on the summary, a program reward | Vibrator double tap + chime | Heavy + chime |
| A workout finished | none | Heavy |
| Rest: the last three seconds | Vibrator tick | Tick |
| Rest over | a pulse only when the rest is set to Buzz | Buzz keeps its 150/600 ms pulse; otherwise Confirm |
| Undo | none | Reject |
| A weigh-in or measurement saved | none | Confirm |

`Platform.softTick` and `doubleTick` are gone. **Every haptic is a phone check: none was felt.** `Round26Test` checks the mapping (the Android constants per API level, a recording platform behind the gate, the model's buzzes reaching the window, Haptic ticks off, Undo as Reject), not how any of it feels.

### What's new (`WhatsNew.kt`)

A full-screen page (`WhatsNewPage`, a window that blinks in like every dialog) over the app: "What's new" and "Clean 1.1.0", the first six changes of the version in `assets/changes.md` as highlights in one glass card (a picture in an accent-tinted disc chosen by the headline's first word, `highlightIcon`; the headline bold; one line), a "See everything" button that swaps (the blink) to every version's list with "Back to the highlights", and one **Continue**. Back closes it too. It shows once after an update (`whatsnew.seen` holds the last version shown): a version never recorded (a fresh install, or an app that never wrote one) is only recorded, so a first start never shows it. Settings, About has "Open the What's new page" at the top of its What's new group. `changes.md` has a 1.1.0 section: six highlights, then nine more lines. The text ships in the app and the public repository.


## Round 27: the number pad closes on a tap, a tick works with it up, Health Connect kept in line (2026-11-23; not run on a device)

Two requests from Rohan. Pictures: `app/build/screens/default/round27-graphite-{dark,light}-*.png` (`Round27Test`). Nothing here was seen or felt on a phone, and nothing Health Connect does was run: Robolectric has no Health Connect.

### The number pad during a workout (`closePadOnTap`, `padGround`, `Active.kt`)

Rohan's words: "during a workout, when the numberpad is up, it should go down if you tap anywhere on the screen except for the number boxes and also i want the set completion checkbox to be tickable with the numberpad up, and for it to not go down". This **replaces** the 2026-11-16 rule (a touch outside closed the pad at touch *down* and its release was swallowed, so nothing under it was pressed). Now:

- A **tap** (finger up within the touch slop and the long-press time, one finger) on anything that is not the pad's own ground closes the pad, **when the finger lifts**. That is the empty list, a card's header, the top bar and the rest panel (all under the Scaffold the observer is on). The observer sits at the `Initial` pointer pass and **consumes nothing**, so what is under the finger still gets its tap: Add set, an exercise's menu or Finish runs **and** the pad closes. A card's name, which opens the exercise page, does too (a tap on it used to be swallowed).
- **Not a tap, so the pad stays up**: a drag or scroll (past the slop), a swipe, a hold (the hold-to-reorder and hold menus), a second finger.
- **The pad's own ground** is `pad.bounds` (the pad) and every spot tagged with `Modifier.padGround()`: the weight, reps (and time, distance) boxes (`PadField`) and the set's **tick box** (both the single row's and the left/right half's). The tag keeps the box's window bounds in `pad.fields`, as the boxes always did. A tap on another number box is not a tap on the outside, so the pad moves to that box (`pad.focus`): it does not close and reopen.
- **A tick with the pad up** works as ever (the toggle gets the touch, the pad is not touched): the set is ticked or unticked, the rest timer starts, the Confirm buzz and PR badges happen, and the pad stays up. **Where focus goes after a tick: nowhere, it stays on the box it was on** (`pad.target` is not touched). Reasons: it is predictable (a tick never moves what the next digit types into), a tick on a set the pad is not on must not move the pad, and the Next key already moves on in one tap; jumping to the next set's weight after a tick would also start that box "fresh", so a stray digit would wipe a value. The cost is one more tap on Next when the pad was on the set just ticked.
- Back closes the pad first (as before); the pad's own keys, RPE, Next and the hardware keyboard are unchanged. The same observer is on the Correct page, which has no ticks.

Tests (`Round27Test`, regression): a tap on a card header closes the pad and the header's tap still opens its page; a tap on the top bar closes it; a tap on another box moves the pad (the next digit goes there); a tick with the pad up ticks, keeps the pad and the box (the next digit adds to it), starts the rest, and unticks the same way; Add set closes the pad and adds the set; a scroll leaves the pad up and a tap after it closes it; a hold leaves it up; Back closes it and stays on the workout; the pad's keys still type. `ReorderHoldTest`'s two pad tests were rewritten for the new rule.

### Health Connect (`Health.kt`, `core/HealthSync.kt`, `AppModel.healthCatchUp`, Settings, Data and backup)

**What is sent.** Three kinds, with the `clientRecordId` scheme unchanged (`clean:workout:<profile>:<start>`, `clean:weight:<profile>:<date>` and the new `clean:bodyfat:<profile>:<date>`), so records written before are updated in place, never doubled:

- a **workout** is an `ExerciseSessionRecord` (strength training) with the name (a blank one is "Workout", as the app shows it; cut at 100 characters), the start, an end, and `notes` = the summary. **No exercise segments**: there are no per-set times and inventing them would be fake data.
- **Summary** (`workoutSummary`): the workout's own note first, a line break, then each exercise (the same exercise twice is one) in the order done, joined by " · ": `Bench Press (Barbell) 3 × 8 @ 60 kg · Squat (Barbell) 5 × 5 @ 100 kg`. Working sets only (warm-ups left out; an exercise with only warm-ups is left out). When every working set is alike: `n × reps @ weight`; when they differ: `n sets, top 62.5 kg × 6` (the most reps at the heaviest weight). In the profile's unit (lb to the nearest half pound, as shown). No weight, no `@`; timed sets `1 × 45 s`; distance-only sets `1 × 5 km`; a set with no reps, time or distance is only counted. Cut at 1000 characters with "· +n more".
- a **weight** is a `WeightRecord`; a **body fat** is a `BodyFatRecord` (0 to 100 per cent) from `body_fat_percentage.csv`; both from the stored entries.
- every record has `Device(type = TYPE_PHONE)` in its metadata (the 1.1.0 API allows it on `manualEntry`).
- **Overlaps** (`trimmedSeconds`): a workout that would end at or after the next workout's start (by start time) ends one second before it; one with no length is a minute; never less than a second. Health Connect can refuse overlapping sessions. The app's own record is untouched; only what is sent is shorter.

**What it does with them: one catch-up, not a call at every edit.** The old code wrote or removed one record at a call site (finishing, correcting, deleting, adding, editing, undoing, importing) and lost every change made with the switch off. It is gone. Now `AppModel.healthCatchUp()` brings Health Connect in line with the database for the active profile, and runs two seconds after the workouts, measurements, profiles or the switch change (so every add, edit, delete and undo, an import, a restore, a rename or merge, a note, a deleted profile and a changed unit are covered by one rule), when the switch is turned on, and at start with the switch on (once the first page is loaded). It reads the rows from the database itself, not from the screens' lists (they lag a profile switch). The diff (`planHealth`): write what is missing or whose fingerprint (a stable hash of everything that is sent) changed; remove what Clean sent that no longer exists (of the active profile, or of a profile that is gone). Other profiles' records are left alone. "Send everything logged so far" forces every record again.

**How it knows what Clean wrote: its own list, not reading Health Connect.** Reading the records back, filtered by Clean's package, would need three READ permissions (exercise, weight, body fat) so that an app that only writes could also read health data: more prompts and a less honest ask. Clean keeps a **ledger** instead: the id and fingerprint of every record Health Connect took, on the phone outside the database (`Platform.healthLedger`; Android: a private `health` preferences file), so a restore or a wipe of the database does not change it, which is right, since it describes what is in Health Connect. Only what went through is entered; a failed record is tried again at the next catch-up. Costs: records Clean wrote before this round for something since deleted stay until "Remove them"; and if the app's data is cleared the ledger goes (the next catch-up writes everything again, in place, so nothing is doubled). **The only permission added is `WRITE_BODY_FAT`**; nothing is read except today's steps, as before. A phone that had the switch on before does not have it: body fat is not written until it is allowed, Settings shows an "Allow body fat" row (it asks), and the last-sync line says "not allowed: body fat".

**Failures** (`runHealthChunks`, `classifyHealthFailure`): the grant is read **once per run**; records go in chunks of at most 1000 per `insertRecords` (ids likewise per `deleteRecords`); a chunk that fails is tried again, alone, after 1 s, 4 s and 15 s (`HEALTH_BACKOFF`); a chunk that went through is never sent twice. A missing permission is not retried and stops the run; a rate limit that outlasts the waits stops it; any other fault leaves that chunk and goes on. `CancellationException` is never swallowed or retried. Every failure is one of **permission removed**, **rate limited** or **failed** with the first words of why. Removes no longer hide inside `runCatching`.

**Settings** (Data and backup, Health Connect): the switch is "Send to Health Connect", summary "Workouts (with a summary of the sets), body weight and body fat"; under it "Last sync: Sent 3, removed 1" (or "Up to date", "Health Connect refused: permission removed", "Sent 2; 5 not sent: rate limited", "Health Connect failed: ...", "(not allowed: body fat)"), kept in kv `health.last` and `health.missing`; "Allow body fat" when it is missing; "Send everything logged so far". A failure is also said once as a toast when it differs from the line already shown. **Switching it off asks once**: "Health Connect is off", "Keep them" / "Remove them". Remove them deletes every record Clean wrote (any profile) by a time-range delete, which Health Connect limits to the calling app's own data, and clears the ledger. **Delete everything** removes them first when the switch is on (its dialog says so), since the switch is wiped with everything else and nothing would remove them later.

Tests: `HealthSyncTest` (core, JVM): the summary (the brief's example, warm-ups, unequal sets, lb, the note first, timed and distance sets, the cut), the title, overlap trimming, the desired records and their ids, the plan (missing, changed, gone, forced, other profiles, deleted profiles, delete everything), the ledger text, chunks of a thousand, a failed chunk retried alone with the backoff, a rate limit and a missing permission stopping the run, another fault going on, cancellation, the three kinds of failure, the words. `Round27Test`: the settings text and last-sync line, the Keep/Remove question, the sync's words with no Health Connect and with no permission. **Everything that talks to Health Connect is a phone check**: that records arrive with title, notes and device, that an edit updates in place, that Keep them and Remove them do what they say, that body fat is accepted, that a rate limit is survived.


## Round 28: two ways to start, the estimate at every rep count, a PR colour of its own, an Add exercise sheet, a card that folds from the top (2026-11-23; not run on a device)

Ten requests from Rohan. Pictures: `app/build/screens/default/round28-graphite-{dark,light}-0{1..6}-*.png` and `round28-<theme>-{dark,light}-07-prs.png` for all eight themes (`Round28Test`). Nothing here was seen or felt on a phone.

1. **Starting a workout** (`WorkoutSheetContent`, `Templates.kt`). The plus button opens the Workout sheet (with a workout running it goes back to that workout), and the sheet now begins with two tiles side by side, above the template search: **Empty workout** (one tap; it replaces the old "Quick start, empty" row) and **Pick exercises**. Pick exercises opens the exercise picker in multi-select (`multiOnly`): a tap on a row picks it, a ring at the row's end shows its place in the order (`PickMark`: 1, 2, 3; the numbers close up when one is taken out), and the bar at the foot says "Start 3" (`AppModel.startWithExercises`: a workout named "Workout" with those cards in that order; with one already running it asks, as a template does). Back, the close control or a tap outside closes the picker and starts nothing. No Do next there. **Reordering after picking is not built** (the request said optional): the order is the order of the taps, and a card can be moved in the workout as ever.
2. **The estimated 1RM at every rep count.** The cut-off was the user's own setting "Most reps it is used for" (`onerm.maxReps`: 10, 12, 15 or 20; 12 by default since 2026-10-22, enforced inside `estimate1Rm`), so 35 kg x 20 never had a figure. `OneRm.shown()` is the same formula with no limit (100 reps), and it is what every figure *beside a set* now uses: History's card (and its left and right columns), the workout page's column and rounds, an exercise page's set rows, `estimateOf`. **Records do not change**: PR judgement, `progressOf`, the charts, Home, Profile, the export and the next target keep the rule's limit, so a set of 20 shows a figure but is never a 1RM PR. The formula is the chosen one; Brzycki, Lander and Average break down above about 30 reps (Brzycki divides by zero at 37), so past 30 they continue with Epley, which Hybrid already is from 10 up. No "~" marker was added: proposed, not built, a "~" before a figure above the user's own limit.
3. **A PR colour of its own per theme** (`PR_COLOURS`, `Look.prColour`, `prColor()`). This **reverses round 20's "PR = accent"**. A gold for seven themes, tuned in Oklch; Ember, whose accent is already an orange-gold, takes a violet. Dark, then Light: Graphite FFD74B 846801, Ember E8D4FE 613EA6, Crimson F6D64C 655401, Moss FFCD45 876602, Ocean FDD343 856801, Plum FED848 836801, Ice FFD343 866701, Rose FBD950 826901. Each is at least 60 degrees of hue from its theme's accent (Graphite's neutral accent: a chromatic gold instead), at least 10 (100 x Oklab) from the done, ready, failure, fatigued and danger colours and 15 from the accent, 3 : 1 against page, card and tile and 4.5 : 1 as text on its own chip tint (`prTint`, 12 % Dark, 8 % Light). Applied through `prColor()` to the History card's count, the workout page's badges, the chips, the timeline and the records tiles, and to the star on Progress's History rows and Profile's. **A matched best (not a PR) is now quiet grey** (`onSurfaceVariant`), not the amber `tertiary`: the gold and the amber were 3 to 5 apart. Colour vision: the PR colour is kept apart from the accent under all three kinds, and from the good and bad colours on a dark page; on a light page the gold and the reds are both a dark yellow-brown to a dichromat (a gold light enough to differ would fail 4.5 : 1 as text), so there the star and the words ("Weight", "1RM", "3 PRs") carry the meaning, as for every meaning colour. `Round20ColoursTest` and `Round22ContrastTest` read `prColour`; `principles.md` says it.
4. **Search closes with the keyboard** (`Search.kt`). A Back (or back swipe) that only puts the keyboard away used to leave the pill out. `SearchPill` now watches the keyboard (`WindowInsets.ime`): when it was up and goes down with **nothing typed**, the pill collapses (`collapsesOnKeyboardDown`). **With text typed** the first Back hides the keyboard and keeps the pill and the text; the second Back (the `BackHandler` of `SearchBox`) clears and collapses. History uses the same `SearchBox`, so it behaves the same. The exercise pickers use a *pinned* pill (`SearchOpener.pinned`): always out, its close control only empties it. A phone check: the real keyboard going down.
5. **The Add exercise sheet and its kin** (`PickerSheet.kt`, `ExercisePicker`). The card in the middle of the screen is now a `PickerSheet`: a glass sheet 92 % of the screen with a grab handle, a title and a close control, the search pill (the Library's) under it, the chips (Muscle, Equipment, Performed, Favourites), then the Library's list (Favourites, Recent with the last day and best set of each, A to Z with the letter rail), and a bar at the foot with the action ("Add 2", "Start 3") once several are ticked. Every `ExercisePicker` has a title now: Add exercise, Replace exercise, Add a variation, Start as a copy of, Use another exercise, Pick an exercise. The audit of the other pickers is in `build-log.md`.
6. **Queue and Do next.** One tap on a row queues it (the end of the workout), the default. **Do next is a small trailing button on each row** (a forward arrow and the words), not a hold: it is found without knowing a gesture, and a row already holds elsewhere. It puts the exercise right after the exercise being done now (`AppModel.addExercise(afterCurrent = true)`: after the card of the next set to do and after the last of its superset; at the end when every card is done). Only the running workout's picker has it; the ring-and-number multi-select still works there (tick one, then more; "Add 2" queues them in order).
7. **The next exercise stays put while a finished one folds** (`ActiveScreen`). The cause: when the last set of an exercise was ticked, the list started an animated scroll to bring the next exercise to the top, its distance worked out from the layout *before* the finished card shrank; the card then shrank under the scroll, so the list overshot and ended below. Now the next card is first made the list's anchor where it stands (`requestScrollToItem(next + 1, -offset)`), so the card above it shrinking cannot move it by a pixel, and the scroll then eases it to the top on the glide curve. With animations off it goes to the top at once, as before (`LocalMotion`; the fold itself is unchanged). `Round28Test` samples the next card's top every 16 ms.
8. **No rest before a drop set.** `rest.drop` ("After a drop set", Settings, Rest by set type) is the rest *after* a drop set and stays. New rule, no setting: ticking a set whose next set in the same card is a drop set starts no rest (and ends one still running), so the drop follows at once; the last drop set of a run rests as `rest.drop` says. Left and right pairs keep the between-sides rest.
9. **Swipe left removes a set; swipe right no longer ticks it.** `Feel.swipeRight` is "off" by default (it was "finish"): a set row swipes only to the left, past 110 dp, with the Tick buzz at the threshold and the Undo pill ("Set removed") as before; the tick box is the only way to finish a set. The "Remove set" item of the row's menu stays for whoever cannot swipe. A phone check: the feel of it.
10. **The current set** (`Glide`, `WorkoutSkin.rowNext`, `SetRow`). The set to do next is a wash of the accent (11 % Dark, 9 % Light, stronger than a done row's 8 %) with a 3 dp accent bar at its leading edge and its number in the accent; once ticked the number goes back to the set kind's colour and the done wash takes over, so it does not fight the PR colour (never the accent now) or the ticked look. "Show the next set" in Settings still switches it off.


## Round 29: the dock names the tab and can be slid along (2026-11-24; not run on a device)

Rohan chose two things from the dock mockups (`artifacts/round5/dock-labels.png`). Pictures: `app/build/screens/default/round29-graphite-{dark,light}-0{1,2,3}-*.png` (`Round29Test`, the tour). Nothing here was seen or felt on a phone.

### The name flashes on a switch (`TabBar`, `TabItem`, `App.kt`)

- When the current tab changes, **by a tap, a swipe of the pages or code**, its name appears under its icon (10.5 sp, semibold, the theme's text colour) and fades out. `TabBar` watches `current`; the first value (the app's start) is skipped. The name fades in over 100 ms, stays `NAME_HOLD` (900 ms), then fades over `NAME_FADE` (300 ms); it is gone about 1.3 s after the switch. A tab that follows another before the first has gone makes the first fade while the new one fades in.
- The icon rises 7 dp (`NAME_LIFT`) while its name is up and settles as it fades (the same animated 0 to 1 as the text's opacity), so both fit in the bar. **The bar never changes size** (its height is `dockHeight`, untouched). **The capsule indicator does not move**: it is as tall as the bar, so lifting it would take it out of the bar; the icon and the name sit inside it.
- **Animations off (`LocalMotion`): nothing shows.** Chosen over "the name for about 1 s with no fade" because a cut is exactly what the setting asks for, and every tab still speaks its name. **Performance tiers do not change it**: it is an opacity and a shift on three small layers, answers a touch and does nothing on its own, so Light keeps it (Round 26's rule is for what moves unasked).
- Accessibility: the slot keeps its role, its selected state and its content description. The name `Text` is `clearAndSetSemantics` (tagged `dock name <Tab>` for tests) so a screen reader does not say it twice.

### A slide along the dock (`TabBar`'s `pointerInput`, `AppModel.dockSliding`)

- A finger down on the dock and moved sideways past the touch slop **slides**: the indicator follows the finger (`finger`, joined to `follow` so `rememberStretch` draws it as it draws a page swipe), the tab the indicator is over (`roundHalfUp`) becomes current **live**, with `Haptic.Tick` at each change, and its name stays up while the finger is down; the hold starts at the lift. The start slot is the origin, so the indicator moves with the finger from where it was touched. Letting go stays on the tab. When the finger stops over the same tab it started on, a speed above 600 dp/s (`DOCK_FLICK`) is a **flick: one tab** in that direction (none past the ends).
- **Taps** are untouched: the slots' own `clickable` takes them; the slide consumes events only once it has begun, so a slot's tap is cancelled by a slide and not otherwise. **A vertical start does nothing** (a drag that goes past the slop mostly up or down is dropped for the rest of the touch). **The plus button is a sibling, not a child**: a slide that begins on the dock stays on the dock whatever it ends over, so it never fires the plus. There is **no long-press** on the dock and nothing else to collide with; the scroll-shrink (round 4 idea 11, `compact`) listens to the page's list, not to touches on the dock, so a slide does not trigger it.
- **How a slide changes pages (the choice).** A tap changes pages with a blink (the pager fades out, jumps, fades in, on one clock) and a new tab change cancels the blink running. A slide across three tabs would restart it three times. So while a finger slides (`AppModel.dockSliding`) `Tabs` **switches the page at once, with no blink, at each tab**; nothing is animated at the end (the page is already the right one). A flick clears the flag before the change, so it blinks once, like a tap. No transition is ever queued or left half done; the shared Activity card is not involved (it only shows while the pager is between Home and Progress at a fractional offset, which a cut never is).

### Also in Round 29 (from the main session)

- **Photos**: `photos.blur` thumbnails are cut to 8 px in common code (`Photos.kt`, `shrunk()`): a phone's JPEG decoder may stop at 1/8, which left the Profile fan sharp.
- **Search**: the Library search pill starts closing as the keyboard starts down (below 85 % of its height, `KeyboardWatch`), with a 140 ms accelerating exit (`Motion.searchOut()`).

Tests: `Round29Test` (regression; the tour is `Tour`): the name after a tap and gone by 1.3 s, never at start, by code, content descriptions kept, animations off; a slide live and ending under the finger, on a middle tab, ticking, over the plus, a flick moving one tab, at the end of the dock, a vertical start, animations off. **Phone checks**: how the name and the lift look at 10.5 sp, the feel of the slide and its ticks, the flick's speed, the pages' cut while sliding.


## Round 30: snapshots blur, a relative dock slide, a set that swipes 1:1, a rail that leaves Back alone, a hold on the workout pill (2026-11-24; not run on a device)

Five items from Rohan's test 42. Pictures: `app/build/screens/default/round30-graphite-{dark,light}-0{1..7}-*.png` (`Round30Test`, the tour). Nothing here was seen or felt on a phone.

1. **Snapshots blur** (`Moments.kt`, `Photos.kt`, Settings). `photos.blur` only ever reached progress photos. `MomentThumb` (the Snapshots fan, 240 px, and the Moments grid, 360 px; there is no other small view of a moment) now calls the same `decodeThumb` as `PhotoThumb`: decoded at 64 px, cut to 8 px (`BLUR_SIDE`), stretched. Full-screen pictures stay sharp. The kv key is unchanged; the label is "Blur photos" with the summary "Progress photos and snapshots, in every small view." **Cache**: `thumbKey(file, px, blur)`: a blurred thumbnail is keyed `file@blur`, a sharp one `file@px`, so a change of the setting is a change of key and shows at once and neither is ever served for the other.
2. **The dock slide is relative** (`TabBar`, `App.kt`). The start is the tab that was current when the finger went down, not the slot under it. The selection is `start + drag / slot width`, rounded and clamped to the dock; nothing changes before half a slot; the indicator follows the same position (the capsule stretches toward the next tab before the selection changes). A flick (above 600 dp/s, selection still the start) moves one tab from the start in its direction. The tick and the name flash are as in round 29. Rohan's example is a test: on Home, finger over Library, one slot right, lands on Progress.
3. **Swipe to delete a set** (`SwipeRow`, `Ui.kt`; `Glide`, `Active.kt`). The rubber band of round 6a is gone for every `SwipeRow`: the row follows the finger 1:1. **Thresholds**: letting go past **40 % of the row's width** removes the set (the colour turns solid and the Tick buzz fires there); a **fling of at least 1000 dp/s over at least 32 dp** in the direction of an action removes it earlier; anything else springs back. A removal slides the row fully off, then its height closes (`rememberRemoval`); the row's own Undo pill ("Set removed") is the one compact `UndoBar`. **Highlight integration**: the current set's wash and 3 dp bar belong to the row: the card's draw reads the row's swipe (`LocalSwipeWatch`) and the row's live place, so both move with it, on the left and right halves of a pair as well. The delete colour is inset to the row's own rectangle (`SwipeRow(inset)`) with the row's corner (`SmallShape`), starting at the row's edge. When the current set is removed the highlight goes with the row and the new current set is lit by fading in where it stands (`Glide.drop`, then `go`), so it never jumps back to the old place.
4. **The Library letter rail and Back** (`Exercises.kt`). The rail took a touch the moment it went down, so a back swipe from the right edge started it. Now it waits: a drag that is clearly vertical once past the touch slop, a press held for the long-press time, or a tap (jumps on lift) takes it over; a touch that moves sideways first is never consumed, so it goes to the system and the page. **Choice on `systemGestures`**: no `systemGestureExclusion` (the rail is as tall as the list, so an exclusion would stop Back along the whole right edge of the Library) and the rail stays at the edge rather than moving in by the 24 to 30 dp gesture zone (that would cover that much of every row); Android's back gesture only claims a horizontal start, so a vertical drag in the zone still reaches the rail. Phone check: a real back swipe from the right edge on the Library.
5. **A hold on the running workout's pill** (`PlusButton`, `App.kt`; `HoldOverlay`). With a workout running, a hold opens a `holdMenu` with the Round 26 Tick: a peek head (the workout's name, "12:34 · 1 of 6 sets", "Now: <exercise>"), then **Resume**, **Add exercise** (the Round 28 `ExercisePicker` sheet; adds to the running workout through `addExercise`, one tap queues, Do next works), **Skip rest** and **Add 30 s** (only while a rest runs; `startRest(0)` and `changeRest(30)`, the workout's own rest options), **Finish workout** and, separated by a divider and red, **Cancel workout**. The workout screen has no menu of its own; these are its Finish, Cancel and rest buttons. Finish and Cancel call `resumeWorkout(request)`: the workout opens (grown out of the pill) and, once the page is up, does what its own buttons do (`AppModel.activeRequest`): Finish asks about unfinished sets or saves, Cancel asks with its slide-to-confirm dialog. No logic is copied. Every hold menu now draws a divider before a deletion that is not first.

Tests: `Round30Test` (regression; the tour is `Tour`), `Round29Test` (the flick is one tab from the start tab). **Phone checks**: the swipe feel and the fling speed, the highlight in motion, a real back swipe on the Library, the hold menu's place beside the pill at the screen's corner, the blurred fan on the phone.


## Round 31: a swipe that goes all the way, Lock photos, a dock slide that waits, a soft blur, a fan without its card (2026-11-24; not run on a device)

Five items from Rohan's test 44. Pictures: `app/build/screens/default/round31-graphite-{dark,light}-0{1,2,3}-*.png` (`Round31Test`, the tour). Nothing here was seen or felt on a phone.

1. **Swipe to delete a set** (`SwipeRow`, `Ui.kt`; `EdgeGesture.kt`). Rohan's video showed the row following the finger, the red growing, and on letting go the row back at rest within one frame and the set still there; then, in his words, "It's not a cancel gesture. I'm just trying to swipe from the right side of the screen to the left side of the screen. Just a quick swipe normally should make it work."
   - **Cause.** The swipe starts at the screen's right edge, where Android's own Back gesture lives. The system takes such a touch away and the app is told it was **cancelled**; the old `onDragCancel` sprang the row home and judged nothing, so a swipe that was well past 40 % (or fast) was thrown away. A cancel and a spring look like "one frame" at 15 fps. The release path itself was right: a quick flick of 35 % in 80 ms from 4 px inside the edge, and a slow swipe, both removed the set in `Round31Test` before the fix; the test that failed before the fix is the cancel one (`aSwipePastFortyPercentThatTheSystemCancelsStillRemovesTheSet`). Robolectric has no system gesture, so the cancel is simulated; whether this is exactly what the phone does is a phone check. Not the cause: the list, Round 27's `closePadOnTap` (Initial pass, consumes nothing), the reorder hold (only after a still hold), the highlight watch.
   - **Fix, two parts.** (a) A cancelled touch is **judged like a let-go**: past 40 % of the row the action runs (the row slides off), short of it the row springs back (animated). A cancel cannot be a fling (there is no release speed to read). (b) The row **keeps the system's Back off a 36 dp strip at each edge it has a swipe for** (`Modifier.keepSystemBackOff`: `systemGestureExclusion` on Android, nothing on iOS), so the system rarely takes the touch at all. **Trade-off**: while a workout shows set rows, a back swipe that starts on those 36 dp strips is the row's, not Back's (Back by the button, or from another height, still works); Android caps an app's exclusion at 200 dp of height per edge, so on a tall list the system keeps the first rows' strips and ignores the rest, and for those the cancel rule is the net. The strips are on the rows, not the page, so nothing else loses Back.
   - **Look.** "A simple swipe that just goes all the way": the colour is the ground behind the row, as wide as the row has moved away, in the row's shape (`SmallShape`), with **the icon alone** at its far end (the word "Remove" is dropped: the bin says it, and the screen reader still has "Remove" as the row's custom action). It is 45 % alpha and turns solid at the threshold with the Tick buzz (the icon swells 25 %). Past the threshold, or on a fling of at least 1000 dp/s over 32 dp, the row slides fully off, the height closes and the compact Undo pill shows. Short of it the row springs back with `Motion.follow()`.
2. **Lock photos** (Settings, Appearance, under "Blur photos"). `Platform.canAuthenticate` and `Platform.authenticate(reason, onResult)`. Android: `androidx.biometric` 1.1.0 `BiometricPrompt` with `BIOMETRIC_WEAK or DEVICE_CREDENTIAL` (fingerprint or face, else the PIN, pattern or password; no button of ours on it); `MainActivity` is now a `FragmentActivity` (a `ComponentActivity` underneath) and `androidx.fragment` 1.8.5 is named in the build, because BiometricPrompt brings an old Fragment whose `FragmentActivity` refuses the Activity Result API's permission request codes (the notification request crashed in a test). iOS: `LAContext.evaluatePolicy(.deviceOwnerAuthentication)` in `IosPlatform` and `NSFaceIDUsageDescription` in `Info.plist`. INTERNET stays out (the build check passes).
   - **Scope.** The pages behind `PhotoGate` (`Common.kt`, used in `App.kt`): Photos, a full photo, Compare and Moments (a full moment is inside it). While the photos are shut the page shows "Photos are locked" with one Unlock button (and Back) and asks the phone's lock as it appears; a refusal or a cancel leaves the button. While shut, **every thumbnail** (Profile's strip and fan, the lists, the Moments grid) takes the blurred path whatever "Blur photos" says (`photosShut()` in `PhotoThumb` and `MomentThumb`; the blurred cache key keeps a sharp copy from being served).
   - **Duration (chosen).** The unlock lasts **until the app goes to the background** (`MainActivity.onStop`, not for a turn of the phone), not for a time: someone who has just opened their photos is not asked again while they look, and anyone who picks the phone up later is. Not a 5-minute timer: a second thing to explain, and a prompt in the middle of Compare is worse than none.
   - **The switch.** Turning it on with no lock on the phone says "Set a screen lock on this phone first, a PIN, pattern, password or fingerprint." and stays off; with one it turns on and shuts the photos at once. **Turning it off asks the phone's lock** ("Turn off Lock photos"). `AppModel.photoLockOn`, `photosShut`, `unlockPhotos`, `lockPhotos`, `setPhotoLock`; kv key `photos.lock`.
3. **The dock slide waits** (`TabBar`, `Tabs`, `App.kt`). Rohan: "The dock swipe has some lag, and isn't it better to have the blink transition instead of the immediate snap transition? Additionally, the activity tab also snaps using the dock slide. I want it to have the behavior of the other transitions."
   - While the finger slides **only the dock moves**: the indicator, the name flash and the tick follow the relative position (Round 30's rule is kept). The app's tab and the page do **not** change during the slide, which also removes the cost of recomposing tabs mid-slide. `AppModel.dockSliding` is gone.
   - On release the tab changes **once, by the same assignment a tap makes** (`model.tab = ...`), so the blink and the Activity card's hand-over between Home and Progress (the overlay, `blinkFrom`/`blinkTo`) happen as they do for a tap. A flick is the same: one tab from the start, changed on release. "The activity tab" is Home's and Progress's Activity card: it snapped because the slide jumped pages at each tab, so the card never saw a blink; now it sees one change like a tap. A slide that ends where it started changes nothing.
4. **A soft blur** (`Photos.kt`: `softBlurred`, `BLUR_SIDE` 32, `BLUR_DECODE` 48). A blurred view decodes at 48 px, is cut to 32 px on its longer side and is blurred **in common code** (three box passes of radius 3 per channel, a close match to a Gaussian of sigma about 3.5 px, the edges repeated so no dark rim, written through a `Canvas`), then drawn larger with the bilinear filter. It is the same on every Android version and on iOS, needs no `Modifier.blur` (Android 12 and up only) and leaves colour shapes only. Only the blurred pixels are kept: the sharp decode is dropped as soon as the 32 px copy is made, and nothing blurred holds the full photo.
5. **Snapshots without the card** (`SnapshotsTile`, `Moments.kt`). No `Tile`: no card background, no padding. The fan sits on the page under Profile's own "Snapshots" heading and is as large as the width allows (`fanLayout`): four cards a step of 62 % of a card apart, the card = (width - 28 dp) / 2.86, at most 150 dp (about 105 dp on a phone 360 dp wide, 76 dp before); the 100:76 frames are still turned (-6, -2, 3, 7 degrees), lifted and tilting with the scroll, and the thumbnails are cut at 360 px. The count, the tap to open Moments and the faint frames with "Add photo" when empty are as they were. **The morph**: `profile:moments` has no card, so the container the Moments page grows from is **the fan's own box** (`opensInto(null, Transparent)`: the page's colour, the card corner), the full width of the section and the fan's height; going back it shrinks into the same box. Nothing changes at the end of the transition (Round 21): the box is the same before and after, which `Round31Test.theFanSitsOnThePageWithNoCardBehindIt` checks.

Tests: `Round31Test` (regression; the tour is `Tour`); `Round29Test`, `Round30Test` and `PhotoBlurSearchTest` updated (the slide asserts the dock's selection while sliding and the tab after the lift; a cancel past 40 % now acts, so those tests let go instead; the blur is 32 px). **Phone checks**: a real quick swipe from the right edge on Android 16 (does the strip keep Back off it, and does a cancel still delete?), how the red looks behind a row, the prompt and its fallback, the blink after a slide, how soft the blur looks at the Profile's size, the fan's size and the page growing out of it.

## Round 32: page backgrounds and frosted cards (2026-11-24; not run on a device)

Rohan chose page backgrounds that let frosted glass show, from the mockup `artifacts/round5/backgrounds-studio.html` (its `CURATED`, `P`, `background()`, `palette()` and `PAIR`). Pictures: `app/build/screens/default/round32-*.png` (`Round32Test`; Home with each of the fourteen in Graphite Dark, the eight themes with Theme's own, a few in Light, the Settings row). The glass in those pictures is the solid form (Robolectric cannot blur), so they show the backgrounds, not the frost. Nothing here was seen on a phone.

### The backgrounds (`Backgrounds.kt`)

Fourteen, each a light, an optional pattern and optional extras designed together. **A pattern covers the whole page at its base strength (`base`) and is drawn stronger (`k`) through a radial mask at the light's position; it is never visible only in the light.** Lights are elliptical radial gradients at fractions of the page, in the accent or one of the theme's `SERIES` colours (the second light of Pools and of Topo is the first chart colour). Light mode uses the Light accent, lights at 0.7 and patterns at 0.9 of their Dark strength. Everything is drawn in design units (1 dp on the page), once per size and theme, into one cached layer behind the four tab pages (`drawBackdrop`: an offscreen layer, so nothing is redrawn while a page scrolls; the status bar's fade strip paints the same `Backdrop` over a scrolled page's top edge). There is no parallax: the background is fixed and the content scrolls over it. Grain is a 128 px grey noise bitmap made once in common code (`Grain`: an LCG, sixteen grey levels) and tiled, drawn with Overlay (Dark, 55 %) or Multiply (Light, 35 %); the vignette is a radial gradient reaching the farthest corner. Photo is the user's snapshot or progress photo blurred by Round 31's `softBlurred` (48 px decode, 32 px, drawn large with the high filter quality and a little extra saturation), under a ground scrim (0.45 Dark, 0.6 Light), no pattern. If Lock photos is on and the photos are shut, or the picked file is gone, the page shows Default (`photoFallsBack`); while it loads it is the plain ground. The pick is in kv `look.background.photo` as `p:` or `m:` and the file name. **Default is `drawAmbient` itself** (the page and the soft top glow), pixel-identical to before (`Round32Test.defaultIsPixelIdenticalToTheOldBackground`, on a 420 x 840 region in every theme and mode); no layer is used for it. **The background is drawn in every tier**, grain included: it costs one textured quad a frame from the cached layer and nothing on a scroll, so Light keeps it (decided here; if a phone shows otherwise, grain is the one to drop).

The reasoning of each, from the mockup:

- **Default** (The plain page with the soft glow at the top: what Clean has today, and the default.) Kept exactly as it is so nobody who updates sees a change. It is the quietest choice: the glow marks the top of the page and nothing competes with the numbers.
- **Pools** (Three soft pools of light: the accent at the top left, one of the theme's chart colours at the right, a faint accent at the bottom. No pattern.) For people who want colour but no texture. The pools sit at the edges, so the cards pick up a different tint as you scroll past them, which is what makes frosted glass visible. Using a chart colour ties the page to the theme's own family.
- **Studio** (The top glow with a fine film grain over the whole page and slightly darker edges.) Grain is the texture that sells glass best: sharp around the cards and smoothed away inside them, without any shape that draws the eye. The darker edges keep attention in the middle, like a lit room.
- **Topo** (Contour lines over the whole page, in two soft pools.) Contour lines read as terrain, a quiet nod to climbing and progress. They are the one organic shape among the patterns, a counterweight to an app made of grids and tables. Even strength everywhere, because the lines already have their own rhythm.
- **Photo** (A snapshot or progress photo you choose, blurred and darkened. A stand-in picture is shown.) The personal one. It is blurred to colour only, so it never shows a face or detail and never fights the text. No pattern on top: a photo already has texture, and a pattern over it looks busy.
- **Dots** (A dot grid over the whole page, brighter under the top glow.) The quietest pattern. The dots share the rhythm of the Activity calendar's squares, so it feels part of the app. The glow lifts them where the page title has the most room.
- **Blueprint** (A fine square grid over the whole page, brighter under the top light.) A grid matches the app's own structure: aligned columns of sets, kilos and reps. Lit from above like a drafting lamp, strongest behind the header and quieter behind long lists, so rows stay easy to read.
- **Marks** (Small plus marks over the whole page, brighter in a light from the top-left corner.) Plus marks look like a printer's registration marks: precise without being a full grid. The light sits where every title starts reading, at the top left, so the pattern frames the entry point of each page.
- **Prism** (A triangle grid over the whole page, brighter in a light from the right edge.) Triangles add angle and direction. Text is left-aligned, so the right side of most rows is empty; lighting from the right puts the stronger pattern where there is space for it and balances the page.
- **Slant** (Fine diagonal lines over the whole page, brighter under a light at the top right.) Diagonals feel like movement, and run across the up-and-down scroll so the frost shows clearly as cards pass over them. The light at the top right reads like a lamp over a gym floor.
- **Ripple** (Rings spreading over the whole page from a light at the top right.) Rings spreading out feel like a pulse or a landing. Their centre sits at the top right, away from the titles, so the tight inner rings never sit behind text and the wide outer arcs cross the cards gently.
- **Tide** (Soft waves over the whole page, brighter on a band of light across the middle.) The calmest of the line patterns: an even, breathing rhythm. The light is in the middle of the page, where the tiles and lists are, so it lights the content instead of the header.
- **Beams** (Rays and arcs over the whole page, falling from the top light.) Stage lighting: you, under the lights. The boldest of the set, so it is symmetric and centred to stay calm. The rays point down into the page and lead the eye to the content.
- **Pulse** (A zigzag over the whole page, brighter in a light rising from behind the dock.) A zigzag reads like a heart-rate trace, the most energetic choice. Its light rises from behind the dock and the plus button, linking the pattern to starting a workout.

Where a page opens over the tabs (Settings, a week, a workout), the page is plain as before: the background lives behind Home, Progress, Library and Profile.

### Theme's own (the default)
