kv `look.background` is `theme` (or nothing) or a background's id. "Theme's own" follows the theme; a specific pick stays across theme changes until Theme's own is chosen again. Graphite is the default theme, so a fresh install and anyone on Graphite sees no change; someone on another theme sees that theme's background after updating, which is intended. The table (`THEME_BACKGROUND`, in `Backgrounds.kt`; the reasons are the mockup's `PAIR`):

| Theme | Background | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Graphite | Default | Unchanged: the neutral baseline, and what Clean looks like today. |
| Ember | Studio | A warm glow with film grain reads like a lit room or old film, which suits an orange accent. |
| Crimson | Pulse | Red with a heart-rate zigzag rising from the dock: the most energetic pair. |
| Moss | Topo | Green with contour lines reads as terrain and the outdoors. |
| Ocean | Tide | Blue with waves: the obvious, calm match. |
| Plum | Ripple | Violet rings spreading out feel soft and a little luxurious. |
| Ice | Blueprint | Cyan on a fine grid looks like a technical drawing: precise and cold. |
| Rose | Pools | Pink as soft pools of light without lines: gentle. |

### Frosted cards

Tiles (`Tile`, Profile's tiles), the Section and MiniPage cards in the four tabs, and the dock (already glass) show the background through frosted glass. **Cost.** There is one blur source, the background (`hazeSource(haze, zIndex = -1f)`, recorded once into Haze's layer, only while the tabs are the page shown, as before), and each card has a `hazeEffect` that reads only that area (`canDrawArea = { it.zIndex < 0f }`), not the scrolling pages above it. So a scroll changes nothing a card reads, and the work per frame is the blur of each visible card's own rectangle at half scale (the `GlassBlurBlock` input scale): about the area of the cards, roughly one screen of pixels at a quarter of the resolution, in the GPU's RenderEffect, no more than the blur the dock and a sheet already cost. A page with ten cards is ten small effects, not ten full-page blurs. This is reasoning, not a measurement; the phone check is a long scroll on Home in Full with the GPU profiler. With Reduce transparency (`glass.reduce`, `LocalSolidGlass`) and in Balanced and Light, `frosted` is the opaque fill, exactly as the cards looked before; so are cards on the pages that open over the tabs (no `LocalFrost` there).

**Tint and contrast.** The glass is the card's own tone at a tint over the blur, in the tone that makes a card on a plain page the colour it always was (`frostTone`), so only where the background has light or lines does it differ. The tint is the least that keeps text readable: `glassTint` raises it from 0.55 until ink on the glass keeps 4.5 : 1 (`FROST_CONTRAST`) against the worst of that background (`worstBehind`: the ground with every light at full strength and the pattern's strongest line over it, softened by the pattern's coverage because the blur averages a line with its surroundings; for Photo a white and a black photo behind the scrim, or the extremes of the chosen photo, which is why a real photo needs less). The inks are text, quiet text and the accent as text, on the card, the tile and Home's accent-tinted hero; where an ink is already below 4.5 on today's card (Crimson's Dark accent is 4.4, Round 20) it must keep 97 % of today's. Result (`round32-contrast.txt`): Default 0.55 to 0.85, the patterned ones 0.55 to 0.96, Photo 0.81 to 0.98 for any photo; the glass is mostly the card's tone, with the background coming through in the lit areas and the lines, less see-through than the mockup's 6.5 % white. `Round32Test.textOnFrostedGlassKeepsFourPointFiveAgainstTheWorstOfEveryBackground` asserts it for every background, in all eight themes in Dark and Light and Pure black. Not covered: text that sits straight on the page outside a card (quiet grey text over a Photo background is a phone check).

**Known edges.** (1) A card that opens a page grows from an opaque container (`opensInto`), so the first frame of that growth is opaque where the card was frosted, and while a page covers the tabs the blur source is off, so a card there is its tint over the unblurred background; the start is a phone check. (2) **A page that grows from a row with no card (Progress's Trends, Trophies, Personal bests, Calendar rows, the Body and History cards' rows) is the plain page colour, so over a patterned background (everything but Default) the row's rectangle shows flat ground where the pattern or light was, for the length of the move, and the last frame of Back differs from rest by that rectangle.** This breaks Round 21's rule for every theme whose own background is not Default (all but Graphite) and for any picked pattern; it is not fixed here (the fix is for the pages that open over the tabs to carry the same `Backdrop`, which touches every page container and the container transform). `Round21Test` and `MotionFramesTest` pinned `look.background` to `default` because their subject is the frames of a move on the plain page (issue I-145; fixed in Round 34, which also replaced this set of fourteen with ten: see its section, which wins where the two disagree).

### Settings, Appearance, Background (`BackgroundSettings.kt`)

The existing Background group (Pure black) now starts with a strip of live previews, 66 by 132 dp, the real background drawn small (`drawBackdropScaled`, 360 design units across): "Theme's own" first, showing the theme's pair, then the fourteen. A tap applies at once; the chosen one is ringed in the accent. Photo asks the phone's lock when the photos are shut and then opens the app's own photo choice (the snapshots, then the progress photos; no system picker); its preview shows the chosen photo, or a stand-in (soft colour shapes, blurred the same way) when none is chosen. The footer says what Theme's own does for the theme in use. The "Background" entry of `SettingsIndex.kt` gained the words.

Tests: `Round32Test` (regression; `tour` is `Tour`). Phone checks: the blur and its cost on a long scroll, the patterns on a real screen at 3x, grain on OLED, Photo with a real photo and the quiet text over it, the card-to-page start, the strip's feel.

## Round 33: the reorder drag's edges, and where Do next inserts (2026-11-24; not run on a device)

Rohan's words: "When reordering, the upper scroll threshold is good but it can be a bit vertically higher, and the lower scroll threshold is not good it should be much vertically lower, (do testing with the number pad up)" and "When adding an exercise during a workout, the do next should be next if the exercise is half done with sets that are not done yet, but if the next exercise is not done at all, meaning no sets done then it should be before that."

1. **Reorder auto-scroll zones** (`Active.kt`, `reorderPush`). While a card is held and dragged, the list scrolls when the finger is near an edge, faster the nearer it is (up to 12 dp a frame). **Top zone: the first 56 dp of the list** (was 72, so it begins nearer the top). **Bottom zone: the last 40 dp of what is seen** (was 72). **Cause of the bottom problem:** the zone was measured from `viewportSize.height`, the list's full height, but the number pad and the rest bar float over the list's bottom (the list only leaves room for them as padding). With the pad up its roughly 300 dp hid the zone behind it: a card dragged down reached the pad without the list moving, and scrolling began only with the finger on the pad. The bottom of what is seen is now `viewport - pad.height - restBarPx` (the top of the pad, or the list's end with no pad), and the zone is 40 dp above that. Without the pad and bar the edge is the list's end, as before. The two sizes are named constants (`REORDER_TOP_ZONE`, `REORDER_BOTTOM_ZONE`); the push is the pure function `reorderPush`. The number pad's box now has the test tag "number pad".
2. **Do next** (`doNextIndex`, `AppModel.addExercise(afterCurrent = true)`; supersedes Round 28's "right after the exercise being done now"). The current exercise is the first card in the workout's order (a superset counts as one unit) that still has a set not done. If it has at least one set done (any card of the superset), the new exercise goes right after it, after its whole superset. If it has no set done, the new one goes before it (before the superset's first card), so the new exercise is the one to do now. When every card is done, at the end. One tap (queue at the end) is unchanged.

Tests: `Round33Test` (regression): with and without the pad, the list holds still just above the bottom zone and scrolls just inside it and at the edge, holds still just below the top zone and scrolls up inside it (the injected finger trails by the drag's touch slop, about 16 dp, so the probes allow for it; `thePushIsMeasuredFromTheVisibleEdge` pins the exact numbers); the four Do next cases, a started, an unstarted and a finished superset, and `addExercise` itself. `Round28Test`'s two Do next tests follow the new rule. Phone checks: that 56 dp and 40 dp feel right with the pad up and down (the bottom 40 dp can sit under a gesture-navigation bar), and that a drag to the pad's top edge scrolls at full speed.

## Round 34: twelve themes, ten backgrounds, pages that carry the background (2026-11-24; not run on a device)

Rohan approved mockup "round 6c" (`artifacts/round6/themes-studio.html` and `themes-proposal.md`; both are kept out of git) and answered its questions: Rose stays as the twelfth theme, the twelve swatches in Settings are one scrolling row, and Graphite stays the default with Plain, so a default user sees no change in layout (Graphite Dark's quiet text is A3A3A7 now, where it was A09C9A). Every colour below is the proposal's computed one, checked in `Round34Test` against the contrast the proposal printed (text 4.5, muted 4.5, accent 3, label on the accent 4.5, series 3 : 1; the worst accent is Ember in Light at 4.26).

### The twelve themes (`ui/theme/Look.kt`, `THEMES`)

Each theme is two `ModeColours` (Dark and Light), designed separately: page, card, text, muted, accent, the label on the accent, good, bad, PR and four chart colours. Light mode is no longer derived from the Dark theme: page, text, quiet text, accent and the rest are the proposal's own. The Dark card is the proposal's (the tint at 5.5 % over the page; the tint is solved from it, `ThemeDef.tint`), a tile 4.5 %, the higher step 10 % of the same tint; in Light the card and tile are white and the higher step is the Light accent at 10 % over white. Graphite Light keeps its own colour scale (`GRAPHITE_LIGHT_SCALE`) and grey outlines. Plain's ambient light is white at 7 % in Graphite and the accent at 15 % in the others (14 to 16 before). Hues (Oklch): Rose 5, Crimson 22, Ember 52, Saffron 84, Green 142, Teal 176, Lagoon 222, Cobalt 268, Iris 302, Orchid 342, then the two neutrals. There is no theme between Saffron and Green on purpose: that is the lime and olive range, and Lime was rejected.

The rest (warm-up, drop, recovering, full, underweight, the amber of `tertiary()`) still go through `apartFrom` against the accent (36 degrees). Rohan may want one of those Light golds nudged.

### The ten backgrounds (`Backgrounds.kt`, from the proposal's `BGS` and `P`)

Lights use the accent only (no chart colour any more). Positions, radii and strengths are the proposal's; Light draws lights at 0.7 and patterns at 0.9 of Dark's, as in Round 32.

| Background | What it is | Own for | Why |

| Pools | Two soft pools of the accent: top left (.08, .04, .78 x .38, .19) and the right edge (1.02, .56, .62 x .30, .11) | Crimson, Lagoon | Colour as faint light; the cards pick up a different tint as they scroll past each pool, which is what makes the frost visible. |
| Corner | One wide glow from the top-right corner (1.05, -.04, 1.15 x .62, .20), eased: a, .72 a at 22 %, .42 a at 45 %, .16 a at 70 %, none at the edge, so no ring shows | Saffron, Orchid | One lamp off to the side, away from where titles start, so the left of every page is the calmest. |

| Grid | Hairlines every 20 dp, 0.6 stroke, alpha .045; 2.6 x stronger under the top light | Cobalt | The old Blueprint made finer and fainter: cells smaller than any row, lines weaker than a row divider, so it is a tone behind a list. |
| Plus | Plus marks every 28 dp (3 dp arms, 1.2 stroke, alpha .18); a light in the top-left corner (0, 0, .9 x .45, .20), 2.2 x stronger there | Slate | Registration marks: precise without being a full grid. |
| Topo | Contour lines (26 irregular waves, 1.1 stroke; alpha .09, every fourth .16), base .8, 1.9 x under a pool at the top left (.1, .05, .78 x .40, .17) and a second light (1, .6, .55 x .26, .07) | Green | The one wave: irregular contours read as land, not a repeating ripple. |
| Paper | See below. Light at the top left (.2, 0, .9 x .5, .17), the texture 1.8 x under it | Ember | A deep orange glow on paper fibre reads as a lamp over a page: warm and tactile, and the orange stays as light rather than a pattern. |
| Studio | The top light (.2) with film grain over the page and slightly darker edges, unchanged | Rose | Grain shows frosted glass better than any shape: the blur smooths it inside a card and leaves it sharp around it. |
| Photo | The user's snapshot or progress photo, blurred, under a ground scrim; unchanged | none | The personal one. |

Dropped: Nodes, Crosses, Fine dots and Chalk (6c); Tide, Ripple and Pulse (one wave is enough); Prism, Slant, Beams and Logbook. The glass tint floor (`glassTint`, `FROST_CONTRAST` 4.5) is unchanged and asserted for all 24 looks and the ten backgrounds, Pure black included.

### Paper (`Paper.kt`)

Rohan said the first Paper "looks like dust", so it was reworked from per-pixel noise to formation (cloudy tone from uneven pulp, as when a sheet is held to the light), a few faint long fibres and the gentlest tooth. A tile is 512 x 512 px, seamless, made once per seed and per mode from a Park-Miller generator (`st = seed * 9973 + 7`, `st = st * 16807 mod 2147483647`). **Formation:** periodic value noise in four octaves (3, 6, 12 and 24 cells across, amplitudes .50, .28, .15, .07, bilinear with smoothstep weights, so no blur pass; the largest cloud is a third of the tile). **Soft contrast:** `sign(v) |v|^1.4`. **Tooth:** plus `(rnd * 2 - 1) * 0.025` per pixel, the only per-pixel noise. **Pixel:** Dark is white above zero and black below; Light is warm light (255, 250, 240) and warm shade (150, 130, 110); the alpha is `min(255, |v| * 120)`, so no blend mode is needed. **Fibres:** 220 per tile, 28 to 96 px long, leaning about 20 degrees (+-30), a quadratic curve bent by up to 14 px, 0.7 to 1.4 px wide, alpha .018 to .05, 70 % light and 30 % shade, each drawn at all nine wrapping offsets. The mockup blurs the fibres by 0.8 px; common code has no mask filter, so each is stroked 0.8 px wider with the same alpha (antialiased, they are as soft). **Two layers:** A is seed 1 at 512 design units; B is seed 2 at 703 (x 1.373) offset by 137 x 61. Opacity is .45 / .30 in Dark and .60 / .42 in Light (A / B), times the strength (1 over the page, 0.8 more under the light), so the two sets of seams and periods never line up across a phone page. `Round34Test` measures it: layer A alone correlates 1.0 with itself 512 px lower and the two layers together stay clearly below that; neighbouring pixels differ by under 4 alpha levels on average (a speck would be 10 or more); and the tile has no seam. The four tiles (two seeds, two modes) are made off the main thread the first time Paper is wanted in a mode (`Paper.warm`, from `rememberBackdrop` and the Background strip: about a million noise samples each); a draw before then paints nothing for Paper, so it never stalls a frame. They are cached bitmaps drawn through `ImageShader`s.

A name already in the new set is left alone (`themeFor`). `ACCENT_CHOICES` is the twelve; `OLD_THEME_CHOICES` the eight.

### Settings

Appearance, Theme: the twelve swatches are one horizontal scrolling row (`LazyRow`, test tag `theme-row`), the chosen one scrolled into view. The Background row (`BackgroundSettings.kt`) shows Theme's own first, then the ten.

### I-145: pages that open over the tabs carry the background

A page container was the flat ground, so a row or card that grew into a page over a pattern showed a flat patch, and Back ended unlike rest there. Now `pageContainer()` is clear and `cardTarget` draws the page's own background behind it: the ground and the same `Backdrop` as the tabs, below the status bar (`PageBackdrop`, filled by `RegisterPageBackdrop` from inside the running-workout tint so the ground matches the tabs'). While a card or row grows into its page that background lies in the page's own frame (the same translation as the page's content, so a pattern is where the tabs' is), clipped to the container, with the card's fill over it, laid over the background and fading as the container grows (`CardSpot.fill` keeps its alpha: a row on the page has none, so it shows the pattern from the first frame; a frosted card has its tone at the glass's tint, the nearest the first frame can come to the blur, so it is no longer opaque where it was frosted). Pages that cover their background on purpose (the workout, Correct workout, Moments) take `pageGround()`, the flat colour. `Round21Test` and `MotionFramesTest` no longer pin `look.background = default`: they run on Cobalt, whose own background is Grid. `Round34Test` opens a row (Trends) over Topo, a card (History) over Plus and a Home tile over Dots and checks the first frames and the last frame of Back against rest. A page's background is drawn uncached (in the page's display list, one offscreen layer for a masked pattern), where the tabs' is a cached layer: a phone check on a page's open and scroll.

Tests: `Round34Test` (regression; `tour` is `Tour`; pictures `round34-*`), `Round6Spec.kt` (the proposal's figures), `LookMigrationTest`, and `LookTest`, `ColourBlindTest`, `Round20ColoursTest`, `Round22ContrastTest`, `Round28Test` and `Round32Test` updated to the new set. Phone checks: Paper's feel on a real screen in both modes (the mockup was judged in a browser), the cost of a page's own backdrop, the first frame of a frosted card opening, Light's look of each theme in daylight.

## Round 35: the backdrop runs up under the status bar (2026-11-25; not run on a device)

Rohan (test 48): a flat black band at the top, "Before it was good and the colour went all the way up even in notifications"; he meant the strip behind the status bar. Round 32 drew the tabs' backdrop in a box padded below the status bar, so the strip above kept the Scaffold's flat ground; Round 34 drew a page's backdrop inside an `inset` of the status bar's height over a flat ground, and the bar of a page was an opaque `TopAppBar`.

- **One origin.** The backdrop (ground, lights, pattern, texture, Photo) is always drawn over the whole screen from its top edge, at 1 dp to a unit. On the tabs `fullBleedTop` makes the backdrop's box reach up by the status bar's height; on a page `PageBackdrop.draw` fills the page. So a page's backdrop lines up with the tabs' (I-145: a card growing into a page, and the last frame of Back equal to rest, still hold) and the pattern is one surface with no seam at the status bar or title bar.
- **Plain** keeps the position it had before Round 32 (the glow centred 40 dp below the top of the screen), which is what `drawAmbientAt` draws from an origin at the screen's top: Graphite looks as it did.
- **BackBar** has a clear container (also scrolled). Its scroll fade is `PageBackdrop.drawFade`: the page's backdrop under a band that fades to clear, so the pattern runs on. The tabs' `topFadeMask` draws the same backdrop moved up by the status bar's height.
- **Flat pages** (`pageGround()`: the workout, Correct, Moments) keep their ground, which fills the screen including the status bar, so the band matches the page; Correct and Moments clear `LocalPageBackdrop` round their bar so its fade is the flat ground.
- Tests: `Round35Test` gives the window real insets (a 24 dp status bar and a 48 dp navigation bar through `ViewCompat.dispatchApplyWindowInsets`; Robolectric gives none, which hid this) and reads the band's pixels: Ember with Paper on Home and the Settings page agree with the backdrop drawn over the whole screen and are not flat ground; Graphite's band is the old glow. Pictures `round35-*.png`.

## Round 36: no flat band over the backdrop, Back up now (2026-11-25; not run on a device)

Rohan: the exercise page's tab row was a solid black band across the pattern ("stylistically inconsistent, find other places where it is"); and "a sync button next to the backup folder button ... days ago or hours ago or minutes ago". Pictures: `app/build/screens/default/round36-*.png` (`Round36Test`; the glass is solid under Robolectric).

**The rule: nothing paints the flat ground over the backdrop. A bar that must be opaque uses the glass material (`glass()`, solid under Reduce transparency), never the flat ground.** Flat on purpose: `pageGround()` pages (workout, Correct, Moments).

### Backup (`AutoBackupSection.kt`)

- Under the Folder row, once a folder is chosen: an `ActionRow` "Back up now" (a button inside a group is an action row, as in the rest of Settings), "Backing up..." and disabled while it runs. It runs `Platform.backupNow()` (Android `AutoBackup.attempt`, iOS `IosAutoBackup.attempt`): the same backup to the same folder as the scheduled one, recording the last-backup time and the error as it does; null when it worked, else the reason. `AppModel.backUpNow` keeps the state and says "Backed up to <folder>" or "Backup failed: <reason>" in the notice. The folder's name is the row's summary now, so the row has room.
- `relativeTime(then, now)` (core, `RelativeTime.kt`): "just now" under a minute (also a time ahead of now), "12 minutes ago", "1 hour ago", "5 hours ago", "1 day ago", "3 days ago"; null from 7 days, where `prettyDate` shows the date. The Last backup line is worked out again every minute while the page is open. Other places keep their day-only helpers (`agoText`, Templates' `ago`: "Today", "Yesterday", weeks and months): they never show a time of day and their words differ on purpose.

### The page map (`SettingsMap.kt`, `SETTINGS_PAGE_DEFS`)

A page is a list of sections (a section is one item of the page's lazy list, keyed; the keys are the search's and the tests' handles). Nothing was dropped and no kv key changed (the set of option keys in `Settings.kt` is the same as before the round).

| About and help | What's new (`s-changes`); Source code (`s-source`); Licences and credits (`s-credits`); Tips (`s-tips`) | What's new, Source, Licences, Tips |

Placed by judgement: the old "Training" group is split: the weekly target, Program and Current split are on Training, but "Suggest what to try" (it shows under an exercise's name in a workout) and "Celebrate on finishing" (the summary after a workout) are on Workout, in "In a workout". The old "When a rest ends" group is split: the Sound/Buzz/Both choice is on Sound and feel next to the rest sound, and "Alert outside the app" and "Go back to the workout when the rest ends" stay on Workout, under the heading "Rest in the background". The two groups of Backup are Automatic backup and Backup file. The old "Rest" group is titled "Rest timer" (the mockup's word); "Backup" is "Backup file". Chips are made of sections (a chip can cover two), and a page has them from three chips up; Health Connect's chip is left out on a phone without it.

### The main page (B)

`SettingsNow` (`rememberSettingsNow`, reading the stored options, so the lines are new whenever a setting changes or a page is left) gives each row one plain line, the most telling first: Workout "Rest 2:00 · auto-fill 1 warm-up, 2 sets, 1 drop"; Training "3 a week · 1RM by Hybrid, up to 12 reps"; Look "Graphite · Plain · Dark" (and "text large" when it is not Medium); Sound and feel "Deep bell · haptics on · Full"; Photos and privacy "Blurred · not locked"; Backup and sync "3 hours ago · <folder> · daily" (Round 36's `relativeTime`, a date after a week, worked out again every minute; "Not set up" with none); Import, export, Health Connect "Health Connect on · last sync <when>" (or "Import and export workouts" without it); Library upkeep "12 templates · 3 renamed · 5 animations"; About and help "Clean 1.1.0 · what's new, source, tips". The rows are in four groups as in the mockup, each with a line icon of the app's own (`ChartLine`, `LookLine`, `SoundLine`, `LockLine`, `TransferLine`, `BooksLine` drawn after the mockup, and existing ones). A row's line is one line, cut with an ellipsis (`SettingsRow.summaryLines`). The pages open by the swap Settings already had (the blink in and out of an `AnimatedContent`, not a card-to-page morph; kept), and settle their first sections in.

### Jump chips (`SettingsChips.kt`, `SlideAlong.kt`)

- **Pinned.** The chips are a `stickyHeader` of the page's list. Once the page has scrolled (past a third of the bar's height) they settle to 90 % on a strip of the glass material (`glass(RectangleShape)`, solid under Reduce transparency; wider than the screen so its side edges are off it), the padding shrinking too; animated (200 ms) when `LocalMotion` is on and the tier is not Light, otherwise at once. Nothing flat is painted over the backdrop.
- **Follows the scroll.** The lit chip is the last whose first section's top has passed under the bar; at the end of a page that can scroll the last chip is lit. After a tap or a slide the chip chosen stays lit where the page came to rest until the page is scrolled again (a short page's end would otherwise light the last chip).
- **Tap** scrolls to the section (animated unless motion is off), leaving it below the settled bar.
- **The dock's gesture, once.** `Modifier.slideAlong` (`SlideAlong.kt`) is the dock's slide moved out of `TabBar` and used by both: relative to the slot that was current, one slot per `slot` width (the chips' average pitch), `Haptic.Tick` at each step, a flick (600 dp/s, within half a slot) moves one, a vertical start is left alone, the end calls back once. The dock's tests (Round 29 to 31) pass unchanged. The chips add the hold: a press still for the long-press time calls `onHold` and the rest of the touch is the hold's.
- **Hold** shows a `PeekPopup` with `PeekHead` and `PeekLine`s (the section's settings with their values, `SettingsNow.lines`) above the finger until it lifts; it is no tap.
- The row does not scroll under a finger (`horizontalScroll(enabled = false)`: only the code moves it, to keep the lit chip in view), so a slide is never a pan.

### Search

The boxed field is gone. Settings has the shared `SearchBox` and `rememberPullToSearch`/`pulled`/`PullHint` exactly as Library and History: an icon in the bar (main page only), a pull down opens it with the keyboard, Back, the close control or the keyboard going down closes it (Round 28/29 rules; the page stays). Results come from `SETTINGS_INDEX`, now with a `group` and finer entries (a setting inside a group: "Default rest", "Bar and plates"...). A result shows its title, "Page › Group · value" (the group's value for a group, the setting's for a setting: `valueOf`) and opens the page scrolled to its section, the section washed in the accent for about a second (`SettingsFlash`; with animations off the wash stays 1.2 s and goes at once).

### The two other search fields (I-155's proposal 1)

- **Workout sheet, "Search templates"**: the shared `SearchBox`, pinned (`SearchOpener.pinned`, always out) as the exercise pickers' pill is: the sheet is not a page that can be pulled.
- **Library picker**: its pill was already the shared pill, pinned, since Round 28. What was left was the old path in `ExerciseList` (an outlined field beside a `leading` back arrow) that nothing used any more; it is removed, `search` is a required parameter and `leading` is gone.

Proposals 2 and 3 of I-155 (the templates sheet's empty state, the one-line "No ... yet" texts) are still for Rohan to choose.

### Tests

`Round37Test` (regression, one tour): every old setting on its page; the main page's lines and that they follow the settings and a return from a page; chips only on pages with three or more; pinned, settled smaller, animated; lit follows the scroll; tap; slide relative with a tick, half a slot, clamp; flick; vertical start; hold peek; pull-to-search, a result's page, group and value, the page opened at the setting with the wash, Back; the two search fields. `SettingsIndexTest` is rewritten for nine pages. The older tests that opened Settings pages use the new page names (Look, Sound and feel, Photos and privacy, Backup and sync, Import export Health Connect).


## Round 38: calm jump chips, a small Back up button, a camera for snapshots (2026-11-25; not run on a device)

Rohan (test 51, Cobalt Dark, Grid): the pinned chips were a solid band across the grid, outlined rectangles with a barely different selected fill, cut at the left edge, and ghosted by the top fade on Look. Pictures: `app/build/screens/default/round38-*.png` (`Round38Test`).

### The chips (`SettingsChips.kt`)

- **Chosen: fixed chips floating on the backdrop, no strip at all.** The two options were chips with their own glass on the backdrop, or a soft glass strip continuing the backdrop. A strip, however soft, is a second surface the eye reads as a band (it was the complaint), and the list's own top fade already continues the backdrop; so the chips sit directly under the bar, on the page's backdrop, and the fade starts below them. Calmer: one surface fewer, and nothing to animate.
- **Not part of the list any more.** `JumpChips` is above the list in a column, not a `stickyHeader`; it is always where it is, the same size, at rest and scrolled. The settle (90 %, padding, glass strip, 200 ms) is gone. Only the lit chip and the row's own sideways scroll change.
- **Never dimmed.** BackBar's fade is drawn over what is below the bar, which is where the chips are; the chips now tell `LocalTopFade` their height (`TopFade.below`, owner-checked so a page swap does not reset the other page's value) and the fade is drawn from the chips' bottom edge instead.
- **`GlassChip`** (new, in `SettingsChips.kt`, a piece of its own so the filter chips elsewhere do not change): a pill, no outline; unselected is `tile(PillShape, frosted = true)` (the app's soft glass tile with its lit edge), selected is filled with the accent (`primary`) with `onPrimary` words, semibold; selection is read out (`Role.Tab`, selected). The dock, which has its own pill, and `CleanChip` are untouched; `slideAlong` is unchanged.
- **No clipping.** The row has the page gutter (`Space.l`) as space at its start and its end inside the scroll, and keeps the lit chip wholly in view with a gutter to spare by the least scroll (not centring, which pushed the first chip off the edge). A row of chips that fits is not scrolled at all.
- Sections a chip or a search leads to are left `CHIP_BAR_STUCK` (44 dp) below the top of the list, clear of the fade, as before.

### The shimmer (`GlassChip`)

When a chip becomes the lit one (tap, slide, flick or the page scrolling), a soft white band (30 % at its middle, fading in and out) sweeps once across its accent fill in 450 ms. Not when the chip is first composed (a page opening), not with Animations off, not in the Light tier (`flourishOn()`), and the fill is the same before and after. `ChipShimmer.started` counts starts for the tests.

### Backup (`AutoBackupSection.kt`, `SmallButton` in `Ui.kt`)

The "Back up now" row is gone. The Folder row ends in a small `SmallButton` "Back up" (the accent's words on the accent at 14 %, which shows on a card where the neutral Quiet tint would not; a screen reader says "Back up now"); with a folder chosen it takes the chevron's place, and the rest of the row still picks the folder. While it runs it reads "Backing up" with a small spinner (a still ring with Animations off) and is disabled. Result, failure, toast and the Last backup line are as Round 36.

### Snapshots with the camera (`Moments.kt`)

Adding a snapshot (the page's plus, its empty state, the Profile fan's "Add photo") opens a small sheet: "Take a photo" and "Choose from the phone", as the progress photos' sheet. The camera is `Platform.rememberCamera` (Android: `TakePicture` into the cache through the file provider, so no camera permission and no new permission; iOS: `UIImagePickerController`, which was already there for progress photos, so nothing new to write; not run on a device). The picture is filed with `addMoment` and the temporary file is discarded afterwards. Snapshots keep their name (a naming question is pending).

### Tests

`Round38Test` (real status-bar insets): the chip region shows the backdrop beside and between the chips and is not flat ground; the lit chip's fill is the same scrolled as at rest; no chip is cut at rest on any page, and the lit one is whole (the last one too, once tapped); the lit chip is the accent; the shimmer runs once per change, never at first, not with Animations off or in Light; Back up now is a button in the Folder row and runs a backup; the snapshot add sheet offers Take a photo. `Round36Test` reaches the button by its tag; `Round37Test`'s settle tests are now "the chips do not change".

## Round 39: one Photos section (2026-11-25; not run on a device)

Rohan asked to rename Snapshots to Photos, and on how that relates to the progress photos: "This should be integrated. Snapshots currently, like the four that are visible, are a way to enter that photos section. You could have labels if you want. You could add some more stats, like how many photos there are, right below the four photos." Pictures: `app/build/screens/default/round39-*.png` (`Round39Test`). Nothing here was seen on a phone.

- **A layer, not a merge of stores.** `Pic` (core, `Photos.kt`) is a progress photo (`photo`, has a pose) or a snapshot (`moment`, no pose); `AppModel.pics` is `mergedPics(photos, moments)`, newest first. Progress photos stay in `filesDir/photos/<profile>/<yyyyMMdd-HHmmss>_<pose>.jpg` and snapshots in `filesDir/moments/<profile>/<yyyyMMdd-HHmmss>_<8 hex>.jpg`; the two kinds never share a name, so the name says the kind. Backup, restore, Delete everything and the Photo background picker are untouched (`Round39Test.theBackupCarriesBothFoldersUnchanged`).
- **Profile.** The section heading is "Photos" with a "See all" link once there is a picture. The fan (`PhotosTile`, in `Moments.kt`; Round 31's card-less layout, key `profile:moments`) shows the newest four of both kinds and opens `Screen.Photos` growing out of the fan's box. Right below it, centred and quiet (`TileLine`), `photosSummary`: `42 photos · 18 progress · since Apr 2026 · last 3 days ago` (the progress count is left out when there are none; the time is `relativeTime` under seven days, a date such as `last 12 Oct` after), and a second line `6 weeks between your first and latest front photo` (`photosSpan`: the pose with most photos, two at least, over more than a day). A tap on the lines is the fan's tap. With none, the pill "Add photo" starts the add flow. The progress strip (`PhotosStrip`) is gone; its Compare shortcut lives on the page.
- **The page** (`PhotosScreen`; the old `Screen.Moments` route, kept so a stored one still opens, shows it on Snapshots). A timeline by day as before (date, how long ago, that day's weigh-in; three to a row, 3:4), a plus in the bar, the empty state "No photos yet" with "Add photo". Filters (`PhotoFilter`): a first row of chips **All, Progress, Snapshots**; **chosen: the poses are a second row (All poses, Front, Side, Back) that is there only while Progress is the kind**, since a pose is a way to look at progress photos and not at snapshots; the first row keeps Progress lit while a pose is. Each picture has a small label under it (`labelSmall`, muted): Front, Side, Back or Snapshot. **Compare** shows under Progress, and under a pose that has two photos (`compareOffered`); never under All or Snapshots.
- **Add** (`rememberPhotoAdd`, one for Profile and the page): the sheet "Add photo" offers Take a photo (the camera into the cache through the file provider) and Choose from the phone (the picker, several); then a second sheet, "What is it?", asks Progress (with the pose chips, the last one remembered as `ui.photo.pose`) or Snapshot (the last kind is remembered as `ui.photo.kind`), and Add files them: `importPhotos` for a progress photo, `importMoments` for a snapshot. A sheet dismissed files nothing and discards a camera file. The pending pictures are in `AppModel.photoPending` (with `photoAsker`, so only the Add that was tapped shows the question).
- **Viewer** (`PhotoScreen`, `Screen.Photo(name, filter)`; the stored form is the filter's name, which for an old route is a pose's, so old routes decode): a pager over the filtered list of both kinds; zoom, Share (snapshots through `Platform.momentBytes`), Delete with Undo (`deletePic`: the right store's aside and restore), Change date. **Change pose** lists Front, Side, Back and Snapshot, so a snapshot becomes a progress photo and back: it was cheap, one `Platform.refile` that renames the file, between the two folders when the kind changes (`refiled` in core picks the free name: a photo gets a second added, a snapshot a new id). It also lets a snapshot change its date, which it could not before. The snapshots' own viewer (`MomentViewer`) and the Moments grid with its stats row are gone.
- **Dropped, said plainly**: the Moments page's long-hold multi-select and its stats row (Photos, This month, Days). Delete is per picture in the viewer, as for progress photos; the stats line on Profile replaces the row.
- **Blur and Lock** (Round 30 and 31) cover the whole section as before: both kinds' small views take `decodeThumb`'s blurred path, and a shut lock blurs both. Settings' texts say Photos (the blur's summary "Every photo in Photos, progress and snapshots, in every small view.", the lock's "Opening Photos asks for..."), the Photo background picker's empty line too; What's new has a Photos line.
- Android-only: `AndroidPlatform.refile` and `momentBytes` (plain file moves); iOS has the same in `IosPictures` (compiled, not run).

Tests: `Round39Test` (regression; the tour is `Tour`), `PhotosTest` (the merge, filters, Compare, the summary and span lines, `refiled`); `ScreenCodecTest`, `Round17Test`, `Round30Test`, `Round31Test` updated for the new texts and the `Screen.Photo` filter. **Phone checks**: the camera and the picker into the question sheet, the fan's growth into the page, the second chip row, a Windows-only trap in the tests (an open file cannot be renamed, so `Round39Test` changes a picture's kind before it opens it).

## Round 40: the heading answers a jump chip (2026-11-25; not run on a device)

A jump chip's tap is answered where it leads, not on the chip (I-168). The Round 38 shimmer on the chip is gone; the chip is lit by the accent fill alone. Once the page is at rest after the tap, the first heading of the chip's first section lights: an accent pill (30 %) the width of the page behind the heading, growing in over 180 ms, holding about half a second and fading over 700 ms, with a white band (45 %) sweeping across it while it is full. It lights also when the page could not scroll to the section (the last chips of a short page), which is where the feedback was missing. Light tier: the fade with no sweep. Animations off: the pill is there for a second and goes at once. One heading per tap (`ShineRequest` is taken by the first `SettingsGroup` title that reads `LocalHeadingShine`), never when a page opens, nothing moves in the layout, and nothing changes when it ends.

## Round 41: the swipe's release and the dock's disc (2026-11-25; not run on a device)

- **A swipe between tabs goes on at the finger's speed when it lifts (I-169).** The user's recording showed a jump at every release; the stock pager snap paused for the first frame after it and then sped past the finger, pulled by its spring. `PagerGlide` replaces it for the tabs pager: the page it goes to is the next one in the swipe's direction above 400 dp/s, else the nearest; it travels there on a cubic Hermite curve that leaves at the release speed and arrives at rest, over 2 d / v (kept within 160 to 420 ms; 300 ms when the finger was going the other way), and moves on the first frame (counted as one 120 Hz frame after the release). Nothing ever goes faster than the finger did.
- **No disc behind a tab's icon (I-170).** E6's accent disc that grew behind the new tab's icon on a page change is gone; the capsule's glide and the name are the change's only signs in the dock.
- **Round 41b: the capsule never flashes back (I-171).** The glitch the user meant: at the end of a swipe the dock's capsule showed the tab the swipe came from for one frame. When a swipe ends, the indicator (`rememberStretch`) keeps drawing where the finger left it until the glide has taken over from that point; nothing is drawn from the glide's old state in between. The exercise page's underline shares it.
